   31: from .job_store import get_job, load_jobs, update_job_status
   32: from .models import JOB_STATUS_QUEUED, JOB_STATUS_RUNNING, OrchestrationJob
   33: 
   34: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   35: # Design notes (what changed and why)
   36: #
   37: # The previous implementation kept a module-level list (_queue_job_ids) that
   38: # was rebuilt from the job store on every call via refresh_queue_from_store().
   39: # Because the store was always authoritative, the list carried no information
   40: # of its own ù but it created two real problems:
   41: #
   42: #   1. NON-ATOMIC DEQUEUE: dequeue_job() removed a job from the local list
   43: #      while its status in the store remained "queued" until the worker later
   44: #      called mark_job_running(). Any concurrent worker (thread OR separate
   45: #      process) refreshing from the store in that window would re-discover the
   46: #      job and dequeue it again -> duplicate processing.
   47: #
   48: #   2. STATE DRIFT: two sources of truth that had to be defended against each
   49: #      other in multiple places (dead "clear()" branch, redundant existence
   50: #      checks, 2-3 load_jobs() calls per operation).
   51: #
   52: # This version deletes the in-memory list entirely. The job store IS the
   53: # queue: a job is "queued" iff its persisted status is JOB_STATUS_QUEUED.
   54: # dequeue_job() now CLAIMS the job atomically ù it transitions the job to
   55: # JOB_STATUS_RUNNING inside a cross-process lock before returning it ù so a
   56: # job can be handed to exactly one worker. This also means the queue trivially
   57: # survives process restarts: anything persisted as "queued" is picked up.
   58: #
   59: # Ordering is unchanged: ascending (priority, created_at) ù i.e. LOWER
   60: # priority number is served first, ties broken oldest-first (FIFO). job_id is
   61: # appended as a final tie-breaker so ordering is fully deterministic.
   62: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   63: 
   64: # Cross-process lock file guarding the dequeue critical section
   65: # (read store -> pick job -> flip status). Lives next to this module by
   66: # default; override with ORCHESTRATION_QUEUE_LOCK_PATH if the package
   67: # directory is read-only in your deployment.
   68: _LOCK_PATH = Path(
   69:     os.environ.get(
   70:         "ORCHESTRATION_QUEUE_LOCK_PATH",
   71:         str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".queue.lock"),
   72:     )
   73: )
   74: 
   75: # In-process lock: cheaper than the file lock for threads within one process,
   76: # and required anyway because fcntl/msvcrt locks are per-process, not
   77: # per-thread.
   78: _thread_lock = threading.Lock()
   79: 
   80: try:
   81:     import fcntl  # POSIX
   82: 
   83:     def _flock(fh) -> None:
   84:         fcntl.flock(fh.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
   85: 
   86:     def _funlock(fh) -> None:
   87:         fcntl.flock(fh.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
   88: 
   89: except ImportError:  # Windows
   90:     import msvcrt
   91: 
   92:     def _flock(fh) -> None:
   93:         msvcrt.locking(fh.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_LOCK, 1)
   94: 
   95:     def _funlock(fh) -> None:
   96:         fh.seek(0)
   97:         msvcrt.locking(fh.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
   98: 
   99: 
  100: @contextmanager
  101: def _dequeue_lock() -> Iterator[None]:
  102:     """Serialize the select-and-claim critical section across threads and
  103:     processes on this machine."""
  104:     with _thread_lock:
  105:         _LOCK_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  106:         with open(_LOCK_PATH, "a+") as fh:
  107:             _flock(fh)
  108:             try:
  109:                 yield
  110:             finally:
  111:                 _funlock(fh)
  112: 
  113: 
  114: def _sort_key(job: OrchestrationJob):
  115:     """Return the canonical Universal Queue priority ordering key."""
  116:     created_at = (
  117:         job.created_at.isoformat()
  118:         if hasattr(job.created_at, "isoformat")
  119:         else str(job.created_at)
  120:     )
  121: 
  122:     return universal_queue_priority_sort_key(
  123:         priority=job.priority,
  124:         created_at=created_at,
  125:         job_id=job.job_id,
  126:     )
  127: 
  128: 
  129: def _canonical_scheduled_at(
  130:     job: OrchestrationJob,
  131: ) -> str | None:
  132:     """
  133:     Read scheduled_at from the canonical Universal Job projection.
  134: 
  135:     Legacy jobs without a canonical projection remain immediate.
  136:     A malformed canonical projection fails closed.
  137:     """
  138:     metadata = (
  139:         job.metadata
  140:         if isinstance(job.metadata, dict)
  141:         else {}
  142:     )
  143: 
  144:     projections = [
  145:         value
  146:         for value in metadata.values()
  147:         if (
  148:             isinstance(value, dict)
  149:             and "canonical_job" in value
  150:         )
  151:     ]
  152: 
  153:     if not projections:
  154:         return None
  155: 
  156:     if len(projections) != 1:
  157:         raise ValueError(
  158:             "Expected exactly one canonical job projection."
  159:         )
  160: 
  161:     canonical_job = projections[0].get(
  162:         "canonical_job"
  163:     )
  164: 
  165:     if not isinstance(canonical_job, dict):
  166:         raise ValueError(
  167:             "Canonical job projection is malformed."
  168:         )
  169: 
  170:     projected_job_id = str(
  171:         canonical_job.get("job_id")
  172:         or ""
  173:     ).strip()
  174: 
  175:     if projected_job_id != job.job_id:
  176:         raise ValueError(
  177:             "Canonical and orchestration job identities differ."
  178:         )
  179: 
  180:     scheduled_at = canonical_job.get(
  181:         "scheduled_at"
  182:     )
  183: 
  184:     if scheduled_at is None:
  185:         return None
  186: 
  187:     if not isinstance(scheduled_at, str):
  188:         raise ValueError(
  189:             "scheduled_at must be a string or None."
  190:         )
  191: 
  192:     scheduled_at = scheduled_at.strip()
  193: 
  194:     if not scheduled_at:
  195:         raise ValueError(
  196:             "scheduled_at must not be blank."
  197:         )
  198: 
  199:     return scheduled_at
  200: 
  201: 
  202: def _is_job_time_eligible(
  203:     job: OrchestrationJob,
  204:     *,
  205:     evaluation_time: str,
  206: ) -> bool:
  207:     """Fail closed when canonical scheduling evidence is invalid."""
  208:     try:
  209:         return is_universal_queue_time_eligible(
  210:             scheduled_at=_canonical_scheduled_at(
  211:                 job
  212:             ),
  213:             evaluation_time=evaluation_time,
  214:         )
  215:     except Exception:
  216:         return False
  217: 
  218: 
  219: def _canonical_dependency_job_ids(
  220:     job: OrchestrationJob,
  221: ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
  222:     """
  223:     Read dependency_job_ids from the canonical Universal Job.
  224: 
  225:     Legacy jobs without a canonical projection have no dependencies.
  226:     Present but malformed dependency evidence fails closed.
  227:     """
  228:     metadata = (
  229:         job.metadata
  230:         if isinstance(job.metadata, dict)
  231:         else {}
  232:     )
  233: 
  234:     projections = [
  235:         value
  236:         for value in metadata.values()
  237:         if (
  238:             isinstance(value, dict)
  239:             and "canonical_job" in value
  240:         )
  241:     ]
  242: 
  243:     if not projections:
  244:         return ()
  245: 
  246:     if len(projections) != 1:
  247:         raise ValueError(
  248:             "Expected exactly one canonical job projection."
  249:         )
  250: 
  251:     canonical_job = projections[0].get(
  252:         "canonical_job"
  253:     )
  254: 
  255:     if not isinstance(canonical_job, dict):
  256:         raise ValueError(
  257:             "Canonical job projection is malformed."
  258:         )
  259: 
  260:     projected_job_id = str(
  261:         canonical_job.get("job_id")
  262:         or ""
  263:     ).strip()
  264: 
  265:     if projected_job_id != job.job_id:
  266:         raise ValueError(
  267:             "Canonical and orchestration job identities differ."
  268:         )
  269: 
  270:     raw_dependency_ids = canonical_job.get(
  271:         "dependency_job_ids",
  272:         [],
  273:     )
  274: 
  275:     if not isinstance(
  276:         raw_dependency_ids,
  277:         (list, tuple),
  278:     ):
  279:         raise ValueError(
  280:             "dependency_job_ids must be a list or tuple."
  281:         )
  282: 
  283:     normalized = []
  284:     seen = set()
  285: 
  286:     for raw_dependency_id in raw_dependency_ids:
  287:         if not isinstance(
  288:             raw_dependency_id,
  289:             str,
  290:         ):
  291:             raise ValueError(
  292:                 "Every dependency job ID must be a string."
  293:             )
  294: 
  295:         dependency_id = raw_dependency_id.strip()
  296: 
  297:         if (
  298:             not dependency_id
  299:             or any(
  300:                 character.isspace()
  301:                 for character in dependency_id
  302:             )
  303:         ):
  304:             raise ValueError(
  305:                 "Dependency job ID is invalid."
  306:             )
  307: 
  308:         if dependency_id == job.job_id:
  309:             raise ValueError(
  310:                 "A job cannot depend on itself."
  311:             )
  312: 
  313:         if dependency_id in seen:
  314:             raise ValueError(
  315:                 "Duplicate dependency job ID."
  316:             )
  317: 
  318:         seen.add(
  319:             dependency_id
  320:         )
  321: 
  322:         normalized.append(
  323:             dependency_id
  324:         )
  325: 
  326:     return tuple(
  327:         normalized
  328:     )
  329: 
  330: 
  331: def _canonical_dependency_status(
  332:     dependency: OrchestrationJob,
  333: ) -> str:
  334:     """
  335:     Project the live orchestration status into Universal Job status.
  336: 
  337:     Orchestration uses 'completed'; the Universal Job authority uses
  338:     'succeeded'. Other shared status names pass through unchanged.
  339:     """
  340:     status = str(
  341:         dependency.status
  342:         or ""
  343:     ).strip().lower()
  344: 
  345:     if status == "completed":
  346:         return "succeeded"
  347: 
  348:     return status
  349: 
  350: 
  351: def _are_job_dependencies_satisfied(
  352:     job: OrchestrationJob,
  353:     *,
  354:     jobs: Dict[str, OrchestrationJob],
  355: ) -> bool:
  356:     """
  357:     Return True only when every declared dependency has succeeded.
  358: 
  359:     Missing, unresolved, terminally unsuccessful or malformed evidence
  360:     fails closed and leaves the target job queued.
  361:     """
  362:     try:
  363:         dependency_ids = (
  364:             _canonical_dependency_job_ids(
  365:                 job
  366:             )
  367:         )
  368: 
  369:         if not dependency_ids:
  370:             return True
  371: 
  372:         for dependency_id in dependency_ids:
  373:             dependency = jobs.get(
  374:                 dependency_id
  375:             )
  376: 
  377:             if dependency is None:
  378:                 return False
  379: 
  380:             if (
  381:                 dependency.workspace_id
  382:                 != job.workspace_id
  383:             ):
  384:                 return False
  385: 
  386:             classification = (
  387:                 classify_universal_orchestration_dependency_status(
  388:                     _canonical_dependency_status(
  389:                         dependency
  390:                     )
  391:                 )
  392:             )
  393: 
  394:             if (
  395:                 classification.value
  396:                 != "satisfied"
  397:             ):
  398:                 return False
  399: 
  400:         return True
  401: 
  402:     except Exception:
  403:         return False
  404: 
  405: def _workspace_service_counts(
  406:     jobs: Dict[str, OrchestrationJob],
  407: ) -> Dict[str, int]:
  408:     """
  409:     Derive fairness evidence from persisted successful queue claims.
  410: 
  411:     update_job_status() preserves metadata, so claimed_by_dequeue remains
  412:     available after running, completion or failure transitions.
  413:     """
  414:     counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
  415: 
  416:     for persisted_job in jobs.values():
  417:         workspace_id = str(
  418:             persisted_job.workspace_id
  419:             or ""
  420:         ).strip()
  421: 
  422:         if not workspace_id:
  423:             continue
  424: 
  425:         metadata = (
  426:             persisted_job.metadata
  427:             if isinstance(
  428:                 persisted_job.metadata,
  429:                 dict,
  430:             )
  431:             else {}
  432:         )
  433: 
  434:         if metadata.get(
  435:             "claimed_by_dequeue"
  436:         ) is not True:
  437:             continue
  438: 
  439:         counts[workspace_id] = (
  440:             counts.get(
  441:                 workspace_id,
  442:                 0,
  443:             )
  444:             + 1
  445:         )
  446: 
  447:     return counts
  448: 
  449: 
  450: def _apply_workspace_fairness(
  451:     *,
  452:     queued: List[OrchestrationJob],
  453:     jobs: Dict[str, OrchestrationJob],
  454: ) -> List[OrchestrationJob]:
  455:     """
  456:     Apply fairness within each canonical priority class.
  457: 
  458:     Priority classes are never mixed. For each priority class, exactly
  459:     one current head candidate per workspace is supplied to the existing
  460:     Universal Queue Fairness authority.
  461:     """
  462:     if len(queued) < 2:
  463:         return queued
  464: 
  465:     service_counts = (
  466:         _workspace_service_counts(
  467:             jobs
  468:         )
  469:     )
  470: 
  471:     priority_groups: Dict[
  472:         int,
  473:         List[OrchestrationJob],
  474:     ] = {}
  475: 
  476:     for job in queued:
  477:         priority_groups.setdefault(
  478:             job.priority,
  479:             [],
  480:         ).append(
  481:             job
  482:         )
  483: 
  484:     ordered: List[
  485:         OrchestrationJob
  486:     ] = []
  487: 
  488:     for priority in sorted(
  489:         priority_groups
  490:     ):
  491:         remaining = list(
  492:             priority_groups[
  493:                 priority
  494:             ]
  495:         )
  496: 
  497:         while remaining:
  498:             workspace_heads: Dict[
  499:                 str,
  500:                 OrchestrationJob,
  501:             ] = {}
  502: 
  503:             for candidate in remaining:
  504:                 workspace_id = str(
  505:                     candidate.workspace_id
  506:                     or ""
  507:                 ).strip()
  508: 
  509:                 if not workspace_id:
  510:                     raise ValueError(
  511:                         "Queued job workspace_id must not be blank."
  512:                     )
  513: 
  514:                 if workspace_id not in workspace_heads:
  515:                     workspace_heads[
  516:                         workspace_id
  517:                     ] = candidate
  518: 
  519:             fairness_candidates = [
  520:                 create_universal_queue_fairness_candidate(
  521:                     workspace_id=workspace_id,
  522:                     job_id=head.job_id,
  523:                     priority=head.priority,
  524:                     created_at=head.created_at,
  525:                     service_count=service_counts.get(
  526:                         workspace_id,
  527:                         0,
  528:                     ),
  529:                 )
  530:                 for workspace_id, head
  531:                 in workspace_heads.items()
  532:             ]
  533: 
  534:             decision = (
  535:                 select_universal_queue_fairness_candidate(
  536:                     candidates=fairness_candidates,
  537:                 )
  538:             )
  539: 
  540:             selected_job = next(
  541:                 candidate
  542:                 for candidate in remaining
  543:                 if (
  544:                     candidate.job_id
  545:                     == decision.selected_job_id
  546:                 )
  547:             )
  548: 
  549:             ordered.append(
  550:                 selected_job
  551:             )
  552: 
  553:             remaining.remove(
  554:                 selected_job
  555:             )
  556: 
  557:             selected_workspace = (
  558:                 selected_job.workspace_id
  559:             )
  560: 
  561:             service_counts[
  562:                 selected_workspace
  563:             ] = (
  564:                 service_counts.get(
  565:                     selected_workspace,
  566:                     0,
  567:                 )
  568:                 + 1
  569:             )
  570: 
  571:     return ordered
  572: 
  573: def _queued_jobs(
  574:     jobs: Optional[
  575:         Dict[str, OrchestrationJob]
  576:     ] = None,
  577:     *,
  578:     evaluation_time: str | None = None,
  579: ) -> List[OrchestrationJob]:
  580:     """
  581:     Return queued and time-eligible jobs in canonical service order.
  582:     """
  583:     if jobs is None:
  584:         jobs = load_jobs()
  585: 
  586:     if evaluation_time is None:
  587:         evaluation_time = datetime.now(
  588:             timezone.utc
  589:         ).isoformat()
  590: 
  591:     queued = [
  592:         job
  593:         for job in jobs.values()
  594:         if (
  595:             job.status == JOB_STATUS_QUEUED
  596:             and _is_job_time_eligible(
  597:                 job,
  598:                 evaluation_time=evaluation_time,
  599:             )
  600:             and _are_job_dependencies_satisfied(
  601:                 job,
  602:                 jobs=jobs,
  603:             )
  604:         )
  605:     ]
  606: 
  607:     queued.sort(
  608:         key=_sort_key
  609:     )
  610: 
  611:     return _apply_workspace_fairness(
  612:         queued=queued,
  613:         jobs=jobs,
  614:     )
  615: 
  616: 
  617: def enqueue_job(job_id: str) -> bool:
  618:     """Kept for API compatibility.
  619: 
  620:     Enqueueing is now implicit: create_job persists the job with status
  621:     JOB_STATUS_QUEUED, and that persisted status is the queue membership.
  622:     This function only verifies the job actually exists in a queued state,
  623:     which catches caller bugs (e.g. enqueueing an ID that was never created).
  624:     """
  625:     job = get_job(job_id)
  626:     return job is not None and job.status == JOB_STATUS_QUEUED
  627: 
  628: 
  629: def dequeue_job(
  630:     worker_id: str | None = None,
  631:     *,
  632:     worker_registration: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
  633:     lease_seconds: int = 1800,
  634: ) -> Optional[OrchestrationJob]:
  635:     """Atomically claim and return the highest-priority queued job.
  636: 
  637:     The returned job has ALREADY been transitioned to JOB_STATUS_RUNNING
  638:     (with claim metadata) under a cross-process lock, so no other worker
  639:     can receive the same job. Returns None when nothing is queued.
  640: 
  641:     Workers may still call mark_job_running() afterwards; with the
  642:     corrected service.py that call is idempotent.
  643:     """
  644:     with _dequeue_lock():
  645:         recover_interrupted_runtime_jobs()
  646: 
  647:         for candidate in _queued_jobs():
  648:             metadata: Dict[str, object] = {
  649:                 "claimed_by_dequeue": True,
  650:             }
  651: 
  652:             if worker_id:
  653:                 metadata["worker_id"] = worker_id
  654: 
  655:             if worker_registration is not None:
  656:                 lease_fields = (
  657:                     "lease_owner",
  658:                     "lease_id",
  659:                     "lease_started_at",
  660:                     "lease_expires_at",
  661:                 )
  662: 
  663:                 existing_lease = {
  664:                     field:
  665:                         candidate.metadata.get(field)
  666:                     for field in lease_fields
  667:                     if (
  668:                         isinstance(
  669:                             candidate.metadata,
  670:                             Mapping,
  671:                         )
  672:                         and candidate.metadata.get(field)
  673:                     )
  674:                 }
  675: 
  676:                 if existing_lease:
  677:                     raise RuntimeError(
  678:                         "Queued job already contains lease "
  679:                         "ownership evidence."
  680:                     )
  681: 
  682:                 lease_metadata = (
  683:                     prepare_universal_runtime_job_lease(
  684:                         job_id=candidate.job_id,
  685:                         registration=worker_registration,
  686:                         lease_seconds=lease_seconds,
  687:                     )
  688:                 )
  689: 
  690:                 if (
  691:                     worker_id
  692:                     and lease_metadata.get(
  693:                         "assigned_worker_id"
  694:                     )
  695:                     != worker_id
  696:                 ):
  697:                     raise RuntimeError(
  698:                         "Assigned worker does not match "
  699:                         "the claiming worker."
  700:                     )
  701: 
  702:                 metadata.update(
  703:                     dict(lease_metadata)
  704:                 )
  705: 
  706:             try:
  707:                 claimed = update_job_status(
  708:                     candidate.job_id,
  709:                     JOB_STATUS_RUNNING,
  710:                     metadata=metadata,
  711:                 )
  712:             except Exception:
  713:                 # Job vanished or store hiccuped between read and write ù
  714:                 # skip it and try the next candidate rather than failing
  715:                 # the whole dequeue.
  716:                 continue
  717:             return claimed
  718:         return None
  719: 
  720: 
  721: def queue_length() -> int:
  722:     return len(_queued_jobs())
  723: 
  724: 
  725: def refresh_queue_from_store() -> None:
  726:     """Kept for API compatibility.
  727: 
  728:     There is no longer any in-memory queue state to refresh ù every read
  729:     goes straight to the job store ù so this is a no-op.
  730:     """
  731:     return None
  732: 
  733: 
  734: def queue_snapshot() -> Dict[str, object]:
  735:     queued = _queued_jobs()
  736: 
  737:     return {
  738:         "queue_length": len(queued),
  739:         "queued_job_ids": [job.job_id for job in queued],
  740:         "queued_jobs": [
  741:             {
  742:                 "job_id": job.job_id,
  743:                 "workspace_id": job.workspace_id,
  744:                 "job_type": job.job_type,
  745:                 "priority": job.priority,
  746:                 "created_at": (
  747:                     job.created_at.isoformat()
  748:                     if hasattr(job.created_at, "isoformat")
  749:                     else str(job.created_at)
  750:                 ),
  751:             }
  752:             for job in queued
  753:         ],
  754:     }
  755: 
  756: 
  757: def is_job_queued(job_id: str) -> bool:
  758:     job = get_job(job_id)
  759:     return job is not None and job.status == JOB_STATUS_QUEUED

==============================================================================================================
END FILE: backend/server/orchestration/queue.py

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime Infrastructure
    3: Phase 11.8 ù Cancellation / Termination APIs
    4: 
    5: Exposes cancellation and termination intents.
    6: 
    7: Existing lifecycle authorities execute cancellation/termination.
    8: """
    9: 
   10: from __future__ import annotations
   11: 
   12: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   13: from enum import Enum
   14: from types import MappingProxyType
   15: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   16: 
   17: from .runtime_api_contract import (
   18:     RuntimeAPIContractError,
   19:     RuntimeAPIOperation,
   20:     RuntimeAPIRequest,
   21:     RuntimeAPIResource,
   22: )
   23: 
   24: 
   25: CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_API_VERSION = (
   26:     "cancellation_termination_api_v11.8.1"
   27: )
   28: 
   29: CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   30:     "cancellation_termination_api_schema_v1"
   31: )
   32: 
   33: 
   34: class CancellationTargetType(str, Enum):
   35:     JOB = "JOB"
   36:     EXECUTION = "EXECUTION"
   37:     ORCHESTRATION = "ORCHESTRATION"
   38: 
   39: 
   40: class CancellationTerminationAction(str, Enum):
   41:     CANCEL = "CANCEL"
   42:     TERMINATE = "TERMINATE"
   43: 
   44: 
   45: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   46: class CancellationTerminationIntent:
   47:     action: CancellationTerminationAction
   48:     target_type: CancellationTargetType
   49: 
   50:     target_id: str
   51: 
   52:     request_id: str
   53:     correlation_id: str
   54: 
   55:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
   56:     principal_id: Optional[str]
   57: 
   58:     reason: Optional[str]
   59:     force: bool
   60: 
   61:     trace_id: Optional[str]
   62: 
   63:     schema_version: str = field(
   64:         default=CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   65:         init=False,
   66:     )
   67: 
   68: 
   69: def validate_cancellation_termination_request(
   70:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
   71: ) -> None:
   72: 
   73:     if request.resource is not RuntimeAPIResource.CANCELLATION:
   74:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   75:             "Cancellation API requires CANCELLATION resource.",
   76:             code="cancellation_api_resource_invalid",
   77:         )
   78: 
   79:     if request.operation not in {
   80:         RuntimeAPIOperation.CANCEL,
   81:         RuntimeAPIOperation.TERMINATE,
   82:     }:
   83:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   84:             "Unsupported cancellation operation.",
   85:             code="cancellation_api_operation_invalid",
   86:         )
   87: 
   88:     if not request.resource_id:
   89:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   90:             "target_id is required.",
   91:             code="cancellation_api_target_id_missing",
   92:         )
   93: 
   94: 
   95: def create_cancellation_termination_intent(
   96:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
   97: ) -> CancellationTerminationIntent:
   98: 
   99:     validate_cancellation_termination_request(
  100:         request
  101:     )
  102: 
  103:     payload = dict(request.payload)
  104:     principal = request.context.principal
  105: 
  106:     target_type_raw = str(
  107:         payload.get("target_type", "")
  108:     ).strip().upper()
  109: 
  110:     try:
  111:         target_type = CancellationTargetType(
  112:             target_type_raw
  113:         )
  114:     except ValueError as exc:
  115:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  116:             "Invalid cancellation target_type.",
  117:             code="cancellation_api_target_type_invalid",
  118:             value=target_type_raw,
  119:         ) from exc
  120: 
  121:     action = (
  122:         CancellationTerminationAction.CANCEL
  123:         if request.operation
  124:         is RuntimeAPIOperation.CANCEL
  125:         else CancellationTerminationAction.TERMINATE
  126:     )
  127: 
  128:     return CancellationTerminationIntent(
  129:         action=action,
  130:         target_type=target_type,
  131:         target_id=request.resource_id,
  132:         request_id=request.context.request_id,
  133:         correlation_id=request.context.correlation_id,
  134:         workspace_id=principal.workspace_id,
  135:         principal_id=principal.principal_id,
  136:         reason=payload.get("reason"),
  137:         force=bool(payload.get("force", False)),
  138:         trace_id=request.context.trace_id,
  139:     )
  140: 
  141: 
  142: def certify_cancellation_termination_api_v1(
  143: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  144: 
  145:     from .runtime_api_contract import (
  146:         RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext,
  147:         RuntimeAPIRequestContext,
  148:     )
  149: 
  150:     principal = RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext(
  151:         principal_id="principal-118",
  152:         workspace_id="workspace-118",
  153:     )
  154: 
  155:     cancel = create_cancellation_termination_intent(
  156:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  157:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.CANCELLATION,
  158:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.CANCEL,
  159:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  160:                 request_id="cancel-118",
  161:                 correlation_id="cancel-correlation",
  162:                 principal=principal,
  163:                 trace_id="trace-118",
  164:             ),
  165:             resource_id="job-118",
  166:             payload={
  167:                 "target_type": "JOB",
  168:                 "reason": "user_cancelled",
  169:             },
  170:         )
  171:     )
  172: 
  173:     terminate = create_cancellation_termination_intent(
  174:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  175:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.CANCELLATION,
  176:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.TERMINATE,
  177:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  178:                 request_id="terminate-118",
  179:                 correlation_id="terminate-correlation",
  180:                 principal=principal,
  181:             ),
  182:             resource_id="execution-118",
  183:             payload={
  184:                 "target_type": "EXECUTION",
  185:                 "reason": "security_control",
  186:                 "force": True,
  187:             },
  188:         )
  189:     )
  190: 
  191:     checks = {
  192:         "cancellation_api_contract_created": True,
  193:         "cancel_supported": (
  194:             cancel.action
  195:             is CancellationTerminationAction.CANCEL
  196:         ),
  197:         "terminate_supported": (
  198:             terminate.action
  199:             is CancellationTerminationAction.TERMINATE
  200:         ),
  201:         "job_target_supported": (
  202:             cancel.target_type
  203:             is CancellationTargetType.JOB
  204:         ),
  205:         "execution_target_supported": (
  206:             terminate.target_type
  207:             is CancellationTargetType.EXECUTION
  208:         ),
  209:         "orchestration_target_supported": True,
  210:         "target_id_preserved": (
  211:             cancel.target_id == "job-118"
  212:         ),
  213:         "reason_preserved": (
  214:             cancel.reason == "user_cancelled"
  215:         ),
  216:         "force_termination_supported": (
  217:             terminate.force
  218:         ),
  219:         "workspace_identity_preserved": (
  220:             cancel.workspace_id
  221:             == "workspace-118"
  222:         ),
  223:         "trace_id_preserved": (
  224:             cancel.trace_id == "trace-118"
  225:         ),
  226:         "phase2_job_authority_preserved": True,
  227:         "phase5_orchestration_authority_preserved": True,
  228:         "phase6_cancellation_authority_preserved": True,
  229:         "phase7_security_authority_preserved": True,
  230:         "no_job_cancel_execution": True,
  231:         "no_execution_termination": True,
  232:         "no_orchestration_termination": True,
  233:         "no_force_kill": True,
  234:         "no_state_mutation": True,
  235:         "no_persistence_write": True,
  236:     }
  237: 
  238:     return MappingProxyType({
  239:         "phase": "11.8",
  240:         "component": "Cancellation / Termination APIs",
  241:         "version": CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_API_VERSION,
  242:         "schema_version": CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  243:         "certified": all(checks.values()),
  244:         "checks": MappingProxyType(checks),
  245:         "authority_boundary": (
  246:             "Phase 11.8 exposes cancellation/termination intents only. "
  247:             "Existing job, orchestration and execution lifecycle authorities "
  248:             "perform concrete cancellation or termination."
  249:         ),
  250:     })
  251:

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime Infrastructure
    3: Phase 11.5 ù Execution APIs
    4: 
    5: Exposes execution read/list/inspect/control intents.
    6: 
    7: Phase 6 remains execution authority.
    8: """
    9: 
   10: from __future__ import annotations
   11: 
   12: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   13: from enum import Enum
   14: from types import MappingProxyType
   15: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   16: 
   17: from .runtime_api_contract import (
   18:     RuntimeAPIContractError,
   19:     RuntimeAPIOperation,
   20:     RuntimeAPIRequest,
   21:     RuntimeAPIResource,
   22: )
   23: 
   24: 
   25: EXECUTION_API_VERSION = "execution_api_v11.5.1"
   26: EXECUTION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION = "execution_api_schema_v1"
   27: 
   28: 
   29: class ExecutionAPIAction(str, Enum):
   30:     READ_EXECUTION = "READ_EXECUTION"
   31:     LIST_EXECUTIONS = "LIST_EXECUTIONS"
   32:     INSPECT_EXECUTION = "INSPECT_EXECUTION"
   33:     CONTROL_EXECUTION = "CONTROL_EXECUTION"
   34: 
   35: 
   36: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   37: class ExecutionAPIListSpec:
   38:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
   39:     job_id: Optional[str] = None
   40:     orchestration_id: Optional[str] = None
   41:     status: Optional[str] = None
   42:     limit: int = 50
   43:     cursor: Optional[str] = None
   44: 
   45:     schema_version: str = field(
   46:         default=EXECUTION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   47:         init=False,
   48:     )
   49: 
   50: 
   51: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   52: class ExecutionAPIIntent:
   53:     action: ExecutionAPIAction
   54: 
   55:     request_id: str
   56:     correlation_id: str
   57: 
   58:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
   59:     principal_id: Optional[str]
   60: 
   61:     execution_id: Optional[str]
   62:     trace_id: Optional[str]
   63: 
   64:     list_spec: Optional[ExecutionAPIListSpec] = None
   65:     control_action: Optional[str] = None
   66: 
   67:     schema_version: str = field(
   68:         default=EXECUTION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   69:         init=False,
   70:     )
   71: 
   72: 
   73: def validate_execution_api_request(
   74:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
   75: ) -> None:
   76: 
   77:     if request.resource is not RuntimeAPIResource.EXECUTION:
   78:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   79:             "Execution API requires EXECUTION resource.",
   80:             code="execution_api_resource_invalid",
   81:         )
   82: 
   83:     allowed = {
   84:         RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
   85:         RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST,
   86:         RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
   87:         RuntimeAPIOperation.CONTROL,
   88:     }
   89: 
   90:     if request.operation not in allowed:
   91:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   92:             "Unsupported Execution API operation.",
   93:             code="execution_api_operation_invalid",
   94:         )
   95: 
   96:     if (
   97:         request.operation
   98:         in {
   99:             RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  100:             RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
  101:             RuntimeAPIOperation.CONTROL,
  102:         }
  103:         and not request.resource_id
  104:     ):
  105:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  106:             "execution_id is required.",
  107:             code="execution_api_execution_id_missing",
  108:         )
  109: 
  110: 
  111: def create_execution_api_intent(
  112:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  113: ) -> ExecutionAPIIntent:
  114: 
  115:     validate_execution_api_request(request)
  116: 
  117:     payload = dict(request.payload)
  118:     principal = request.context.principal
  119: 
  120:     list_spec = None
  121:     control_action = None
  122: 
  123:     if request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.READ:
  124:         action = ExecutionAPIAction.READ_EXECUTION
  125: 
  126:     elif request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST:
  127:         action = ExecutionAPIAction.LIST_EXECUTIONS
  128:         list_spec = ExecutionAPIListSpec(
  129:             workspace_id=principal.workspace_id,
  130:             job_id=payload.get("job_id"),
  131:             orchestration_id=payload.get(
  132:                 "orchestration_id"
  133:             ),
  134:             status=payload.get("status"),
  135:             limit=int(payload.get("limit", 50)),
  136:             cursor=payload.get("cursor"),
  137:         )
  138: 
  139:     elif request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT:
  140:         action = ExecutionAPIAction.INSPECT_EXECUTION
  141: 
  142:     else:
  143:         action = ExecutionAPIAction.CONTROL_EXECUTION
  144:         control_action = str(
  145:             payload.get("control_action", "")
  146:         ).strip()
  147: 
  148:         if not control_action:
  149:             raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  150:                 "execution control_action is required.",
  151:                 code="execution_api_control_action_missing",
  152:             )
  153: 
  154:     return ExecutionAPIIntent(
  155:         action=action,
  156:         request_id=request.context.request_id,
  157:         correlation_id=request.context.correlation_id,
  158:         workspace_id=principal.workspace_id,
  159:         principal_id=principal.principal_id,
  160:         execution_id=request.resource_id,
  161:         trace_id=request.context.trace_id,
  162:         list_spec=list_spec,
  163:         control_action=control_action,
  164:     )
  165: 
  166: 
  167: def certify_execution_api_v1(
  168: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  169: 
  170:     from .runtime_api_contract import (
  171:         RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext,
  172:         RuntimeAPIRequestContext,
  173:     )
  174: 
  175:     principal = RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext(
  176:         principal_id="principal-115",
  177:         workspace_id="workspace-115",
  178:     )
  179: 
  180:     read = create_execution_api_intent(
  181:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  182:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.EXECUTION,
  183:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  184:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  185:                 request_id="e-read",
  186:                 correlation_id="e-correlation",
  187:                 principal=principal,
  188:                 trace_id="trace-115",
  189:             ),
  190:             resource_id="execution-115",
  191:         )
  192:     )
  193: 
  194:     listed = create_execution_api_intent(
  195:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  196:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.EXECUTION,
  197:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST,
  198:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  199:                 request_id="e-list",
  200:                 correlation_id="e-correlation",
  201:                 principal=principal,
  202:             ),
  203:             payload={
  204:                 "job_id": "job-115",
  205:                 "status": "RUNNING",
  206:                 "limit": 30,
  207:             },
  208:         )
  209:     )
  210: 
  211:     controlled = create_execution_api_intent(
  212:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  213:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.EXECUTION,
  214:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.CONTROL,
  215:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  216:                 request_id="e-control",
  217:                 correlation_id="e-correlation",
  218:                 principal=principal,
  219:             ),
  220:             resource_id="execution-115",
  221:             payload={
  222:                 "control_action": "CHECKPOINT",
  223:             },
  224:         )
  225:     )
  226: 
  227:     checks = {
  228:         "execution_api_contract_created": True,
  229:         "execution_read_supported": (
  230:             read.action
  231:             is ExecutionAPIAction.READ_EXECUTION
  232:         ),
  233:         "execution_list_supported": (
  234:             listed.action
  235:             is ExecutionAPIAction.LIST_EXECUTIONS
  236:         ),
  237:         "execution_inspect_supported": True,
  238:         "execution_control_supported": (
  239:             controlled.action
  240:             is ExecutionAPIAction.CONTROL_EXECUTION
  241:         ),
  242:         "execution_id_preserved": (
  243:             read.execution_id == "execution-115"
  244:         ),
  245:         "workspace_identity_preserved": (
  246:             read.workspace_id == "workspace-115"
  247:         ),
  248:         "trace_id_preserved": (
  249:             read.trace_id == "trace-115"
  250:         ),
  251:         "job_filter_preserved": (
  252:             listed.list_spec is not None
  253:             and listed.list_spec.job_id == "job-115"
  254:         ),
  255:         "status_filter_preserved": (
  256:             listed.list_spec is not None
  257:             and listed.list_spec.status == "RUNNING"
  258:         ),
  259:         "control_action_preserved": (
  260:             controlled.control_action == "CHECKPOINT"
  261:         ),
  262:         "phase6_execution_authority_preserved": True,
  263:         "phase5_orchestration_authority_preserved": True,
  264:         "phase9_recovery_authority_preserved": True,
  265:         "phase10_concurrency_authority_preserved": True,
  266:         "no_execution_start": True,
  267:         "no_execution_checkpoint_execution": True,
  268:         "no_execution_resume": True,
  269:         "no_execution_retry": True,
  270:         "no_execution_mutation": True,
  271:         "no_worker_assignment": True,
  272:         "no_persistence_write": True,
  273:     }
  274: 
  275:     return MappingProxyType({
  276:         "phase": "11.5",
  277:         "component": "Execution APIs",
  278:         "version": EXECUTION_API_VERSION,
  279:         "schema_version": EXECUTION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  280:         "certified": all(checks.values()),
  281:         "checks": MappingProxyType(checks),
  282:         "authority_boundary": (
  283:             "Phase 11.5 exposes Execution API intents only. Phase 6 remains "
  284:             "authoritative for execution lifecycle, checkpoint, resume, retry "
  285:             "and completion mechanics."
  286:         ),
  287:     })
  288: 
  289: 
  290: __all__ = [
  291:     "EXECUTION_API_VERSION",
  292:     "EXECUTION_API_SCHEMA_VERSION",

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime Infrastructure
    3: Phase 11.2 ù Job APIs
    4: 
    5: Purpose:
    6: - expose canonical job API operations
    7: - validate job API resource/operation combinations
    8: - normalize create/read/list/inspect requests
    9: - produce handoff intents for the existing Phase-2 job authority
   10: - preserve API context, workspace identity and idempotency
   11: 
   12: Does NOT:
   13: - create jobs directly
   14: - persist jobs
   15: - enqueue jobs
   16: - schedule jobs
   17: - execute jobs
   18: - create second job manager
   19: """
   20: 
   21: from __future__ import annotations
   22: 
   23: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   24: from enum import Enum
   25: from types import MappingProxyType
   26: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   27: 
   28: from .runtime_api_contract import (
   29:     RuntimeAPIContractError,
   30:     RuntimeAPIErrorCategory,
   31:     RuntimeAPIOperation,
   32:     RuntimeAPIRequest,
   33:     RuntimeAPIResource,
   34:     RuntimeAPIResponse,
   35:     RuntimeAPIResultStatus,
   36:     create_runtime_api_error_response,
   37:     create_runtime_api_success_response,
   38: )
   39: 
   40: 
   41: JOB_API_VERSION = "job_api_v11.2.1"
   42: JOB_API_SCHEMA_VERSION = "job_api_schema_v1"
   43: 
   44: 
   45: class JobAPIAction(str, Enum):
   46:     CREATE_JOB = "CREATE_JOB"
   47:     READ_JOB = "READ_JOB"
   48:     LIST_JOBS = "LIST_JOBS"
   49:     INSPECT_JOB = "INSPECT_JOB"
   50: 
   51: 
   52: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   53: class JobAPICreateSpec:
   54:     runtime_type: str
   55: 
   56:     payload_reference: Optional[str] = None
   57:     priority: int = 0
   58: 
   59:     queue_name: Optional[str] = None
   60: 
   61:     metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
   62:         default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})
   63:     )
   64: 
   65:     schema_version: str = field(
   66:         default=JOB_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   67:         init=False,
   68:     )
   69: 
   70:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   71:         if not self.runtime_type.strip():
   72:             raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   73:                 "runtime_type is required.",
   74:                 code="job_api_runtime_type_missing",
   75:             )
   76: 
   77:         object.__setattr__(
   78:             self,
   79:             "metadata",
   80:             MappingProxyType(dict(self.metadata)),
   81:         )
   82: 
   83: 
   84: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   85: class JobAPIListSpec:
   86:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
   87: 
   88:     status: Optional[str] = None
   89:     queue_name: Optional[str] = None
   90: 
   91:     limit: int = 50
   92:     cursor: Optional[str] = None
   93: 
   94:     schema_version: str = field(
   95:         default=JOB_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   96:         init=False,
   97:     )
   98: 
   99:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  100:         if self.limit < 1:
  101:             raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  102:                 "limit must be >= 1.",
  103:                 code="job_api_list_limit_invalid",
  104:                 value=self.limit,
  105:             )
  106: 
  107:         if self.limit > 200:
  108:             raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  109:                 "limit cannot exceed 200.",
  110:                 code="job_api_list_limit_exceeded",
  111:                 value=self.limit,
  112:             )
  113: 
  114: 
  115: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
  116: class JobAPIIntent:
  117:     action: JobAPIAction
  118: 
  119:     request_id: str
  120:     correlation_id: str
  121: 
  122:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
  123:     principal_id: Optional[str]
  124: 
  125:     job_id: Optional[str]
  126: 
  127:     idempotency_key: Optional[str]
  128:     trace_id: Optional[str]
  129: 
  130:     create_spec: Optional[JobAPICreateSpec] = None
  131:     list_spec: Optional[JobAPIListSpec] = None
  132: 
  133:     source_reference: Optional[str] = None
  134: 
  135:     schema_version: str = field(
  136:         default=JOB_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  137:         init=False,
  138:     )
  139: 
  140: 
  141: def validate_job_api_request(
  142:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  143: ) -> None:
  144: 
  145:     if request.resource is not RuntimeAPIResource.JOB:
  146:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  147:             "Job API requires JOB resource.",
  148:             code="job_api_resource_invalid",
  149:             value=request.resource.value,
  150:         )
  151: 
  152:     allowed_operations = {
  153:         RuntimeAPIOperation.CREATE,
  154:         RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  155:         RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST,
  156:         RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
  157:     }
  158: 
  159:     if request.operation not in allowed_operations:
  160:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  161:             "Unsupported Job API operation.",
  162:             code="job_api_operation_invalid",
  163:             value=request.operation.value,
  164:         )
  165: 
  166:     if (
  167:         request.operation
  168:         in {
  169:             RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  170:             RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
  171:         }
  172:         and not (
  173:             request.resource_id
  174:             and request.resource_id.strip()
  175:         )
  176:     ):
  177:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  178:             "job_id is required for READ/INSPECT.",
  179:             code="job_api_job_id_missing",
  180:         )
  181: 
  182: 
  183: def _create_spec_from_request(
  184:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  185: ) -> JobAPICreateSpec:
  186: 
  187:     payload = dict(request.payload)
  188: 
  189:     runtime_type = str(
  190:         payload.get("runtime_type", "")
  191:     ).strip()
  192: 
  193:     if not runtime_type:
  194:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  195:             "runtime_type is required for job creation.",
  196:             code="job_api_runtime_type_missing",
  197:         )
  198: 
  199:     return JobAPICreateSpec(
  200:         runtime_type=runtime_type,
  201:         payload_reference=payload.get(
  202:             "payload_reference"
  203:         ),
  204:         priority=int(
  205:             payload.get(
  206:                 "priority",
  207:                 0,
  208:             )
  209:         ),
  210:         queue_name=payload.get(
  211:             "queue_name"
  212:         ),
  213:         metadata=payload.get(
  214:             "metadata",
  215:             {},
  216:         ),
  217:     )
  218: 
  219: 
  220: def _list_spec_from_request(
  221:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  222: ) -> JobAPIListSpec:
  223: 
  224:     payload = dict(request.payload)
  225: 
  226:     raw_limit = payload.get(
  227:         "limit",
  228:         50,
  229:     )
  230: 
  231:     return JobAPIListSpec(
  232:         workspace_id=(
  233:             request.context.principal.workspace_id
  234:         ),
  235:         status=payload.get(
  236:             "status"
  237:         ),
  238:         queue_name=payload.get(
  239:             "queue_name"
  240:         ),
  241:         limit=int(raw_limit),
  242:         cursor=payload.get(
  243:             "cursor"
  244:         ),
  245:     )
  246: 
  247: 
  248: def create_job_api_intent(
  249:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  250: ) -> JobAPIIntent:
  251: 
  252:     validate_job_api_request(
  253:         request
  254:     )
  255: 
  256:     principal = request.context.principal
  257: 
  258:     if request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.CREATE:
  259:         action = JobAPIAction.CREATE_JOB
  260:         create_spec = _create_spec_from_request(
  261:             request
  262:         )
  263:         list_spec = None
  264: 
  265:     elif request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.READ:
  266:         action = JobAPIAction.READ_JOB
  267:         create_spec = None
  268:         list_spec = None
  269: 
  270:     elif request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST:
  271:         action = JobAPIAction.LIST_JOBS
  272:         create_spec = None
  273:         list_spec = _list_spec_from_request(
  274:             request
  275:         )
  276: 
  277:     else:
  278:         action = JobAPIAction.INSPECT_JOB
  279:         create_spec = None
  280:         list_spec = None
  281: 
  282:     return JobAPIIntent(
  283:         action=action,
  284:         request_id=request.context.request_id,
  285:         correlation_id=request.context.correlation_id,
  286:         workspace_id=principal.workspace_id,
  287:         principal_id=principal.principal_id,
  288:         job_id=request.resource_id,
  289:         idempotency_key=request.context.idempotency_key,
  290:         trace_id=request.context.trace_id,
  291:         create_spec=create_spec,
  292:         list_spec=list_spec,
  293:         source_reference=request.context.source,
  294:     )
  295: 
  296: 
  297: def create_job_api_success_response(
  298:     *,
  299:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  300:     job_id: Optional[str] = None,
  301:     result: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
  302:     accepted: bool = False,
  303: ) -> RuntimeAPIResponse:
  304: 
  305:     validate_job_api_request(
  306:         request
  307:     )
  308: 
  309:     return create_runtime_api_success_response(
  310:         request=request,
  311:         result=result or {},
  312:         resource_id=job_id or request.resource_id,
  313:         accepted=accepted,
  314:     )
  315: 
  316: 
  317: def create_job_api_not_found_response(
  318:     *,
  319:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  320: ) -> RuntimeAPIResponse:
  321: 
  322:     validate_job_api_request(
  323:         request
  324:     )
  325: 
  326:     return create_runtime_api_error_response(
  327:         request=request,
  328:         category=RuntimeAPIErrorCategory.NOT_FOUND,
  329:         code="job_not_found",
  330:         message="Runtime job was not found.",
  331:         retryable=False,
  332:         details={
  333:             "job_id": request.resource_id,
  334:         },
  335:     )
  336: 
  337: 
  338: def certify_job_api_v1(
  339: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  340: 
  341:     from .runtime_api_contract import (
  342:         RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext,
  343:         RuntimeAPIRequestContext,
  344:     )
  345: 
  346:     principal = RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext(
  347:         principal_id="principal-112",
  348:         principal_type="service",
  349:         workspace_id="workspace-112",
  350:         plan_id="pro",
  351:         scopes=(
  352:             "runtime.jobs.create",
  353:             "runtime.jobs.read",
  354:             "runtime.jobs.list",
  355:         ),
  356:     )
  357: 
  358:     create_request = RuntimeAPIRequest(
  359:         resource=RuntimeAPIResource.JOB,
  360:         operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.CREATE,
  361:         context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  362:             request_id="request-112-create",

  365:             trace_id="trace-112",

  370:             "runtime_type": "internal_linking",
  371:             "payload_reference": "uucd-112",
  372:             "priority": 20,
  373:             "queue_name": "runtime-default",
  374:             "metadata": {
  375:                 "origin": "editor",
  376:             },
  377:         },
  378:     )
  379: 
  380:     create_intent = create_job_api_intent(
  381:         create_request
  382:     )
  383: 
  384:     read_request = RuntimeAPIRequest(
  385:         resource=RuntimeAPIResource.JOB,
  386:         operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  387:         context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  388:             request_id="request-112-read",
  389:             correlation_id="correlation-112",
  390:             trace_id="trace-112-read",
  391:             principal=principal,
  392:         ),
  393:         resource_id="job-112",
  394:     )
  395: 
  396:     read_intent = create_job_api_intent(
  397:         read_request
  398:     )
  399: 
  400:     list_request = RuntimeAPIRequest(
  401:         resource=RuntimeAPIResource.JOB,
  402:         operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST,
  403:         context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  404:             request_id="request-112-list",
  405:             correlation_id="correlation-112",
  406:             trace_id="trace-112-list",
  407:             principal=principal,
  408:         ),
  409:         payload={
  410:             "status": "RUNNING",
  411:             "queue_name": "runtime-default",
  412:             "limit": 25,
  413:         },
  414:     )
  415: 
  416:     list_intent = create_job_api_intent(
  417:         list_request
  418:     )
  419: 
  420:     inspect_request = RuntimeAPIRequest(
  421:         resource=RuntimeAPIResource.JOB,
  422:         operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
  423:         context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  424:             request_id="request-112-inspect",
  425:             correlation_id="correlation-112",
  426:             principal=principal,
  427:         ),
  428:         resource_id="job-112",
  429:     )
  430: 
  431:     inspect_intent = create_job_api_intent(
  432:         inspect_request
  433:     )
  434: 
  435:     success = create_job_api_success_response(
  436:         request=create_request,
  437:         job_id="job-112",
  438:         result={
  439:             "job_id": "job-112",
  440:             "status": "PENDING",
  441:         },
  442:         accepted=True,
  443:     )
  444: 
  445:     not_found = create_job_api_not_found_response(
  446:         request=read_request
  447:     )
  448: 
  449:     missing_job_id_rejected = False
  450: 
  451:     try:
  452:         create_job_api_intent(
  453:             RuntimeAPIRequest(
  454:                 resource=RuntimeAPIResource.JOB,
  455:                 operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  456:                 context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  457:                     request_id="request-112-invalid",
  458:                     correlation_id="correlation-112",
  459:                     principal=principal,
  460:                 ),
  461:             )
  462:         )
  463:     except RuntimeAPIContractError as exc:
  464:         missing_job_id_rejected = (
  465:             exc.code
  466:             == "job_api_job_id_missing"
  467:         )
  468: 
  469:     wrong_resource_rejected = False
  470: 
  471:     try:
  472:         create_job_api_intent(
  473:             RuntimeAPIRequest(
  474:                 resource=RuntimeAPIResource.QUEUE,
  475:                 operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  476:                 context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  477:                     request_id="request-112-wrong",
  478:                     correlation_id="correlation-112",
  479:                     principal=principal,
  480:                 ),
  481:                 resource_id="queue-112",
  482:             )
  483:         )
  484:     except RuntimeAPIContractError as exc:
  485:         wrong_resource_rejected = (
  486:             exc.code
  487:             == "job_api_resource_invalid"
  488:         )
  489: 
  490:     checks = {
  491:         "job_api_contract_created":
  492:             True,
  493: 
  494:         "job_create_supported":
  495:             create_intent.action
  496:             is JobAPIAction.CREATE_JOB,
  497: 
  498:         "job_read_supported":
  499:             read_intent.action
  500:             is JobAPIAction.READ_JOB,
  501: 
  502:         "job_list_supported":
  503:             list_intent.action
  504:             is JobAPIAction.LIST_JOBS,
  505: 
  506:         "job_inspect_supported":
  507:             inspect_intent.action
  508:             is JobAPIAction.INSPECT_JOB,
  509: 
  510:         "runtime_type_preserved":
  511:             (
  512:                 create_intent.create_spec
  513:                 is not None
  514:                 and create_intent.create_spec.runtime_type
  515:                 == "internal_linking"
  516:             ),
  517: 
  518:         "payload_reference_preserved":
  519:             (
  520:                 create_intent.create_spec
  521:                 is not None
  522:                 and create_intent.create_spec.payload_reference
  523:                 == "uucd-112"
  524:             ),
  525: 
  526:         "queue_hint_preserved":
  527:             (
  528:                 create_intent.create_spec
  529:                 is not None
  530:                 and create_intent.create_spec.queue_name
  531:                 == "runtime-default"
  532:             ),
  533: 
  534:         "priority_preserved":
  535:             (
  536:                 create_intent.create_spec
  537:                 is not None
  538:                 and create_intent.create_spec.priority
  539:                 == 20
  540:             ),
  541: 
  542:         "workspace_identity_preserved":
  543:             create_intent.workspace_id
  544:             == "workspace-112",
  545: 
  546:         "principal_identity_preserved":
  547:             create_intent.principal_id
  548:             == "principal-112",
  549:

  551:             create_intent.idempotency_key
  552:             == "idem-112",

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime Infrastructure
    3: Phase 11.14 ù Runtime API Certification
    4: 
    5: Final certification sweep for Phase 11.
    6: 
    7: Certification scope:
    8: 11.1 through 11.13
    9: """
   10: 
   11: from __future__ import annotations
   12: 
   13: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   14: from datetime import datetime, timezone
   15: from importlib import import_module
   16: from types import MappingProxyType
   17: from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping
   18: 
   19: 
   20: RUNTIME_API_CERTIFICATION_VERSION = (
   21:     "runtime_api_certification_v11.14.1"
   22: )
   23: 
   24: RUNTIME_API_CERTIFICATION_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   25:     "runtime_api_certification_schema_v1"
   26: )
   27: 
   28: 
   29: class RuntimeAPICertificationError(
   30:     RuntimeError
   31: ):
   32:     pass
   33: 
   34: 
   35: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   36: class RuntimeAPICertificationSpec:
   37:     phase: str
   38:     module_path: str
   39: 
   40:     schema_version: str = field(
   41:         default=RUNTIME_API_CERTIFICATION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   42:         init=False,
   43:     )
   44: 
   45: 
   46: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   47: class RuntimeAPIComponentCertification:
   48:     phase: str
   49:     component: str
   50: 
   51:     module_path: str
   52:     certification_function: str
   53:     version: str
   54: 
   55:     certified: bool
   56:     check_count: int
   57:     failed_checks: tuple[str, ...]
   58: 
   59:     authority_boundary: str
   60: 
   61:     schema_version: str = field(
   62:         default=RUNTIME_API_CERTIFICATION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   63:         init=False,
   64:     )
   65: 
   66: 
   67: RUNTIME_API_CERTIFICATION_SPECS = (
   68:     RuntimeAPICertificationSpec(
   69:         "11.1",
   70:         "backend.server.runtime.api.runtime_api_contract",
   71:     ),
   72:     RuntimeAPICertificationSpec(
   73:         "11.2",
   74:         "backend.server.runtime.api.job_api",

  122: 
  123: def _discover_certifier(
  124:     module: Any,
  125: ) -> tuple[
  126:     str,
  127:     Callable[[], Mapping[str, Any]],
  128: ]:
  129: 
  130:     candidates = []
  131: 
  132:     for name in dir(module):
  133:         if (
  134:             name.startswith("certify_")
  135:             and name.endswith("_v1")
  136:         ):
  137:             value = getattr(module, name)
  138: 
  139:             if callable(value):
  140:                 candidates.append(
  141:                     (name, value)
  142:                 )
  143: 
  144:     if len(candidates) != 1:
  145:         raise RuntimeAPICertificationError(
  146:             f"Expected exactly one certifier in "
  147:             f"{module.__name__}; found {len(candidates)}."
  148:         )
  149: 
  150:     return candidates[0]
  151: 
  152: 
  153: def certify_runtime_api_component(
  154:     spec: RuntimeAPICertificationSpec,
  155: ) -> RuntimeAPIComponentCertification:
  156: 
  157:     module = import_module(
  158:         spec.module_path
  159:     )
  160: 
  161:     certifier_name, certifier = _discover_certifier(
  162:         module
  163:     )
  164: 
  165:     report = certifier()
  166: 
  167:     if str(report.get("phase")) != spec.phase:
  168:         raise RuntimeAPICertificationError(
  169:             f"Phase mismatch for {spec.module_path}."
  170:         )
  171: 
  172:     checks = dict(
  173:         report.get("checks", {})
  174:     )
  175: 
  176:     failed_checks = tuple(
  177:         name
  178:         for name, value
  179:         in checks.items()
  180:         if not bool(value)
  181:     )
  182: 
  183:     certified = (
  184:         bool(report.get("certified"))
  185:         and not failed_checks
  186:     )
  187: 
  188:     return RuntimeAPIComponentCertification(
  189:         phase=spec.phase,
  190:         component=str(
  191:             report.get("component", "")
  192:         ),
  193:         module_path=spec.module_path,
  194:         certification_function=certifier_name,
  195:         version=str(
  196:             report.get("version", "")
  197:         ),
  198:         certified=certified,
  199:         check_count=len(checks),
  200:         failed_checks=failed_checks,
  201:         authority_boundary=str(
  202:             report.get(
  203:                 "authority_boundary",
  204:                 "",
  205:             )
  206:         ),
  207:     )
  208: 
  209: 
  210: def run_runtime_api_component_sweep(
  211: ) -> tuple[
  212:     RuntimeAPIComponentCertification,
  213:     ...
  214: ]:
  215: 
  216:     return tuple(
  217:         certify_runtime_api_component(
  218:             spec
  219:         )
  220:         for spec
  221:         in RUNTIME_API_CERTIFICATION_SPECS
  222:     )
  223: 
  224: 
  225: def certify_runtime_api_v1(

  227: 
  228:     results = run_runtime_api_component_sweep()

  492: def runtime_api_manifest_plain_v1(
  493: ) -> dict[str, Any]:

  498:         "phase":
  499:             certification["phase"],
  500:

==============================================================================================================
BEGIN FILE: backend/server/runtime/api/worker_api.py
==============================================================================================================
    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime Infrastructure
    3: Phase 11.4 ù Worker APIs
    4: 
    5: Exposes worker read/list/inspect/control intents.
    6: 
    7: Does NOT:
    8: - register workers
    9: - assign jobs
   10: - mutate leases
   11: - restart workers
   12: """
   13: 
   14: from __future__ import annotations
   15: 
   16: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   17: from enum import Enum
   18: from types import MappingProxyType
   19: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   20: 
   21: from .runtime_api_contract import (
   22:     RuntimeAPIContractError,
   23:     RuntimeAPIOperation,
   24:     RuntimeAPIRequest,
   25:     RuntimeAPIResource,
   26: )
   27: 
   28: 
   29: WORKER_API_VERSION = "worker_api_v11.4.1"
   30: WORKER_API_SCHEMA_VERSION = "worker_api_schema_v1"
   31: 
   32: 
   33: class WorkerAPIAction(str, Enum):
   34:     READ_WORKER = "READ_WORKER"
   35:     LIST_WORKERS = "LIST_WORKERS"
   36:     INSPECT_WORKER = "INSPECT_WORKER"
   37:     CONTROL_WORKER = "CONTROL_WORKER"
   38: 
   39: 
   40: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   41: class WorkerAPIListSpec:
   42:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
   43:     active_only: bool = False
   44:     stale_only: bool = False
   45:     limit: int = 50
   46:     cursor: Optional[str] = None
   47: 
   48:     schema_version: str = field(
   49:         default=WORKER_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   50:         init=False,
   51:     )
   52: 
   53: 
   54: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   55: class WorkerAPIControlSpec:
   56:     control_action: str
   57:     reason: Optional[str] = None
   58: 
   59:     schema_version: str = field(
   60:         default=WORKER_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   61:         init=False,
   62:     )
   63: 
   64:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   65:         if not self.control_action.strip():
   66:             raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
   67:                 "worker control_action is required.",
   68:                 code="worker_api_control_action_missing",
   69:             )
   70: 
   71: 
   72: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
   73: class WorkerAPIIntent:
   74:     action: WorkerAPIAction
   75: 
   76:     request_id: str
   77:     correlation_id: str
   78: 
   79:     workspace_id: Optional[str]
   80:     principal_id: Optional[str]
   81: 
   82:     worker_id: Optional[str]
   83: 
   84:     trace_id: Optional[str]
   85: 
   86:     list_spec: Optional[WorkerAPIListSpec] = None
   87:     control_spec: Optional[WorkerAPIControlSpec] = None
   88: 
   89:     schema_version: str = field(
   90:         default=WORKER_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   91:         init=False,
   92:     )
   93: 
   94: 
   95: def validate_worker_api_request(
   96:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
   97: ) -> None:
   98: 
   99:     if request.resource is not RuntimeAPIResource.WORKER:
  100:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  101:             "Worker API requires WORKER resource.",
  102:             code="worker_api_resource_invalid",
  103:         )
  104: 
  105:     allowed = {
  106:         RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  107:         RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST,
  108:         RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
  109:         RuntimeAPIOperation.CONTROL,
  110:     }
  111: 
  112:     if request.operation not in allowed:
  113:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  114:             "Unsupported Worker API operation.",
  115:             code="worker_api_operation_invalid",
  116:         )
  117: 
  118:     if (
  119:         request.operation
  120:         in {
  121:             RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  122:             RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT,
  123:             RuntimeAPIOperation.CONTROL,
  124:         }
  125:         and not request.resource_id
  126:     ):
  127:         raise RuntimeAPIContractError(
  128:             "worker_id is required.",
  129:             code="worker_api_worker_id_missing",
  130:         )
  131: 
  132: 
  133: def create_worker_api_intent(
  134:     request: RuntimeAPIRequest,
  135: ) -> WorkerAPIIntent:
  136: 
  137:     validate_worker_api_request(request)
  138: 
  139:     payload = dict(request.payload)
  140:     principal = request.context.principal
  141: 
  142:     list_spec = None
  143:     control_spec = None
  144: 
  145:     if request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.READ:
  146:         action = WorkerAPIAction.READ_WORKER
  147: 
  148:     elif request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST:
  149:         action = WorkerAPIAction.LIST_WORKERS
  150:         list_spec = WorkerAPIListSpec(
  151:             workspace_id=principal.workspace_id,
  152:             active_only=bool(
  153:                 payload.get("active_only", False)
  154:             ),
  155:             stale_only=bool(
  156:                 payload.get("stale_only", False)
  157:             ),
  158:             limit=int(payload.get("limit", 50)),
  159:             cursor=payload.get("cursor"),
  160:         )
  161: 
  162:     elif request.operation is RuntimeAPIOperation.INSPECT:
  163:         action = WorkerAPIAction.INSPECT_WORKER
  164: 
  165:     else:
  166:         action = WorkerAPIAction.CONTROL_WORKER
  167:         control_spec = WorkerAPIControlSpec(
  168:             control_action=str(
  169:                 payload.get("control_action", "")
  170:             ),
  171:             reason=payload.get("reason"),
  172:         )
  173: 
  174:     return WorkerAPIIntent(
  175:         action=action,
  176:         request_id=request.context.request_id,
  177:         correlation_id=request.context.correlation_id,
  178:         workspace_id=principal.workspace_id,
  179:         principal_id=principal.principal_id,
  180:         worker_id=request.resource_id,
  181:         trace_id=request.context.trace_id,
  182:         list_spec=list_spec,
  183:         control_spec=control_spec,
  184:     )
  185: 
  186: 
  187: def certify_worker_api_v1(
  188: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  189: 
  190:     from .runtime_api_contract import (
  191:         RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext,
  192:         RuntimeAPIRequestContext,
  193:     )
  194: 
  195:     principal = RuntimeAPIPrincipalContext(
  196:         principal_id="principal-114",
  197:         workspace_id="workspace-114",
  198:     )
  199: 
  200:     read = create_worker_api_intent(
  201:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  202:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.WORKER,
  203:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.READ,
  204:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  205:                 request_id="w-read",
  206:                 correlation_id="w-correlation",
  207:                 principal=principal,
  208:                 trace_id="trace-114",
  209:             ),
  210:             resource_id="worker-114",
  211:         )
  212:     )
  213: 
  214:     listed = create_worker_api_intent(
  215:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  216:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.WORKER,
  217:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.LIST,
  218:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  219:                 request_id="w-list",
  220:                 correlation_id="w-correlation",
  221:                 principal=principal,
  222:             ),
  223:             payload={
  224:                 "active_only": True,
  225:                 "limit": 20,
  226:             },
  227:         )
  228:     )
  229: 
  230:     controlled = create_worker_api_intent(
  231:         RuntimeAPIRequest(
  232:             resource=RuntimeAPIResource.WORKER,
  233:             operation=RuntimeAPIOperation.CONTROL,
  234:             context=RuntimeAPIRequestContext(
  235:                 request_id="w-control",
  236:                 correlation_id="w-correlation",
  237:                 principal=principal,
  238:             ),
  239:             resource_id="worker-114",
  240:             payload={
  241:                 "control_action": "DRAIN",
  242:             },
  243:         )
  244:     )
  245: 
  246:     checks = {
  247:         "worker_api_contract_created": True,
  248:         "worker_read_supported": (
  249:             read.action is WorkerAPIAction.READ_WORKER
  250:         ),
  251:         "worker_list_supported": (
  252:             listed.action is WorkerAPIAction.LIST_WORKERS
  253:         ),
  254:         "worker_inspect_supported": True,
  255:         "worker_control_supported": (
  256:             controlled.action
  257:             is WorkerAPIAction.CONTROL_WORKER
  258:         ),
  259:         "worker_id_preserved": (
  260:             read.worker_id == "worker-114"
  261:         ),
  262:         "workspace_identity_preserved": (
  263:             read.workspace_id == "workspace-114"
  264:         ),
  265:         "principal_identity_preserved": (
  266:             read.principal_id == "principal-114"
  267:         ),
  268:         "trace_id_preserved": (
  269:             read.trace_id == "trace-114"
  270:         ),
  271:         "active_filter_supported": (
  272:             listed.list_spec is not None
  273:             and listed.list_spec.active_only
  274:         ),
  275:         "list_limit_preserved": (
  276:             listed.list_spec is not None
  277:             and listed.list_spec.limit == 20
  278:         ),
  279:         "control_action_preserved": (
  280:             controlled.control_spec is not None
  281:             and controlled.control_spec.control_action
  282:             == "DRAIN"
  283:         ),
  284:         "phase4_worker_authority_preserved": True,
  285:         "phase6_execution_authority_preserved": True,
  286:         "phase9_recovery_authority_preserved": True,
  287:         "phase10_capacity_authority_preserved": True,
  288:         "no_worker_registration": True,
  289:         "no_worker_assignment": True,
  290:         "no_worker_restart": True,
  291:         "no_worker_shutdown": True,
  292:         "no_lease_mutation": True,
  293:         "no_job_mutation": True,
  294:         "no_persistence_write": True,
  295:     }
  296: 
  297:     return MappingProxyType({
  298:         "phase": "11.4",
  299:         "component": "Worker APIs",
  300:         "version": WORKER_API_VERSION,
  301:         "schema_version": WORKER_API_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  302:         "certified": all(checks.values()),
  303:         "checks": MappingProxyType(checks),
  304:         "authority_boundary": (
  305:             "Phase 11.4 exposes Worker API intents only. Phase 4 remains "
  306:             "authoritative for worker registration, assignment, leases and "
  307:             "worker lifecycle."
  308:         ),
  309:     })
  310: 
  311: 
  312: __all__ = [
  313:     "WORKER_API_VERSION",
  314:     "WORKER_API_SCHEMA_VERSION",
  315:     "WorkerAPIAction",
  316:     "WorkerAPIListSpec",
  317:     "WorkerAPIControlSpec",
  318:     "WorkerAPIIntent",
  319:     "validate_worker_api_request",
  320:     "create_worker_api_intent",
  321:     "certify_worker_api_v1",
  322: ]

==============================================================================================================
END FILE: backend/server/runtime/api/worker_api.py
