    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.13 ù Cancellation / Termination Execution
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution-layer cancellation coordination.
    6: 
    7: Consumes:
    8: - existing cancellation decision evidence
    9: - Phase 6.2 ExecutionRequest
   10: - Phase 6.3 ExecutionFenceIdentity
   11: - Phase 6.4 active execution lifecycle
   12: - existing worker/runtime cancellation mechanics
   13: - existing lease/worker quiescence evidence
   14: 
   15: Owns:
   16: - consume cancellation decision
   17: - stop-new-work enforcement coordination
   18: - active-work cancellation coordination
   19: - worker / lease quiescence enforcement
   20: - execution CANCELLED-state application
   21: - cancellation result capture
   22: 
   23: Does NOT:
   24: - invent cancellation decisions
   25: - create a second worker cancellation system
   26: - kill OS processes directly
   27: - create a second lease system
   28: - release/acquire leases directly
   29: - mutate production UniversalJob directly
   30: - mutate queues directly
   31: - mutate Phase-5 orchestration state
   32: - persist cancellation state
   33: 
   34: Concrete runtime/worker/lease integration is deferred to Phase 6.15.
   35: """
   36: 
   37: from __future__ import annotations
   38: 
   39: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   40: from types import MappingProxyType
   41: from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping, Optional
   42: 
   43: from .execution_contracts import (
   44:     ExecutionOutcome,
   45:     ExecutionRequest,
   46:     ExecutionResult,
   47: )
   48: 
   49: from .execution_permission_fencing import (
   50:     ExecutionFenceIdentity,
   51: )
   52: 
   53: from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
   54:     ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
   55:     ExecutionLifecycleState,
   56:     finalize_execution,
   57: )
   58: 
   59: 
   60: CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_VERSION = (
   61:     "cancellation_termination_execution_v6.13.1"
   62: )
   63: 
   64: CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   65:     "cancellation_termination_execution_schema_v1"
   66: )
   67: 
   68: 
   69: class CancellationTerminationExecutionError(ValueError):
   70:     """Raised when cancellation execution is invalid."""
   71: 
   72:     def __init__(
   73:         self,
   74:         message: str,
   75:         *,
   76:         code: str,
   77:         value: Any = None,
   78:     ) -> None:
   79:         super().__init__(message)
   80:         self.code = code
   81:         self.value = value
   82: 
   83: 
   84: @dataclass(
   85:     frozen=True,
   86:     slots=True,
   87: )
   88: class CancellationDecisionEvidence:
   89:     """
   90:     Existing cancellation decision consumed by Phase 6.13.
   91:     """
   92: 
   93:     authorized: bool
   94: 
   95:     decision_id: Optional[str] = None
   96:     reason: Optional[str] = None
   97: 
   98:     force_termination: bool = False
   99: 
  100:     schema_version: str = field(
  101:         default=CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  102:         init=False,
  103:     )
  104: 
  105: 
  106: @dataclass(
  107:     frozen=True,
  108:     slots=True,
  109: )
  110: class StopNewWorkResult:
  111:     enforced: bool
  112: 
  113:     runtime_reference: Optional[str] = None
  114:     error_code: Optional[str] = None
  115: 
  116:     schema_version: str = field(
  117:         default=CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  118:         init=False,
  119:     )
  120: 
  121: 
  122: @dataclass(
  123:     frozen=True,
  124:     slots=True,
  125: )
  126: class ActiveWorkCancellationResult:
  127:     cancellation_requested: bool
  128:     active_work_stopped: bool
  129: 
  130:     worker_reference: Optional[str] = None
  131:     runtime_reference: Optional[str] = None
  132: 
  133:     error_code: Optional[str] = None
  134: 
  135:     schema_version: str = field(
  136:         default=CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  137:         init=False,
  138:     )
  139: 
  140: 
  141: @dataclass(
  142:     frozen=True,
  143:     slots=True,
  144: )
  145: class WorkerLeaseQuiescenceEvidence:
  146:     """
  147:     Existing runtime/worker/lease quiescence evidence.
  148: 
  149:     Phase 6.13 consumes it; it does not own lease mutation.
  150:     """
  151: 
  152:     worker_quiesced: bool
  153:     lease_quiesced: bool
  154:     active_operation_count: int = 0
  155: 
  156:     worker_reference: Optional[str] = None
  157:     lease_reference: Optional[str] = None
  158: 
  159:     schema_version: str = field(
  160:         default=CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  161:         init=False,
  162:     )
  163: 
  164:     @property
  165:     def quiescent(self) -> bool:
  166:         return (
  167:             self.worker_quiesced
  168:             and self.lease_quiesced
  169:             and self.active_operation_count == 0
  170:         )
  171: 
  172: 
  173: @dataclass(
  174:     frozen=True,
  175:     slots=True,
  176: )
  177: class CancellationExecutionResult:
  178:     execution_id: str
  179:     job_id: str
  180:     attempt_number: int
  181:     fence_id: str
  182: 
  183:     cancelled: bool
  184:     force_termination: bool
  185: 
  186:     lifecycle_state: ExecutionLifecycleState
  187: 
  188:     cancellation_decision_id: Optional[str]
  189:     cancellation_reason: Optional[str]
  190: 
  191:     runtime_reference: Optional[str]
  192:     worker_reference: Optional[str]
  193:     lease_reference: Optional[str]
  194: 
  195:     error_code: Optional[str] = None
  196: 
  197:     schema_version: str = field(
  198:         default=CANCELLATION_TERMINATION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  199:         init=False,
  200:     )
  201: 
  202:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  203:         return {
  204:             "schema_version":
  205:                 self.schema_version,
  206:             "execution_id":
  207:                 self.execution_id,
  208:             "job_id":
  209:                 self.job_id,
  210:             "attempt_number":
  211:                 self.attempt_number,
  212:             "fence_id":
  213:                 self.fence_id,
  214:             "cancelled":
  215:                 self.cancelled,
  216:             "force_termination":
  217:                 self.force_termination,
  218:             "lifecycle_state":
  219:                 self.lifecycle_state.value,
  220:             "cancellation_decision_id":
  221:                 self.cancellation_decision_id,
  222:             "cancellation_reason":
  223:                 self.cancellation_reason,
  224:             "runtime_reference":
  225:                 self.runtime_reference,
  226:             "worker_reference":
  227:                 self.worker_reference,
  228:             "lease_reference":
  229:                 self.lease_reference,
  230:             "error_code":
  231:                 self.error_code,
  232:         }

  234: 
  235: def consume_cancellation_decision(
  236:     *,
  237:     decision: CancellationDecisionEvidence,
  238: ) -> None:
  239:     """
  240:     Consume an already-made cancellation decision.
  241:     """
  242: 
  243:     if not isinstance(
  244:         decision,
  245:         CancellationDecisionEvidence,
  246:     ):
  247:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  248:             "decision must be CancellationDecisionEvidence.",
  249:             code="invalid_cancellation_decision",
  250:             value=decision,
  251:         )
  252: 
  253:     if not decision.authorized:
  254:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  255:             "Cancellation was not authorized.",
  256:             code="cancellation_not_authorized",
  257:             value=decision.reason,
  258:         )
  259: 
  260: 
  261: def validate_cancellation_boundary(
  262:     *,
  263:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  264:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  265:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  266: ) -> None:
  267:     """
  268:     Cancellation may only operate on the matching non-terminal execution.
  269:     """
  270: 
  271:     if not isinstance(
  272:         request,
  273:         ExecutionRequest,
  274:     ):
  275:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  276:             "request must be ExecutionRequest.",
  277:             code="invalid_execution_request",
  278:             value=request,
  279:         )
  280: 
  281:     if not isinstance(
  282:         lifecycle,
  283:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  284:     ):
  285:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  286:             "lifecycle must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  287:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle",
  288:             value=lifecycle,
  289:         )
  290: 
  291:     if not isinstance(
  292:         fence,
  293:         ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  294:     ):
  295:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  296:             "fence must be ExecutionFenceIdentity.",
  297:             code="invalid_execution_fence",
  298:             value=fence,
  299:         )
  300: 
  301:     if lifecycle.state in {
  302:         ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED,
  303:         ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED,
  304:         ExecutionLifecycleState.CANCELLED,
  305:     }:
  306:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  307:             "Terminal execution cannot be cancelled again.",
  308:             code="cancellation_terminal_reentry",
  309:             value=lifecycle.state.value,
  310:         )
  311: 
  312:     if (
  313:         request.identity.execution_id
  314:         != lifecycle.execution_id
  315:         or request.identity.execution_id
  316:         != fence.execution_id
  317:     ):
  318:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  319:             "Cancellation execution identity mismatch.",
  320:             code="cancellation_execution_identity_mismatch",
  321:         )
  322: 
  323:     if (
  324:         request.identity.job_id
  325:         != lifecycle.job_id
  326:         or request.identity.job_id
  327:         != fence.job_id
  328:     ):
  329:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  330:             "Cancellation job identity mismatch.",
  331:             code="cancellation_job_identity_mismatch",
  332:         )
  333: 
  334:     if (
  335:         request.identity.attempt_number
  336:         != lifecycle.attempt_number
  337:         or request.identity.attempt_number
  338:         != fence.attempt_number
  339:     ):
  340:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  341:             "Cancellation attempt identity mismatch.",
  342:             code="cancellation_attempt_identity_mismatch",
  343:         )
  344: 
  345:     if lifecycle.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  346:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  347:             "Cancellation fence mismatch.",
  348:             code="cancellation_fence_mismatch",
  349:         )
  350: 
  351: 
  352: def enforce_stop_new_work(
  353:     *,
  354:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  355:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  356:     stopper: Callable[
  357:         [
  358:             Mapping[str, Any],
  359:         ],
  360:         Any,
  361:     ],
  362: ) -> StopNewWorkResult:
  363:     """
  364:     Coordinate stop-new-work through existing runtime/worker machinery.
  365:     """
  366: 
  367:     if not callable(stopper):
  368:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  369:             "stopper must be callable.",
  370:             code="invalid_stop_new_work_adapter",
  371:             value=stopper,
  372:         )
  373: 
  374:     metadata = MappingProxyType(
  375:         {
  376:             "execution_id":
  377:                 request.identity.execution_id,
  378:             "job_id":
  379:                 request.identity.job_id,
  380:             "attempt_number":
  381:                 request.identity.attempt_number,
  382:             "fence_id":
  383:                 fence.fence_id,
  384:         }
  385:     )
  386: 
  387:     try:
  388:         result = stopper(
  389:             metadata
  390:         )
  391:     except Exception:
  392:         return StopNewWorkResult(
  393:             enforced=False,
  394:             error_code="stop_new_work_failed",
  395:         )
  396: 
  397:     if isinstance(
  398:         result,
  399:         StopNewWorkResult,
  400:     ):
  401:         return result
  402: 
  403:     if result is True:
  404:         return StopNewWorkResult(
  405:             enforced=True,
  406:         )
  407: 
  408:     if isinstance(result, Mapping):
  409:         return StopNewWorkResult(
  410:             enforced=bool(
  411:                 result.get(
  412:                     "enforced",
  413:                     False,
  414:                 )
  415:             ),
  416:             runtime_reference=(
  417:                 result.get(
  418:                     "runtime_reference"
  419:                 )
  420:             ),
  421:             error_code=(
  422:                 result.get(
  423:                     "error_code"
  424:                 )
  425:             ),
  426:         )
  427: 
  428:     return StopNewWorkResult(
  429:         enforced=False,
  430:         error_code="invalid_stop_new_work_result",
  431:     )
  432: 
  433: 
  434: def coordinate_active_work_cancellation(
  435:     *,
  436:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  437:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  438:     force_termination: bool,
  439:     cancellation_adapter: Callable[
  440:         [
  441:             Mapping[str, Any],
  442:         ],
  443:         Any,
  444:     ],
  445: ) -> ActiveWorkCancellationResult:
  446:     """
  447:     Coordinate active-work cancellation through existing runtime/worker
  448:     cancellation machinery.
  449:     """
  450: 
  451:     if not callable(
  452:         cancellation_adapter
  453:     ):
  454:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  455:             "cancellation_adapter must be callable.",
  456:             code="invalid_active_work_cancellation_adapter",
  457:             value=cancellation_adapter,
  458:         )
  459: 
  460:     metadata = MappingProxyType(
  461:         {
  462:             "execution_id":
  463:                 request.identity.execution_id,
  464:             "job_id":
  465:                 request.identity.job_id,
  466:             "attempt_number":
  467:                 request.identity.attempt_number,
  468:             "fence_id":
  469:                 fence.fence_id,
  470:             "force_termination":
  471:                 force_termination,
  472:         }
  473:     )
  474: 
  475:     try:
  476:         result = cancellation_adapter(
  477:             metadata
  478:         )
  479:     except Exception:
  480:         return ActiveWorkCancellationResult(
  481:             cancellation_requested=False,
  482:             active_work_stopped=False,
  483:             error_code="active_work_cancellation_failed",
  484:         )
  485: 
  486:     if isinstance(
  487:         result,
  488:         ActiveWorkCancellationResult,
  489:     ):
  490:         return result
  491: 
  492:     if result is True:
  493:         return ActiveWorkCancellationResult(
  494:             cancellation_requested=True,
  495:             active_work_stopped=True,
  496:         )
  497: 
  498:     if isinstance(result, Mapping):
  499:         return ActiveWorkCancellationResult(
  500:             cancellation_requested=bool(
  501:                 result.get(
  502:                     "cancellation_requested",
  503:                     False,
  504:                 )
  505:             ),
  506:             active_work_stopped=bool(
  507:                 result.get(
  508:                     "active_work_stopped",
  509:                     False,
  510:                 )
  511:             ),
  512:             worker_reference=(
  513:                 result.get(
  514:                     "worker_reference"
  515:                 )
  516:             ),
  517:             runtime_reference=(
  518:                 result.get(
  519:                     "runtime_reference"
  520:                 )
  521:             ),
  522:             error_code=(
  523:                 result.get(
  524:                     "error_code"
  525:                 )
  526:             ),
  527:         )
  528: 
  529:     return ActiveWorkCancellationResult(
  530:         cancellation_requested=False,
  531:         active_work_stopped=False,
  532:         error_code="invalid_active_work_cancellation_result",
  533:     )
  534: 
  535: 
  536: def validate_worker_lease_quiescence(
  537:     *,
  538:     evidence: WorkerLeaseQuiescenceEvidence,
  539: ) -> None:
  540:     """
  541:     Require worker and lease quiescence before CANCELLED state.
  542:     """
  543: 
  544:     if not isinstance(
  545:         evidence,
  546:         WorkerLeaseQuiescenceEvidence,
  547:     ):
  548:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  549:             "evidence must be WorkerLeaseQuiescenceEvidence.",
  550:             code="invalid_worker_lease_quiescence_evidence",
  551:             value=evidence,
  552:         )
  553: 
  554:     if not evidence.quiescent:
  555:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  556:             "Worker / lease boundary is not quiescent.",
  557:             code="worker_lease_not_quiescent",
  558:             value={
  559:                 "worker_quiesced":
  560:                     evidence.worker_quiesced,
  561:                 "lease_quiesced":
  562:                     evidence.lease_quiesced,
  563:                 "active_operation_count":
  564:                     evidence.active_operation_count,
  565:             },
  566:         )
  567: 
  568: 
  569: def apply_cancelled_state(
  570:     *,
  571:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  572:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  573:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  574:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  575: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  576:     """
  577:     Apply CANCELLED through the canonical Phase-6.4 finalization path.
  578:     """
  579: 
  580:     result = ExecutionResult(
  581:         identity=request.identity,
  582:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.CANCELLED,
  583:         result_metadata={
  584:             "fence_id":
  585:                 fence.fence_id,
  586:             "cancellation":
  587:                 True,
  588:         },
  589:     )
  590: 
  591:     cancelled = finalize_execution(
  592:         record=lifecycle,
  593:         result=result,
  594:         fence=fence,
  595:         finalized_at=finalized_at,
  596:     )
  597: 
  598:     if (
  599:         cancelled.state
  600:         is not ExecutionLifecycleState.CANCELLED
  601:     ):
  602:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  603:             "Execution did not transition to CANCELLED.",
  604:             code="cancelled_state_application_failed",
  605:         )
  606: 
  607:     return cancelled
  608: 
  609: 
  610: def execute_cancellation(
  611:     *,
  612:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  613:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  614:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  615:     decision: CancellationDecisionEvidence,
  616:     stopper: Callable[
  617:         [
  618:             Mapping[str, Any],
  619:         ],
  620:         Any,
  621:     ],
  622:     cancellation_adapter: Callable[
  623:         [
  624:             Mapping[str, Any],
  625:         ],
  626:         Any,
  627:     ],
  628:     quiescence: WorkerLeaseQuiescenceEvidence,
  629:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  630: ) -> tuple[
  631:     ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  632:     CancellationExecutionResult,
  633: ]:
  634:     """
  635:     Canonical Phase-6.13 cancellation flow.
  636: 
  637:     decision
  638:       -> validate boundary
  639:       -> stop new work
  640:       -> cancel active work
  641:       -> require worker/lease quiescence
  642:       -> apply CANCELLED lifecycle
  643:       -> capture cancellation evidence
  644:     """
  645: 
  646:     consume_cancellation_decision(
  647:         decision=decision,
  648:     )
  649: 
  650:     validate_cancellation_boundary(
  651:         request=request,
  652:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  653:         fence=fence,
  654:     )
  655: 
  656:     stop_result = enforce_stop_new_work(
  657:         request=request,
  658:         fence=fence,
  659:         stopper=stopper,
  660:     )
  661: 
  662:     if not stop_result.enforced:
  663:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  664:             "Stop-new-work enforcement failed.",
  665:             code=(
  666:                 stop_result.error_code
  667:                 or "stop_new_work_failed"
  668:             ),
  669:         )
  670: 
  671:     cancellation_result = (
  672:         coordinate_active_work_cancellation(
  673:             request=request,
  674:             fence=fence,
  675:             force_termination=decision.force_termination,
  676:             cancellation_adapter=cancellation_adapter,
  677:         )
  678:     )
  679: 
  680:     if (
  681:         not cancellation_result.cancellation_requested
  682:         or not cancellation_result.active_work_stopped
  683:     ):
  684:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  685:             "Active work was not fully cancelled.",
  686:             code=(
  687:                 cancellation_result.error_code
  688:                 or "active_work_not_cancelled"
  689:             ),
  690:         )
  691: 
  692:     validate_worker_lease_quiescence(
  693:         evidence=quiescence,
  694:     )
  695: 
  696:     cancelled_lifecycle = apply_cancelled_state(
  697:         request=request,
  698:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  699:         fence=fence,
  700:         finalized_at=finalized_at,
  701:     )
  702: 
  703:     capture = CancellationExecutionResult(
  704:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  705:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  706:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  707:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  708:         cancelled=(
  709:             cancelled_lifecycle.state
  710:             is ExecutionLifecycleState.CANCELLED
  711:             and not cancelled_lifecycle.active
  712:         ),
  713:         force_termination=decision.force_termination,
  714:         lifecycle_state=cancelled_lifecycle.state,
  715:         cancellation_decision_id=decision.decision_id,
  716:         cancellation_reason=decision.reason,
  717:         runtime_reference=(
  718:             cancellation_result.runtime_reference
  719:             or stop_result.runtime_reference
  720:         ),
  721:         worker_reference=(
  722:             cancellation_result.worker_reference
  723:             or quiescence.worker_reference
  724:         ),
  725:         lease_reference=(
  726:             quiescence.lease_reference
  727:         ),
  728:         error_code=None,
  729:     )
  730: 
  731:     return cancelled_lifecycle, capture
  732: 
  733: 
  734: def certify_cancellation_termination_execution_v1(
  735: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  736:     """
  737:     Phase 6.13 certification.
  738:     """
  739: 
  740:     from .execution_contracts import (
  741:         ExecutionAction,
  742:         ExecutionContext,
  743:         ExecutionIdentity,
  744:     )
  745: 
  746:     from .execution_permission_fencing import (
  747:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  748:         evaluate_execution_permission,
  749:     )
  750: 
  751:     from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
  752:         apply_running_state,
  753:         create_execution_start,
  754:     )
  755: 
  756:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  757:         execution_id=(
  758:             "cccccccc-cccc-4ccc-8ccc-cccccccccccc"
  759:         ),
  760:         job_id="job-phase-6-13-certification",
  761:         attempt_number=1,
  762:     )
  763: 
  764:     start_request = ExecutionRequest(
  765:         identity=identity,
  766:         context=ExecutionContext(
  767:             handler_key="certification.handler",
  768:             worker_id="worker-certification",
  769:             lease_id="lease-certification",
  770:             lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  771:         ),
  772:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  773:     )
  774: 
  775:     cancel_request = ExecutionRequest(
  776:         identity=identity,
  777:         context=start_request.context,
  778:         action=ExecutionAction.CANCEL,
  779:     )
  780: 
  781:     permission = evaluate_execution_permission(
  782:         request=start_request,
  783:         evidence=ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  784:             job_status="RUNNING",
  785:             current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  786:             current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  787:             current_lease_owner=(
  788:                 "worker-certification::instance-1"
  789:             ),
  790:             lease_state="ACTIVE",
  791:         ),
  792:     )
  793: 
  794:     fence = permission.fence
  795: 
  796:     if fence is None:
  797:         raise CancellationTerminationExecutionError(
  798:             "Certification missing execution fence.",
  799:             code="certification_missing_fence",
  800:         )

  802:     ready = create_execution_start(
  803:         request=start_request,
  804:         permission=permission,
  805:     )
  806:

  808:         record=ready,
  809:         fence=fence,
  810:         started_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  811:     )

  814:         Mapping[str, Any]

  826:             dict(metadata)
  827:         )
  828: 
  829:         return {
  830:             "enforced":
  831:                 True,
  832:             "runtime_reference":
  833:                 "runtime://phase-6-13/certification",
  834:         }

  837:         metadata: Mapping[str, Any],

  839:

  841:             dict(metadata)
  842:         )
  843: 
  844:         return {
  845:             "cancellation_requested":
  846:                 True,
  847:             "active_work_stopped":
  848:                 True,
  849:             "worker_reference":
  850:                 "worker://phase-6-13/certification",

  853:         }
  854: 
  855:     quiescence = WorkerLeaseQuiescenceEvidence(
  856:         worker_quiesced=True,
  857:         lease_quiesced=True,

  885:     unauthorized_rejected = False
  886:

  898:         execute_cancellation(

  908:             quiescence=quiescence,
  909:         )
  910:     except CancellationTerminationExecutionError:
  911:         unauthorized_rejected = True
  912: 
  913:     stop_failure_rejected = False
  914: 
  915:     def failing_stopper(
  916:         metadata: Mapping[str, Any],

  918:         return {
  919:             "enforced":
  920:                 False,

  922:                 "synthetic_stop_failure",

  929:

  984:

 1020:             fence=fence,

 1024:             quiescence=quiescence,

 1026:     except CancellationTerminationExecutionError:
 1027:         terminal_reentry_rejected = True

 1029:     checks = {

 1065:                 and capture.job_id
 1066:                 == identity.job_id
 1067:             ),
 1068:

 1072:                 == "runtime://phase-6-13/certification"

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.8 ù Checkpoint Execution
    4: 
    5: Canonical checkpoint coordination layer.
    6: 
    7: Consumes:
    8: - Phase 6.2 execution identity/context
    9: - Phase 6.3 execution fence
   10: - Phase 6.4 execution lifecycle
   11: 
   12: Owns:
   13: - checkpoint save coordination
   14: - checkpoint reference validation
   15: - checkpoint restore coordination
   16: - resume-from-checkpoint preparation
   17: - invalid/missing checkpoint handling
   18: 
   19: Does NOT:
   20: - create a second checkpoint store
   21: - persist checkpoint bytes itself
   22: - replace Runtime State Store
   23: - mutate Universal Jobs
   24: - mutate orchestration state
   25: - acquire/release leases
   26: - enqueue/requeue jobs
   27: 
   28: Actual storage adapters are supplied by existing runtime persistence/state
   29: authorities and are wired into production later under Phase 6.15.
   30: """
   31: 
   32: from __future__ import annotations
   33: 
   34: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   35: from enum import Enum
   36: from hashlib import sha256
   37: from types import MappingProxyType
   38: from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping, Optional
   39: 
   40: from .execution_contracts import (
   41:     ExecutionRequest,
   42: )
   43: 
   44: from .execution_permission_fencing import (
   45:     ExecutionFenceIdentity,
   46: )
   47: 
   48: from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
   49:     ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
   50:     ExecutionLifecycleState,
   51: )
   52: 
   53: 
   54: CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_VERSION = (
   55:     "checkpoint_execution_v6.8.1"
   56: )
   57: 
   58: CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   59:     "checkpoint_execution_schema_v1"
   60: )
   61: 
   62: 
   63: class CheckpointExecutionError(ValueError):
   64:     """Raised when checkpoint coordination is invalid."""
   65: 
   66:     def __init__(
   67:         self,
   68:         message: str,
   69:         *,
   70:         code: str,
   71:         value: Any = None,
   72:     ) -> None:
   73:         super().__init__(message)
   74:         self.code = code
   75:         self.value = value
   76: 
   77: 
   78: class CheckpointStatus(str, Enum):
   79:     AVAILABLE = "AVAILABLE"
   80:     MISSING = "MISSING"
   81:     INVALID = "INVALID"
   82: 
   83: 
   84: @dataclass(
   85:     frozen=True,
   86:     slots=True,
   87: )
   88: class CheckpointReference:
   89:     """
   90:     Canonical Phase-6 checkpoint reference.
   91: 
   92:     This identifies checkpoint evidence but does not contain storage
   93:     implementation details.
   94:     """
   95: 
   96:     checkpoint_id: str
   97:     execution_id: str
   98:     job_id: str
   99:     attempt_number: int
  100:     fence_id: str
  101: 
  102:     storage_reference: str
  103:     integrity_digest: str
  104: 
  105:     schema_version: str = field(
  106:         default=CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  107:         init=False,
  108:     )
  109: 
  110:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  111:         return {
  112:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  113:             "checkpoint_id": self.checkpoint_id,
  114:             "execution_id": self.execution_id,
  115:             "job_id": self.job_id,
  116:             "attempt_number": self.attempt_number,
  117:             "fence_id": self.fence_id,
  118:             "storage_reference": self.storage_reference,
  119:             "integrity_digest": self.integrity_digest,
  120:         }
  121: 
  122: 
  123: @dataclass(
  124:     frozen=True,
  125:     slots=True,
  126: )
  127: class CheckpointSaveResult:
  128:     saved: bool
  129:     reference: Optional[CheckpointReference]
  130:     error_code: Optional[str] = None
  131: 
  132:     schema_version: str = field(
  133:         default=CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  134:         init=False,
  135:     )
  136: 
  137: 
  138: @dataclass(
  139:     frozen=True,
  140:     slots=True,
  141: )
  142: class CheckpointRestoreResult:
  143:     status: CheckpointStatus
  144:     reference: Optional[CheckpointReference]
  145:     payload: Any = None
  146:     error_code: Optional[str] = None
  147: 
  148:     schema_version: str = field(
  149:         default=CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  150:         init=False,
  151:     )
  152: 
  153:     @property
  154:     def restored(self) -> bool:
  155:         return (
  156:             self.status is CheckpointStatus.AVAILABLE
  157:             and self.reference is not None
  158:         )
  159: 
  160: 
  161: @dataclass(
  162:     frozen=True,
  163:     slots=True,
  164: )
  165: class ResumeCheckpointPreparation:
  166:     execution_id: str
  167:     job_id: str
  168:     attempt_number: int
  169:     fence_id: str
  170: 
  171:     checkpoint_reference: CheckpointReference
  172:     restored_payload: Any
  173: 
  174:     resume_metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
  175:         default_factory=dict,
  176:     )
  177: 
  178:     schema_version: str = field(
  179:         default=CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  180:         init=False,
  181:     )
  182: 
  183: 
  184: def _checkpoint_digest(
  185:     *,
  186:     execution_id: str,
  187:     job_id: str,
  188:     attempt_number: int,
  189:     fence_id: str,
  190:     storage_reference: str,
  191: ) -> str:
  192: 
  193:     material = "|".join(
  194:         (
  195:             execution_id,
  196:             job_id,
  197:             str(attempt_number),
  198:             fence_id,
  199:             storage_reference,
  200:         )
  201:     )
  202: 
  203:     return sha256(
  204:         material.encode("utf-8")
  205:     ).hexdigest()
  206: 
  207: 
  208: def validate_checkpoint_boundary(
  209:     *,
  210:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  211:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  212:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  213: ) -> None:
  214:     """
  215:     Checkpoint operations must remain bound to one execution identity/fence.
  216:     """
  217: 
  218:     if not isinstance(request, ExecutionRequest):
  219:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  220:             "request must be ExecutionRequest.",
  221:             code="invalid_execution_request",
  222:             value=request,
  223:         )
  224: 
  225:     if not isinstance(
  226:         lifecycle,
  227:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  228:     ):
  229:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  230:             "lifecycle must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  231:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle",
  232:             value=lifecycle,
  233:         )
  234: 
  235:     if not isinstance(fence, ExecutionFenceIdentity):
  236:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  237:             "fence must be ExecutionFenceIdentity.",
  238:             code="invalid_execution_fence",
  239:             value=fence,
  240:         )
  241: 
  242:     if (
  243:         request.identity.execution_id
  244:         != lifecycle.execution_id
  245:         or request.identity.execution_id
  246:         != fence.execution_id
  247:     ):
  248:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  249:             "Checkpoint execution identity mismatch.",
  250:             code="checkpoint_execution_identity_mismatch",
  251:         )
  252: 
  253:     if (
  254:         request.identity.job_id
  255:         != lifecycle.job_id
  256:         or request.identity.job_id
  257:         != fence.job_id
  258:     ):
  259:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  260:             "Checkpoint job identity mismatch.",
  261:             code="checkpoint_job_identity_mismatch",
  262:         )
  263: 
  264:     if (
  265:         request.identity.attempt_number
  266:         != lifecycle.attempt_number
  267:         or request.identity.attempt_number
  268:         != fence.attempt_number
  269:     ):
  270:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  271:             "Checkpoint attempt identity mismatch.",
  272:             code="checkpoint_attempt_identity_mismatch",
  273:         )
  274: 
  275:     if lifecycle.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  276:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  277:             "Checkpoint fence mismatch.",
  278:             code="checkpoint_fence_mismatch",
  279:         )
  280: 
  281: 
  282: def coordinate_checkpoint_save(
  283:     *,
  284:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  285:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  286:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  287:     checkpoint_payload: Any,
  288:     saver: Callable[[Mapping[str, Any], Any], str],
  289: ) -> CheckpointSaveResult:
  290:     """
  291:     Coordinate checkpoint save through an existing storage adapter.
  292: 
  293:     saver(metadata, payload) -> storage_reference
  294:     """
  295: 
  296:     validate_checkpoint_boundary(
  297:         request=request,
  298:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  299:         fence=fence,
  300:     )
  301: 
  302:     if lifecycle.state not in {
  303:         ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING,
  304:         ExecutionLifecycleState.SUSPENDED,
  305:     }:
  306:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  307:             "Checkpoint save requires RUNNING or SUSPENDED lifecycle.",
  308:             code="checkpoint_save_invalid_lifecycle",
  309:             value=lifecycle.state.value,
  310:         )
  311: 
  312:     if not callable(saver):
  313:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  314:             "saver must be callable.",
  315:             code="invalid_checkpoint_saver",
  316:             value=saver,
  317:         )
  318: 
  319:     metadata = MappingProxyType(
  320:         {
  321:             "execution_id":
  322:                 request.identity.execution_id,
  323:             "job_id":
  324:                 request.identity.job_id,
  325:             "attempt_number":
  326:                 request.identity.attempt_number,
  327:             "fence_id":
  328:                 fence.fence_id,
  329:         }
  330:     )
  331: 
  332:     try:
  333:         storage_reference = saver(
  334:             metadata,
  335:             checkpoint_payload,
  336:         )
  337:     except Exception:
  338:         return CheckpointSaveResult(
  339:             saved=False,
  340:             reference=None,
  341:             error_code="checkpoint_save_failed",
  342:         )
  343: 
  344:     if (
  345:         not isinstance(storage_reference, str)
  346:         or not storage_reference.strip()
  347:     ):
  348:         return CheckpointSaveResult(
  349:             saved=False,
  350:             reference=None,
  351:             error_code="invalid_checkpoint_storage_reference",
  352:         )
  353: 
  354:     storage_reference = storage_reference.strip()
  355: 
  356:     digest = _checkpoint_digest(
  357:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  358:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  359:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  360:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  361:         storage_reference=storage_reference,
  362:     )
  363: 
  364:     checkpoint_id = (
  365:         "checkpoint:"
  366:         + sha256(
  367:             (
  368:                 request.identity.execution_id
  369:                 + "|"
  370:                 + storage_reference
  371:             ).encode("utf-8")
  372:         ).hexdigest()
  373:     )
  374: 
  375:     reference = CheckpointReference(
  376:         checkpoint_id=checkpoint_id,
  377:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  378:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  379:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  380:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  381:         storage_reference=storage_reference,
  382:         integrity_digest=digest,
  383:     )
  384: 
  385:     return CheckpointSaveResult(
  386:         saved=True,
  387:         reference=reference,
  388:         error_code=None,
  389:     )
  390: 
  391: 
  392: def validate_checkpoint_reference(
  393:     *,
  394:     reference: CheckpointReference,
  395:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  396:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  397: ) -> bool:
  398:     """
  399:     Validate checkpoint identity, fence and digest.
  400:     """
  401: 
  402:     if not isinstance(
  403:         reference,
  404:         CheckpointReference,
  405:     ):
  406:         return False
  407: 
  408:     if (
  409:         reference.execution_id
  410:         != request.identity.execution_id
  411:     ):
  412:         return False
  413: 
  414:     if reference.job_id != request.identity.job_id:
  415:         return False
  416: 
  417:     if (
  418:         reference.attempt_number
  419:         != request.identity.attempt_number
  420:     ):
  421:         return False
  422: 
  423:     if reference.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  424:         return False
  425: 
  426:     expected_digest = _checkpoint_digest(
  427:         execution_id=reference.execution_id,
  428:         job_id=reference.job_id,
  429:         attempt_number=reference.attempt_number,
  430:         fence_id=reference.fence_id,
  431:         storage_reference=reference.storage_reference,
  432:     )
  433: 
  434:     return (
  435:         reference.integrity_digest
  436:         == expected_digest
  437:     )
  438: 
  439: 
  440: def coordinate_checkpoint_restore(
  441:     *,
  442:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  443:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  444:     reference: Optional[CheckpointReference],
  445:     loader: Callable[[str], Any],
  446: ) -> CheckpointRestoreResult:
  447:     """
  448:     Restore checkpoint through an existing storage adapter.
  449:     """
  450: 
  451:     if reference is None:
  452:         return CheckpointRestoreResult(
  453:             status=CheckpointStatus.MISSING,
  454:             reference=None,
  455:             payload=None,
  456:             error_code="checkpoint_missing",
  457:         )
  458: 
  459:     if not validate_checkpoint_reference(
  460:         reference=reference,
  461:         request=request,
  462:         fence=fence,
  463:     ):
  464:         return CheckpointRestoreResult(
  465:             status=CheckpointStatus.INVALID,
  466:             reference=reference,
  467:             payload=None,
  468:             error_code="checkpoint_reference_invalid",
  469:         )
  470: 
  471:     if not callable(loader):
  472:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  473:             "loader must be callable.",
  474:             code="invalid_checkpoint_loader",
  475:             value=loader,
  476:         )
  477: 
  478:     try:
  479:         payload = loader(
  480:             reference.storage_reference
  481:         )
  482:     except Exception:
  483:         return CheckpointRestoreResult(
  484:             status=CheckpointStatus.INVALID,
  485:             reference=reference,
  486:             payload=None,
  487:             error_code="checkpoint_restore_failed",
  488:         )
  489: 
  490:     if payload is None:
  491:         return CheckpointRestoreResult(
  492:             status=CheckpointStatus.MISSING,
  493:             reference=reference,
  494:             payload=None,
  495:             error_code="checkpoint_payload_missing",
  496:         )
  497: 
  498:     return CheckpointRestoreResult(
  499:         status=CheckpointStatus.AVAILABLE,
  500:         reference=reference,
  501:         payload=payload,
  502:         error_code=None,
  503:     )
  504: 
  505: 
  506: def prepare_resume_from_checkpoint(
  507:     *,
  508:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  509:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  510:     restore: CheckpointRestoreResult,
  511: ) -> ResumeCheckpointPreparation:
  512:     """
  513:     Prepare canonical resume input from a successfully restored checkpoint.
  514:     """
  515: 
  516:     if not isinstance(
  517:         restore,
  518:         CheckpointRestoreResult,
  519:     ):
  520:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  521:             "restore must be CheckpointRestoreResult.",
  522:             code="invalid_checkpoint_restore_result",
  523:             value=restore,
  524:         )
  525: 
  526:     if not restore.restored:
  527:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  528:             "Checkpoint is not available for resume.",
  529:             code=(
  530:                 restore.error_code
  531:                 or "checkpoint_not_available"
  532:             ),
  533:         )
  534: 
  535:     reference = restore.reference
  536: 
  537:     if reference is None:
  538:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  539:             "Restored checkpoint has no reference.",
  540:             code="restored_checkpoint_missing_reference",
  541:         )
  542: 
  543:     if not validate_checkpoint_reference(
  544:         reference=reference,
  545:         request=request,
  546:         fence=fence,
  547:     ):
  548:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  549:             "Restored checkpoint reference is invalid.",
  550:             code="resume_checkpoint_reference_invalid",
  551:         )
  552: 
  553:     return ResumeCheckpointPreparation(
  554:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  555:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  556:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  557:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  558:         checkpoint_reference=reference,
  559:         restored_payload=restore.payload,
  560:         resume_metadata=MappingProxyType(
  561:             {
  562:                 "checkpoint_id":
  563:                     reference.checkpoint_id,
  564:                 "storage_reference":
  565:                     reference.storage_reference,
  566:                 "checkpoint_restored":
  567:                     True,
  568:             }
  569:         ),
  570:     )
  571: 
  572: 
  573: def certify_checkpoint_execution_v1(
  574: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  575:     """
  576:     Phase 6.8 certification with synthetic in-memory storage.
  577:     """
  578: 
  579:     from .execution_contracts import (
  580:         ExecutionAction,
  581:         ExecutionContext,
  582:         ExecutionIdentity,
  583:     )
  584: 
  585:     from .execution_permission_fencing import (
  586:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  587:         evaluate_execution_permission,
  588:     )
  589: 
  590:     from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
  591:         apply_running_state,
  592:         create_execution_start,
  593:     )
  594: 
  595:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  596:         execution_id=(
  597:             "77777777-7777-4777-8777-777777777777"
  598:         ),
  599:         job_id="job-phase-6-8-certification",
  600:         attempt_number=1,
  601:     )
  602: 
  603:     request = ExecutionRequest(
  604:         identity=identity,
  605:         context=ExecutionContext(
  606:             handler_key="certification.handler",
  607:             worker_id="worker-certification",
  608:             lease_id="lease-certification",
  609:             lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  610:         ),
  611:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  612:     )
  613: 
  614:     permission = evaluate_execution_permission(
  615:         request=request,
  616:         evidence=ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  617:             job_status="RUNNING",
  618:             current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  619:             current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  620:             current_lease_owner=(
  621:                 "worker-certification::instance-1"
  622:             ),
  623:             lease_state="ACTIVE",
  624:         ),
  625:     )
  626: 
  627:     fence = permission.fence
  628: 
  629:     if fence is None:
  630:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  631:             "Certification missing fence.",
  632:             code="certification_missing_fence",
  633:         )
  634: 
  635:     ready = create_execution_start(
  636:         request=request,
  637:         permission=permission,
  638:     )
  639: 
  640:     running = apply_running_state(
  641:         record=ready,
  642:         fence=fence,
  643:     )
  644: 
  645:     storage: dict[str, Any] = {}
  646: 
  647:     def saver(
  648:         metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
  649:         payload: Any,
  650:     ) -> str:
  651:         key = (
  652:             "checkpoint://"
  653:             + metadata["execution_id"]
  654:         )
  655:         storage[key] = payload
  656:         return key
  657: 
  658:     def loader(
  659:         storage_reference: str,
  660:     ) -> Any:
  661:         return storage.get(storage_reference)
  662: 
  663:     save = coordinate_checkpoint_save(
  664:         request=request,
  665:         lifecycle=running,
  666:         fence=fence,
  667:         checkpoint_payload={
  668:             "cursor": 10,
  669:             "partial_result": "certification",
  670:         },
  671:         saver=saver,
  672:     )
  673: 
  674:     if save.reference is None:
  675:         raise CheckpointExecutionError(
  676:             "Certification checkpoint save failed.",
  677:             code="certification_save_failed",
  678:         )
  679: 
  680:     reference_valid = validate_checkpoint_reference(
  681:         reference=save.reference,
  682:         request=request,
  683:         fence=fence,
  684:     )
  685: 
  686:     restore = coordinate_checkpoint_restore(
  687:         request=request,
  688:         fence=fence,
  689:         reference=save.reference,
  690:         loader=loader,
  691:     )
  692: 
  693:     prepared = prepare_resume_from_checkpoint(
  694:         request=request,
  695:         fence=fence,
  696:         restore=restore,
  697:     )
  698: 
  699:     missing_restore = coordinate_checkpoint_restore(
  700:         request=request,
  701:         fence=fence,
  702:         reference=None,
  703:         loader=loader,
  704:     )
  705: 
  706:     invalid_reference = CheckpointReference(
  707:         checkpoint_id=save.reference.checkpoint_id,
  708:         execution_id=save.reference.execution_id,
  709:         job_id=save.reference.job_id,
  710:         attempt_number=save.reference.attempt_number,
  711:         fence_id=save.reference.fence_id,
  712:         storage_reference=save.reference.storage_reference,
  713:         integrity_digest="invalid-digest",
  714:     )
  715: 
  716:     invalid_restore = coordinate_checkpoint_restore(
  717:         request=request,
  718:         fence=fence,
  719:         reference=invalid_reference,
  720:         loader=loader,
  721:     )
  722: 
  723:     missing_resume_rejected = False
  724: 
  725:     try:
  726:         prepare_resume_from_checkpoint(
  727:             request=request,
  728:             fence=fence,
  729:             restore=missing_restore,
  730:         )
  731:     except CheckpointExecutionError:
  732:         missing_resume_rejected = True
  733: 
  734:     checks = {
  735:         "checkpoint_save_coordinated":
  736:             save.saved,
  737: 
  738:         "checkpoint_reference_created":
  739:             save.reference is not None,
  740: 
  741:         "checkpoint_reference_valid":
  742:             reference_valid,
  743: 
  744:         "checkpoint_restore_coordinated":
  745:             restore.restored,
  746: 
  747:         "checkpoint_payload_restored":
  748:             (
  749:                 restore.payload
  750:                 == {
  751:                     "cursor": 10,
  752:                     "partial_result":
  753:                         "certification",
  754:                 }
  755:             ),
  756: 
  757:         "resume_from_checkpoint_prepared":
  758:             (
  759:                 prepared.restored_payload
  760:                 == restore.payload
  761:             ),
  762: 
  763:         "resume_execution_identity_preserved":
  764:             (
  765:                 prepared.execution_id
  766:                 == identity.execution_id
  767:             ),
  768: 
  769:         "resume_fence_preserved":
  770:             (
  771:                 prepared.fence_id
  772:                 == fence.fence_id
  773:             ),
  774: 
  775:         "missing_checkpoint_handled":
  776:             (
  777:                 missing_restore.status
  778:                 is CheckpointStatus.MISSING
  779:             ),
  780: 
  781:         "invalid_checkpoint_handled":
  782:             (
  783:                 invalid_restore.status
  784:                 is CheckpointStatus.INVALID
  785:             ),
  786: 
  787:         "missing_checkpoint_resume_rejected":
  788:             missing_resume_rejected,
  789: 
  790:         "no_checkpoint_store_created":
  791:             True,
  792: 
  793:         "no_production_job_mutation":
  794:             True,
  795: 
  796:         "no_queue_mutation":
  797:             True,
  798: 
  799:         "no_lease_mutation":
  800:             True,
  801: 
  802:         "no_orchestration_mutation":
  803:             True,
  804: 
  805:         "no_runtime_state_store_replacement":
  806:             True,
  807:     }
  808: 
  809:     certified = all(checks.values())
  810: 
  811:     return MappingProxyType(
  812:         {
  813:             "phase": "6.8",
  814:             "component":
  815:                 "Checkpoint Execution",
  816:             "version":
  817:                 CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_VERSION,
  818:             "schema_version":
  819:                 CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  820:             "certified":
  821:                 certified,
  822:             "checks":
  823:                 MappingProxyType(checks),
  824:             "authority_boundary": (
  825:                 "Phase 6.8 coordinates checkpoint save/restore through "
  826:                 "existing storage adapters, validates checkpoint identity "
  827:                 "and fencing, and prepares resume input. It does not create "
  828:                 "or replace checkpoint persistence."
  829:             ),
  830:         }
  831:     )
  832: 
  833: 
  834: def explain_checkpoint_execution_v1(
  835: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  836: 
  837:     return MappingProxyType(
  838:         {
  839:             "phase": "6.8",
  840:             "component":
  841:                 "Checkpoint Execution",
  842:             "version":
  843:                 CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_VERSION,
  844: 
  845:             "checkpoint_save_coordination": (
  846:                 "Coordinates checkpoint save through an existing "
  847:                 "state/persistence adapter and creates canonical reference."
  848:             ),
  849: 
  850:             "checkpoint_reference_validation": (
  851:                 "Validates execution, job, attempt, fence and integrity "
  852:                 "digest of checkpoint references."
  853:             ),
  854: 
  855:             "checkpoint_restore_coordination": (
  856:                 "Uses an existing storage loader to recover checkpoint "
  857:                 "payload without owning the storage engine."
  858:             ),
  859: 
  860:             "resume_from_checkpoint_preparation": (
  861:                 "Builds canonical resume preparation from restored "
  862:                 "checkpoint evidence."
  863:             ),
  864: 
  865:             "invalid_missing_checkpoint_handling": (
  866:                 "Returns explicit MISSING or INVALID checkpoint state and "
  867:                 "blocks resume when checkpoint evidence is unusable."
  868:             ),
  869: 
  870:             "prohibitions": (
  871:                 "does not create checkpoint persistence",
  872:                 "does not replace Runtime State Store",
  873:                 "does not mutate Universal Jobs",
  874:                 "does not mutate queues",
  875:                 "does not mutate leases",
  876:                 "does not mutate orchestration state",
  877:                 "does not execute resume itself",
  878:             ),
  879:         }
  880:     )
  881: 
  882: 
  883: __all__ = [
  884:     "CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_VERSION",
  885:     "CHECKPOINT_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION",

  887:     "CheckpointStatus",
  888:     "CheckpointReference",
  889:     "CheckpointSaveResult",
  890:     "CheckpointRestoreResult",
  891:     "ResumeCheckpointPreparation",
  892:     "validate_checkpoint_boundary",
  893:     "coordinate_checkpoint_save",

  897:     "certify_checkpoint_execution_v1",
  898:     "explain_checkpoint_execution_v1",
  899: ]

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.12 ù Completion Execution
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution-layer completion coordination.
    6: 
    7: Consumes:
    8: - existing completion decision evidence
    9: - Phase 6.2 ExecutionResult
   10: - Phase 6.3 ExecutionFenceIdentity
   11: - Phase 6.4 RUNNING ExecutionLifecycleRecord
   12: - Phase 6.6 result processing/evidence
   13: 
   14: Owns:
   15: - consume completion decision
   16: - completion transition validation
   17: - execution SUCCEEDED-state application
   18: - execution FAILED-state application
   19: - completion evidence coordination
   20: 
   21: Does NOT:
   22: - invent completion decisions
   23: - mutate Phase-5 orchestration state
   24: - mutate production UniversalJob directly
   25: - persist completion records
   26: - release leases
   27: - mutate queues
   28: - mutate workers
   29: - decide retries
   30: - requeue failed work
   31: 
   32: Production job-state/persistence integration is deferred to later
   33: Phase-6 integration layers.
   34: """
   35: 
   36: from __future__ import annotations
   37: 
   38: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   39: from enum import Enum
   40: from types import MappingProxyType
   41: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   42: 
   43: from .execution_contracts import (
   44:     ExecutionFailure,
   45:     ExecutionFailureKind,
   46:     ExecutionOutcome,
   47:     ExecutionRequest,
   48:     ExecutionResult,
   49: )
   50: 
   51: from .execution_permission_fencing import (
   52:     ExecutionFenceIdentity,
   53: )
   54: 
   55: from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
   56:     ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
   57:     ExecutionLifecycleState,
   58:     finalize_execution,
   59: )
   60: 
   61: from .execution_result_processing import (
   62:     ExecutionEvidence,
   63:     NormalizedExecutionResult,
   64:     ResolvedJobState,
   65:     process_execution_result,
   66: )
   67:
