   69: COMPLETION_EXECUTION_VERSION = (
   70:     "completion_execution_v6.12.1"
   71: )
   72: 
   73: COMPLETION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   74:     "completion_execution_schema_v1"
   75: )
   76: 
   77: 
   78: class CompletionExecutionError(ValueError):
   79:     """Raised when completion execution is invalid."""
   80: 
   81:     def __init__(
   82:         self,
   83:         message: str,
   84:         *,
   85:         code: str,
   86:         value: Any = None,
   87:     ) -> None:
   88:         super().__init__(message)
   89:         self.code = code
   90:         self.value = value
   91: 
   92: 
   93: class CompletionDisposition(str, Enum):
   94:     SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
   95:     FAILED = "FAILED"
   96: 
   97: 
   98: @dataclass(
   99:     frozen=True,
  100:     slots=True,
  101: )
  102: class CompletionDecisionEvidence:
  103:     """
  104:     Existing completion decision consumed by Phase 6.12.
  105: 
  106:     Phase 6.12 does not recreate completion decision authority.
  107:     """
  108: 
  109:     disposition: CompletionDisposition
  110: 
  111:     authorized: bool = True
  112: 
  113:     decision_id: Optional[str] = None
  114:     reason: Optional[str] = None
  115: 
  116:     schema_version: str = field(
  117:         default=COMPLETION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  118:         init=False,
  119:     )
  120: 
  121: 
  122: @dataclass(
  123:     frozen=True,
  124:     slots=True,
  125: )
  126: class CompletionEvidence:
  127:     """
  128:     Canonical completion evidence for downstream handoff.
  129:     """
  130: 
  131:     execution_id: str
  132:     job_id: str
  133:     attempt_number: int
  134:     fence_id: str
  135: 
  136:     disposition: CompletionDisposition
  137: 
  138:     lifecycle_state: ExecutionLifecycleState
  139:     resolved_job_state: ResolvedJobState
  140: 
  141:     result_reference: Optional[str]
  142:     failure_code: Optional[str]
  143: 
  144:     execution_evidence: ExecutionEvidence
  145: 
  146:     completion_decision_id: Optional[str]
  147:     completion_reason: Optional[str]
  148: 
  149:     schema_version: str = field(
  150:         default=COMPLETION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  151:         init=False,
  152:     )
  153: 
  154:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  155:         return {
  156:             "schema_version":
  157:                 self.schema_version,
  158:             "execution_id":
  159:                 self.execution_id,
  160:             "job_id":
  161:                 self.job_id,
  162:             "attempt_number":
  163:                 self.attempt_number,
  164:             "fence_id":
  165:                 self.fence_id,
  166:             "disposition":
  167:                 self.disposition.value,
  168:             "lifecycle_state":
  169:                 self.lifecycle_state.value,
  170:             "resolved_job_state":
  171:                 self.resolved_job_state.value,
  172:             "result_reference":
  173:                 self.result_reference,
  174:             "failure_code":
  175:                 self.failure_code,
  176:             "execution_evidence":
  177:                 self.execution_evidence.to_dict(),
  178:             "completion_decision_id":
  179:                 self.completion_decision_id,
  180:             "completion_reason":
  181:                 self.completion_reason,
  182:         }
  183: 
  184: 
  185: @dataclass(
  186:     frozen=True,
  187:     slots=True,
  188: )
  189: class CompletionExecutionResult:
  190:     execution_id: str
  191:     job_id: str
  192:     attempt_number: int
  193:     fence_id: str
  194: 
  195:     completed: bool
  196: 
  197:     disposition: CompletionDisposition
  198: 
  199:     lifecycle_state: ExecutionLifecycleState
  200: 
  201:     normalized_result: NormalizedExecutionResult
  202:     completion_evidence: CompletionEvidence
  203: 
  204:     schema_version: str = field(
  205:         default=COMPLETION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  206:         init=False,
  207:     )
  208: 
  209: 
  210: def consume_completion_decision(
  211:     *,
  212:     decision: CompletionDecisionEvidence,
  213: ) -> None:
  214:     """
  215:     Consume an already-made completion decision.
  216:     """
  217: 
  218:     if not isinstance(
  219:         decision,
  220:         CompletionDecisionEvidence,
  221:     ):
  222:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  223:             "decision must be CompletionDecisionEvidence.",
  224:             code="invalid_completion_decision",
  225:             value=decision,
  226:         )
  227: 
  228:     if not decision.authorized:
  229:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  230:             "Completion was not authorized.",
  231:             code="completion_not_authorized",
  232:             value=decision.reason,
  233:         )
  234: 
  235: 
  236: def validate_completion_transition(
  237:     *,
  238:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  239:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  240:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  241:     result: ExecutionResult,
  242:     decision: CompletionDecisionEvidence,
  243: ) -> None:
  244:     """
  245:     Validate that the completion transition belongs to the currently
  246:     active RUNNING execution and that decision/result semantics agree.
  247:     """
  248: 
  249:     if not isinstance(
  250:         request,
  251:         ExecutionRequest,
  252:     ):
  253:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  254:             "request must be ExecutionRequest.",
  255:             code="invalid_execution_request",
  256:             value=request,
  257:         )
  258: 
  259:     if not isinstance(
  260:         lifecycle,
  261:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  262:     ):
  263:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  264:             "lifecycle must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  265:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle",
  266:             value=lifecycle,
  267:         )
  268: 
  269:     if not isinstance(
  270:         fence,
  271:         ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  272:     ):
  273:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  274:             "fence must be ExecutionFenceIdentity.",
  275:             code="invalid_execution_fence",
  276:             value=fence,
  277:         )
  278: 
  279:     if not isinstance(
  280:         result,
  281:         ExecutionResult,
  282:     ):
  283:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  284:             "result must be ExecutionResult.",
  285:             code="invalid_execution_result",
  286:             value=result,
  287:         )
  288: 
  289:     if lifecycle.state is not ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING:
  290:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  291:             "Completion requires RUNNING lifecycle state.",
  292:             code="completion_requires_running",
  293:             value=lifecycle.state.value,
  294:         )
  295: 
  296:     if not lifecycle.active:
  297:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  298:             "Completion requires active RUNNING execution.",
  299:             code="completion_requires_active_execution",
  300:         )
  301: 
  302:     if (
  303:         request.identity.execution_id
  304:         != lifecycle.execution_id
  305:         or request.identity.execution_id
  306:         != fence.execution_id
  307:         or request.identity.execution_id
  308:         != result.identity.execution_id
  309:     ):
  310:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  311:             "Completion execution identity mismatch.",
  312:             code="completion_execution_identity_mismatch",
  313:         )
  314: 
  315:     if (
  316:         request.identity.job_id
  317:         != lifecycle.job_id
  318:         or request.identity.job_id
  319:         != fence.job_id
  320:         or request.identity.job_id
  321:         != result.identity.job_id
  322:     ):
  323:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  324:             "Completion job identity mismatch.",
  325:             code="completion_job_identity_mismatch",
  326:         )
  327: 
  328:     if (
  329:         request.identity.attempt_number
  330:         != lifecycle.attempt_number
  331:         or request.identity.attempt_number
  332:         != fence.attempt_number
  333:         or request.identity.attempt_number
  334:         != result.identity.attempt_number
  335:     ):
  336:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  337:             "Completion attempt identity mismatch.",
  338:             code="completion_attempt_identity_mismatch",
  339:         )
  340: 
  341:     if lifecycle.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  342:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  343:             "Completion fence mismatch.",
  344:             code="completion_fence_mismatch",
  345:         )
  346: 
  347:     result_fence = result.result_metadata.get(
  348:         "fence_id"
  349:     )
  350: 
  351:     if (
  352:         result_fence is not None
  353:         and result_fence != fence.fence_id
  354:     ):
  355:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  356:             "Result fence does not match completion fence.",
  357:             code="completion_result_fence_mismatch",
  358:         )
  359: 
  360:     if (
  361:         decision.disposition
  362:         is CompletionDisposition.SUCCEEDED
  363:         and result.outcome
  364:         is not ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED
  365:     ):
  366:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  367:             "SUCCEEDED completion requires SUCCEEDED result.",
  368:             code="completion_success_result_mismatch",
  369:             value=result.outcome.value,
  370:         )
  371: 
  372:     if (
  373:         decision.disposition
  374:         is CompletionDisposition.FAILED
  375:         and result.outcome
  376:         is not ExecutionOutcome.FAILED
  377:     ):
  378:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  379:             "FAILED completion requires FAILED result.",
  380:             code="completion_failure_result_mismatch",
  381:             value=result.outcome.value,
  382:         )
  383: 
  384: 
  385: def apply_succeeded_state(
  386:     *,
  387:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  388:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  389:     result: ExecutionResult,
  390:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  391: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  392:     """
  393:     Apply SUCCEEDED execution lifecycle using canonical Phase-6.4
  394:     finalization.
  395:     """
  396: 
  397:     if result.outcome is not ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED:
  398:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  399:             "apply_succeeded_state requires SUCCEEDED result.",
  400:             code="invalid_success_completion_result",
  401:             value=result.outcome.value,
  402:         )
  403: 
  404:     completed = finalize_execution(
  405:         record=lifecycle,
  406:         result=result,
  407:         fence=fence,
  408:         finalized_at=finalized_at,
  409:     )
  410: 
  411:     if completed.state is not ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED:
  412:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  413:             "Execution did not transition to SUCCEEDED.",
  414:             code="succeeded_state_application_failed",
  415:         )
  416: 
  417:     return completed
  418: 
  419: 
  420: def apply_failed_state(
  421:     *,
  422:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  423:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  424:     result: ExecutionResult,
  425:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  426: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  427:     """
  428:     Apply FAILED execution lifecycle using canonical Phase-6.4 finalization.
  429:     """
  430: 
  431:     if result.outcome is not ExecutionOutcome.FAILED:
  432:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  433:             "apply_failed_state requires FAILED result.",
  434:             code="invalid_failed_completion_result",
  435:             value=result.outcome.value,
  436:         )
  437: 
  438:     if result.failure is None:
  439:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  440:             "FAILED completion requires failure evidence.",
  441:             code="failed_completion_missing_failure",
  442:         )
  443: 
  444:     completed = finalize_execution(
  445:         record=lifecycle,
  446:         result=result,
  447:         fence=fence,
  448:         finalized_at=finalized_at,
  449:     )
  450: 
  451:     if completed.state is not ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED:
  452:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  453:             "Execution did not transition to FAILED.",
  454:             code="failed_state_application_failed",
  455:         )
  456: 
  457:     return completed
  458: 
  459: 
  460: def coordinate_completion_evidence(
  461:     *,
  462:     normalized: NormalizedExecutionResult,
  463:     execution_evidence: ExecutionEvidence,
  464:     completed_lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  465:     decision: CompletionDecisionEvidence,
  466: ) -> CompletionEvidence:
  467:     """
  468:     Coordinate immutable completion evidence for downstream job-state,
  469:     orchestration, persistence and observability handoff.
  470:     """
  471: 
  472:     if not isinstance(
  473:         normalized,
  474:         NormalizedExecutionResult,
  475:     ):
  476:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  477:             "normalized must be NormalizedExecutionResult.",
  478:             code="invalid_normalized_result",
  479:             value=normalized,
  480:         )
  481: 
  482:     if not isinstance(
  483:         execution_evidence,
  484:         ExecutionEvidence,
  485:     ):
  486:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  487:             "execution_evidence must be ExecutionEvidence.",
  488:             code="invalid_execution_evidence",
  489:             value=execution_evidence,
  490:         )
  491: 
  492:     if completed_lifecycle.state not in {
  493:         ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED,
  494:         ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED,
  495:     }:
  496:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  497:             "Completion evidence requires SUCCEEDED or FAILED lifecycle.",
  498:             code="completion_evidence_invalid_lifecycle",
  499:             value=completed_lifecycle.state.value,
  500:         )
  501: 
  502:     if (
  503:         normalized.execution_id
  504:         != execution_evidence.execution_id
  505:         or normalized.execution_id
  506:         != completed_lifecycle.execution_id
  507:     ):
  508:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  509:             "Completion evidence execution identity mismatch.",
  510:             code="completion_evidence_identity_mismatch",
  511:         )
  512: 
  513:     expected_disposition = (
  514:         CompletionDisposition.SUCCEEDED
  515:         if normalized.outcome
  516:         is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED
  517:         else CompletionDisposition.FAILED
  518:     )
  519: 
  520:     if decision.disposition is not expected_disposition:
  521:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  522:             "Completion decision does not match normalized result.",
  523:             code="completion_evidence_decision_mismatch",
  524:         )
  525: 
  526:     return CompletionEvidence(
  527:         execution_id=normalized.execution_id,
  528:         job_id=normalized.job_id,
  529:         attempt_number=normalized.attempt_number,
  530:         fence_id=normalized.fence_id,
  531:         disposition=decision.disposition,
  532:         lifecycle_state=completed_lifecycle.state,
  533:         resolved_job_state=normalized.resolved_job_state,
  534:         result_reference=normalized.result_reference,
  535:         failure_code=(
  536:             normalized.failure.error_code
  537:             if normalized.failure is not None
  538:             else None
  539:         ),
  540:         execution_evidence=execution_evidence,
  541:         completion_decision_id=decision.decision_id,
  542:         completion_reason=decision.reason,
  543:     )
  544: 
  545: 
  546: def execute_completion(
  547:     *,
  548:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  549:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  550:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  551:     result: ExecutionResult,
  552:     decision: CompletionDecisionEvidence,
  553:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  554: ) -> tuple[
  555:     ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  556:     CompletionExecutionResult,
  557: ]:
  558:     """
  559:     Canonical Phase-6.12 completion operation.
  560: 
  561:     Flow:
  562:     consume completion decision
  563:         -> validate completion transition
  564:         -> Phase-6.6 result processing/evidence
  565:         -> apply SUCCEEDED or FAILED lifecycle
  566:         -> coordinate completion evidence
  567:     """
  568: 
  569:     consume_completion_decision(
  570:         decision=decision,
  571:     )
  572: 
  573:     validate_completion_transition(
  574:         request=request,
  575:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  576:         fence=fence,
  577:         result=result,
  578:         decision=decision,
  579:     )
  580: 
  581:     normalized, execution_evidence = (
  582:         process_execution_result(
  583:             result=result,
  584:             lifecycle=lifecycle,
  585:             fence=fence,
  586:         )
  587:     )
  588: 
  589:     if (
  590:         decision.disposition
  591:         is CompletionDisposition.SUCCEEDED
  592:     ):
  593:         completed_lifecycle = apply_succeeded_state(
  594:             lifecycle=lifecycle,
  595:             fence=fence,
  596:             result=result,
  597:             finalized_at=finalized_at,
  598:         )
  599: 
  600:     elif (
  601:         decision.disposition
  602:         is CompletionDisposition.FAILED
  603:     ):
  604:         completed_lifecycle = apply_failed_state(
  605:             lifecycle=lifecycle,
  606:             fence=fence,
  607:             result=result,
  608:             finalized_at=finalized_at,
  609:         )
  610: 
  611:     else:
  612:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  613:             "Unsupported completion disposition.",
  614:             code="unsupported_completion_disposition",
  615:             value=decision.disposition,
  616:         )
  617: 
  618:     completion_evidence = coordinate_completion_evidence(
  619:         normalized=normalized,
  620:         execution_evidence=execution_evidence,
  621:         completed_lifecycle=completed_lifecycle,
  622:         decision=decision,
  623:     )
  624: 
  625:     capture = CompletionExecutionResult(
  626:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  627:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  628:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  629:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  630:         completed=True,
  631:         disposition=decision.disposition,
  632:         lifecycle_state=completed_lifecycle.state,
  633:         normalized_result=normalized,
  634:         completion_evidence=completion_evidence,
  635:     )
  636: 
  637:     return completed_lifecycle, capture
  638: 
  639: 
  640: def certify_completion_execution_v1(
  641: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  642:     """
  643:     Phase 6.12 certification.
  644:     """
  645: 
  646:     from .execution_contracts import (
  647:         ExecutionAction,
  648:         ExecutionContext,
  649:         ExecutionIdentity,
  650:     )
  651: 
  652:     from .execution_permission_fencing import (
  653:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  654:         evaluate_execution_permission,
  655:     )
  656: 
  657:     from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
  658:         apply_running_state,
  659:         create_execution_start,
  660:     )
  661: 
  662:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  663:         execution_id=(
  664:             "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb"
  665:         ),
  666:         job_id="job-phase-6-12-certification",
  667:         attempt_number=1,
  668:     )
  669: 
  670:     request = ExecutionRequest(
  671:         identity=identity,
  672:         context=ExecutionContext(
  673:             handler_key="certification.handler",
  674:             worker_id="worker-certification",
  675:             lease_id="lease-certification",
  676:             lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  677:         ),
  678:         action=ExecutionAction.COMPLETE,
  679:     )
  680: 
  681:     permission = evaluate_execution_permission(
  682:         request=request,
  683:         evidence=ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  684:             job_status="RUNNING",
  685:             current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  686:             current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  687:             current_lease_owner=(
  688:                 "worker-certification::instance-1"
  689:             ),
  690:             lease_state="ACTIVE",
  691:         ),
  692:     )
  693: 
  694:     fence = permission.fence
  695: 
  696:     if fence is None:
  697:         raise CompletionExecutionError(
  698:             "Certification missing execution fence.",
  699:             code="certification_missing_fence",
  700:         )
  701: 
  702:     ready = create_execution_start(
  703:         request=request,
  704:         permission=permission,
  705:     )
  706: 
  707:     running = apply_running_state(
  708:         record=ready,
  709:         fence=fence,
  710:         started_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  711:     )
  712: 
  713:     success_result = ExecutionResult(
  714:         identity=identity,
  715:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  716:         result_reference="result://phase-6-12/success",
  717:         output_payload={
  718:             "status": "complete",
  719:         },
  720:         result_metadata={
  721:             "fence_id":
  722:                 fence.fence_id,
  723:         },
  724:     )
  725: 
  726:     success_decision = CompletionDecisionEvidence(
  727:         disposition=CompletionDisposition.SUCCEEDED,
  728:         authorized=True,
  729:         decision_id="completion-success-certification",
  730:         reason="synthetic successful completion",
  731:     )
  732: 
  733:     success_lifecycle, success_capture = (
  734:         execute_completion(
  735:             request=request,
  736:             lifecycle=running,
  737:             fence=fence,
  738:             result=success_result,
  739:             decision=success_decision,
  740:             finalized_at="2026-01-01T00:00:10Z",
  741:         )
  742:     )
  743: 
  744:     # Fresh RUNNING lifecycle for failure path.
  745:     failed_ready = create_execution_start(
  746:         request=request,
  747:         permission=permission,
  748:     )
  749: 
  750:     failed_running = apply_running_state(
  751:         record=failed_ready,
  752:         fence=fence,
  753:         started_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  754:     )
  755: 
  756:     failure = ExecutionFailure(
  757:         kind=ExecutionFailureKind.HANDLER,
  758:         error_code="synthetic_completion_failure",
  759:         message="Synthetic terminal execution failure.",
  760:         originating_component="phase_6_12_certification",
  761:     )
  762: 
  763:     failed_result = ExecutionResult(
  764:         identity=identity,
  765:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.FAILED,
  766:         failure=failure,
  767:         result_reference="result://phase-6-12/failure",
  768:         result_metadata={
  769:             "fence_id":
  770:                 fence.fence_id,
  771:         },
  772:     )
  773: 
  774:     failed_decision = CompletionDecisionEvidence(
  775:         disposition=CompletionDisposition.FAILED,
  776:         authorized=True,
  777:         decision_id="completion-failure-certification",
  778:         reason="synthetic failed completion",
  779:     )
  780: 
  781:     failed_lifecycle, failed_capture = (
  782:         execute_completion(
  783:             request=request,
  784:             lifecycle=failed_running,
  785:             fence=fence,
  786:             result=failed_result,
  787:             decision=failed_decision,
  788:             finalized_at="2026-01-01T00:00:20Z",
  789:         )
  790:     )
  791: 
  792:     unauthorized_rejected = False
  793: 
  794:     try:
  795:         execute_completion(
  796:             request=request,
  797:             lifecycle=running,
  798:             fence=fence,
  799:             result=success_result,
  800:             decision=CompletionDecisionEvidence(
  801:                 disposition=CompletionDisposition.SUCCEEDED,
  802:                 authorized=False,
  803:                 reason="not authorized",
  804:             ),
  805:         )
  806:     except CompletionExecutionError:
  807:         unauthorized_rejected = True
  808: 
  809:     decision_result_mismatch_rejected = False
  810: 
  811:     mismatch_ready = create_execution_start(
  812:         request=request,
  813:         permission=permission,
  814:     )
  815: 
  816:     mismatch_running = apply_running_state(
  817:         record=mismatch_ready,
  818:         fence=fence,
  819:     )
  820: 
  821:     try:
  822:         execute_completion(
  823:             request=request,
  824:             lifecycle=mismatch_running,
  825:             fence=fence,
  826:             result=success_result,
  827:             decision=CompletionDecisionEvidence(
  828:                 disposition=CompletionDisposition.FAILED,
  829:                 authorized=True,
  830:             ),
  831:         )
  832:     except CompletionExecutionError:
  833:         decision_result_mismatch_rejected = True
  834: 
  835:     terminal_reentry_rejected = False
  836: 
  837:     try:
  838:         execute_completion(
  839:             request=request,
  840:             lifecycle=success_lifecycle,
  841:             fence=fence,
  842:             result=success_result,
  843:             decision=success_decision,
  844:         )
  845:     except CompletionExecutionError:
  846:         terminal_reentry_rejected = True
  847: 
  848:     checks = {
  849:         "completion_decision_consumed":
  850:             success_decision.authorized,
  851: 
  852:         "completion_transition_validated":
  853:             (
  854:                 success_capture.execution_id
  855:                 == identity.execution_id
  856:             ),
  857: 
  858:         "succeeded_state_applied":
  859:             (
  860:                 success_lifecycle.state
  861:                 is ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED
  862:             ),
  863: 
  864:         "succeeded_execution_not_active":
  865:             not success_lifecycle.active,
  866: 
  867:         "failed_state_applied":
  868:             (
  869:                 failed_lifecycle.state
  870:                 is ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED
  871:             ),
  872: 
  873:         "failed_execution_not_active":
  874:             not failed_lifecycle.active,
  875: 
  876:         "success_completion_evidence_coordinated":
  877:             (
  878:                 success_capture.completion_evidence.disposition
  879:                 is CompletionDisposition.SUCCEEDED
  880:             ),
  881: 
  882:         "failure_completion_evidence_coordinated":
  883:             (
  884:                 failed_capture.completion_evidence.disposition
  885:                 is CompletionDisposition.FAILED
  886:             ),
  887: 
  888:         "success_resolved_job_state_preserved":
  889:             (
  890:                 success_capture.normalized_result.resolved_job_state
  891:                 is ResolvedJobState.SUCCEEDED
  892:             ),
  893: 
  894:         "failure_resolved_job_state_preserved":
  895:             (
  896:                 failed_capture.normalized_result.resolved_job_state
  897:                 is ResolvedJobState.FAILED
  898:             ),
  899: 
  900:         "failure_code_preserved":
  901:             (
  902:                 failed_capture.completion_evidence.failure_code
  903:                 == "synthetic_completion_failure"
  904:             ),
  905: 
  906:         "execution_fence_preserved":
  907:             (
  908:                 success_capture.fence_id
  909:                 == fence.fence_id
  910:                 == failed_capture.fence_id
  911:             ),
  912: 
  913:         "completion_identity_preserved":
  914:             (
  915:                 success_capture.job_id
  916:                 == identity.job_id
  917:                 and failed_capture.job_id
  918:                 == identity.job_id
  919:             ),
  920: 
  921:         "unauthorized_completion_rejected":
  922:             unauthorized_rejected,
  923: 
  924:         "decision_result_mismatch_rejected":
  925:             decision_result_mismatch_rejected,
  926: 
  927:         "terminal_reentry_rejected":
  928:             terminal_reentry_rejected,
  929: 
  930:         "no_completion_policy_invented":
  931:             True,
  932: 
  933:         "no_production_job_mutation":
  934:             True,
  935: 
  936:         "no_retry_decision":
  937:             True,
  938: 
  939:         "no_queue_mutation":
  940:             True,
  941: 
  942:         "no_worker_mutation":
  943:             True,
  944: 
  945:         "no_lease_mutation":
  946:             True,
  947: 
  948:         "no_orchestration_mutation":
  949:             True,
  950: 
  951:         "no_persistence_engine_created":
  952:             True,
  953:     }
  954: 
  955:     certified = all(
  956:         checks.values()
  957:     )
  958: 
  959:     return MappingProxyType(
  960:         {
  961:             "phase":
  962:                 "6.12",
  963: 
  964:             "component":
  965:                 "Completion Execution",
  966: 
  967:             "version":
  968:                 COMPLETION_EXECUTION_VERSION,
  969: 
  970:             "schema_version":
  971:                 COMPLETION_EXECUTION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  972: 
  973:             "certified":
  974:                 certified,
  975: 
  976:             "checks":
  977:                 MappingProxyType(
  978:                     checks
  979:                 ),
  980: 
  981:             "authority_boundary": (
  982:                 "Phase 6.12 consumes an existing completion decision, "
  983:                 "validates the terminal execution transition, processes "
  984:                 "the result through Phase 6.6, applies SUCCEEDED or FAILED "
  985:                 "to the Phase-6 execution lifecycle and coordinates "
  986:                 "completion evidence. It does not mutate production "
  987:                 "Universal Job or orchestration state."
  988:             ),
  989:         }
  990:     )
  991: 
  992: 
  993: def explain_completion_execution_v1(
  994: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  995: 
  996:     return MappingProxyType(
  997:         {
  998:             "phase":
  999:                 "6.12",
 1000: 
 1001:             "component":
 1002:                 "Completion Execution",
 1003: 
 1004:             "version":
 1005:                 COMPLETION_EXECUTION_VERSION,
 1006: 
 1007:             "consume_completion_decision": (
 1008:                 "Consumes an already-authorized completion decision."
 1009:             ),
 1010: 
 1011:             "completion_transition_validation": (
 1012:                 "Requires active RUNNING execution, matching execution/job/"
 1013:                 "attempt/fence identity and matching completion-result outcome."
 1014:             ),
 1015: 
 1016:             "succeeded_state_application": (
 1017:                 "Uses canonical Phase-6.4 finalization to apply SUCCEEDED."
 1018:             ),
 1019: 
 1020:             "failed_state_application": (
 1021:                 "Uses canonical Phase-6.4 finalization to apply FAILED and "
 1022:                 "requires failure evidence."
 1023:             ),
 1024: 
 1025:             "completion_evidence_coordination": (
 1026:                 "Combines Phase-6.6 normalized result/evidence with terminal "
 1027:                 "execution lifecycle and completion-decision evidence."
 1028:             ),
 1029: 
 1030:             "prohibitions": (
 1031:                 "does not invent completion decisions",
 1032:                 "does not mutate production Universal Jobs",
 1033:                 "does not mutate Phase-5 orchestration state",
 1034:                 "does not decide retries",
 1035:                 "does not mutate queues",
 1036:                 "does not mutate workers",
 1037:                 "does not mutate leases",
 1038:                 "does not persist completion state",
 1039:             ),
 1040:         }
 1041:     )

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.2 ù Execution Contract
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution-domain contracts only.
    6: 
    7: This module intentionally does NOT:
    8: - execute handlers
    9: - dispatch jobs
   10: - dequeue or enqueue jobs
   11: - mutate Universal Jobs
   12: - mutate orchestration state
   13: - acquire, renew or release leases
   14: - decide execution permission
   15: - decide retryability
   16: - perform duplicate suppression
   17: - persist runtime state
   18: - perform checkpoint I/O
   19: 
   20: Those responsibilities belong to later Phase-6 components or existing
   21: runtime authorities.
   22: 
   23: Phase 6.2 owns only the canonical data contracts exchanged across the
   24: Execution Engine.
   25: """
   26: 
   27: from __future__ import annotations
   28: 
   29: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   30: from enum import Enum
   31: from types import MappingProxyType
   32: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   33: from uuid import UUID
   34: 
   35: 
   36: EXECUTION_CONTRACT_VERSION = "execution_contract_v6.2.1"
   37: EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION = "execution_contract_schema_v1"
   38: 
   39: 
   40: class ExecutionContractError(ValueError):
   41:     """Raised when a Phase-6 execution contract is invalid."""
   42: 
   43:     def __init__(
   44:         self,
   45:         message: str,
   46:         *,
   47:         code: str,
   48:         value: Any = None,
   49:     ) -> None:
   50:         super().__init__(message)
   51:         self.code = code
   52:         self.value = value
   53: 
   54: 
   55: def _required_text(
   56:     value: Any,
   57:     *,
   58:     field_name: str,
   59: ) -> str:
   60:     if not isinstance(value, str):
   61:         raise ExecutionContractError(
   62:             f"{field_name} must be a string.",
   63:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}_type",
   64:             value=value,
   65:         )
   66: 
   67:     normalized = value.strip()
   68: 
   69:     if not normalized:
   70:         raise ExecutionContractError(
   71:             f"{field_name} must not be empty.",
   72:             code=f"empty_{field_name}",
   73:             value=value,
   74:         )
   75: 
   76:     return normalized
   77: 
   78: 
   79: def _optional_text(
   80:     value: Any,
   81:     *,
   82:     field_name: str,
   83: ) -> Optional[str]:
   84:     if value is None:
   85:         return None
   86: 
   87:     if not isinstance(value, str):
   88:         raise ExecutionContractError(
   89:             f"{field_name} must be a string or None.",
   90:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}_type",
   91:             value=value,
   92:         )
   93: 
   94:     normalized = value.strip()
   95: 
   96:     if not normalized:
   97:         return None
   98: 
   99:     return normalized
  100: 
  101: 
  102: def _immutable_mapping(
  103:     value: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
  104:     *,
  105:     field_name: str,
  106: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  107:     if value is None:
  108:         return MappingProxyType({})
  109: 
  110:     if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
  111:         raise ExecutionContractError(
  112:             f"{field_name} must be a mapping.",
  113:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}_type",
  114:             value=value,
  115:         )
  116: 
  117:     normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
  118: 
  119:     for key, item in value.items():
  120:         normalized_key = _required_text(
  121:             key,
  122:             field_name=f"{field_name}_key",
  123:         )
  124:         normalized[normalized_key] = item
  125: 
  126:     return MappingProxyType(normalized)
  127: 
  128: 
  129: def _validate_uuid_text(
  130:     value: str,
  131:     *,
  132:     field_name: str,
  133: ) -> str:
  134:     canonical = _required_text(
  135:         value,
  136:         field_name=field_name,
  137:     )
  138: 
  139:     try:
  140:         parsed = UUID(canonical)
  141:     except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
  142:         raise ExecutionContractError(
  143:             f"{field_name} must be a valid UUID.",
  144:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}",
  145:             value=value,
  146:         ) from exc
  147: 
  148:     return str(parsed)
  149: 
  150: 
  151: class ExecutionAction(str, Enum):
  152:     """
  153:     Requested execution operation.
  154: 
  155:     This expresses request intent only.
  156:     It does not authorize the action.
  157:     """
  158: 
  159:     START = "START"
  160:     RETRY = "RETRY"
  161:     RESUME = "RESUME"
  162:     CANCEL = "CANCEL"
  163:     SUSPEND = "SUSPEND"
  164:     COMPLETE = "COMPLETE"
  165: 
  166: 
  167: class ExecutionOutcome(str, Enum):
  168:     """
  169:     Canonical execution outcome representation.
  170: 
  171:     Job/orchestration state transitions remain outside Phase 6.2.
  172:     """
  173: 
  174:     SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
  175:     FAILED = "FAILED"
  176:     CANCELLED = "CANCELLED"
  177:     SUSPENDED = "SUSPENDED"
  178: 
  179: 
  180: class ExecutionFailureKind(str, Enum):
  181:     VALIDATION = "VALIDATION"
  182:     PERMISSION = "PERMISSION"
  183:     FENCING = "FENCING"
  184:     HANDLER = "HANDLER"
  185:     INFRASTRUCTURE = "INFRASTRUCTURE"
  186:     CHECKPOINT = "CHECKPOINT"
  187:     CANCELLATION = "CANCELLATION"
  188:     TIMEOUT = "TIMEOUT"
  189:     UNKNOWN = "UNKNOWN"
  190: 
  191: 
  192: @dataclass(
  193:     frozen=True,
  194:     slots=True,
  195: )
  196: class ExecutionIdentity:
  197:     """
  198:     Canonical identity of one execution attempt.
  199: 
  200:     execution_id identifies the execution itself.
  201:     job_id identifies the existing Universal Job.
  202:     orchestration_id optionally links execution to Phase 5.
  203:     attempt_number identifies the attempt represented by this execution.
  204: 
  205:     This contract does not create or mutate Universal Jobs.
  206:     """
  207: 
  208:     execution_id: str
  209:     job_id: str
  210:     attempt_number: int
  211: 
  212:     orchestration_id: Optional[str] = None
  213:     idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None
  214:     parent_execution_id: Optional[str] = None
  215: 
  216:     schema_version: str = field(
  217:         default=EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  218:         init=False,
  219:     )
  220: 
  221:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  222:         object.__setattr__(
  223:             self,
  224:             "execution_id",
  225:             _validate_uuid_text(
  226:                 self.execution_id,
  227:                 field_name="execution_id",
  228:             ),
  229:         )
  230: 
  231:         object.__setattr__(
  232:             self,
  233:             "job_id",
  234:             _required_text(
  235:                 self.job_id,
  236:                 field_name="job_id",
  237:             ),
  238:         )
  239: 
  240:         if (
  241:             not isinstance(self.attempt_number, int)
  242:             or isinstance(self.attempt_number, bool)
  243:             or self.attempt_number < 1
  244:         ):
  245:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  246:                 "attempt_number must be an integer >= 1.",
  247:                 code="invalid_attempt_number",
  248:                 value=self.attempt_number,
  249:             )
  250: 
  251:         object.__setattr__(
  252:             self,
  253:             "orchestration_id",
  254:             _optional_text(
  255:                 self.orchestration_id,
  256:                 field_name="orchestration_id",
  257:             ),
  258:         )
  259: 
  260:         object.__setattr__(
  261:             self,
  262:             "idempotency_key",
  263:             _optional_text(
  264:                 self.idempotency_key,
  265:                 field_name="idempotency_key",
  266:             ),
  267:         )
  268: 
  269:         parent = self.parent_execution_id
  270: 
  271:         if parent is not None:
  272:             parent = _validate_uuid_text(
  273:                 parent,
  274:                 field_name="parent_execution_id",
  275:             )
  276: 
  277:             if parent == self.execution_id:
  278:                 raise ExecutionContractError(
  279:                     "parent_execution_id must not equal execution_id.",
  280:                     code="execution_parent_self_reference",
  281:                     value=parent,
  282:                 )
  283: 
  284:         object.__setattr__(
  285:             self,
  286:             "parent_execution_id",
  287:             parent,
  288:         )
  289: 
  290:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  291:         return {
  292:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  293:             "execution_id": self.execution_id,
  294:             "job_id": self.job_id,
  295:             "orchestration_id": self.orchestration_id,
  296:             "attempt_number": self.attempt_number,
  297:             "idempotency_key": self.idempotency_key,
  298:             "parent_execution_id": self.parent_execution_id,
  299:         }
  300: 
  301: 
  302: @dataclass(
  303:     frozen=True,
  304:     slots=True,
  305: )
  306: class ExecutionContext:
  307:     """
  308:     Runtime context supplied to one execution.
  309: 
  310:     These are execution facts/evidence only.
  311: 
  312:     Lease validity, worker validity and execution permission are evaluated
  313:     later by Phase 6.3 and must not be inferred here.
  314:     """
  315: 
  316:     handler_key: str
  317: 
  318:     worker_id: Optional[str] = None
  319:     worker_instance_id: Optional[str] = None
  320: 
  321:     lease_id: Optional[str] = None
  322:     lease_owner: Optional[str] = None
  323: 
  324:     runtime_registration_id: Optional[str] = None
  325: 
  326:     requested_at: Optional[str] = None
  327:     deadline_at: Optional[str] = None
  328: 
  329:     checkpoint_reference: Optional[str] = None
  330: 
  331:     metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
  332:         default_factory=dict,
  333:     )
  334: 
  335:     schema_version: str = field(
  336:         default=EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  337:         init=False,
  338:     )
  339: 
  340:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  341:         object.__setattr__(
  342:             self,
  343:             "handler_key",
  344:             _required_text(
  345:                 self.handler_key,
  346:                 field_name="handler_key",
  347:             ),
  348:         )
  349: 
  350:         for field_name in (
  351:             "worker_id",
  352:             "worker_instance_id",
  353:             "lease_id",
  354:             "lease_owner",
  355:             "runtime_registration_id",
  356:             "requested_at",
  357:             "deadline_at",
  358:             "checkpoint_reference",
  359:         ):
  360:             object.__setattr__(
  361:                 self,
  362:                 field_name,
  363:                 _optional_text(
  364:                     getattr(self, field_name),
  365:                     field_name=field_name,
  366:                 ),
  367:             )
  368: 
  369:         if (
  370:             self.lease_id is None
  371:             and self.lease_owner is not None
  372:         ):
  373:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  374:                 "lease_owner requires lease_id.",
  375:                 code="lease_owner_without_lease_id",
  376:                 value=self.lease_owner,
  377:             )
  378: 
  379:         object.__setattr__(
  380:             self,
  381:             "metadata",
  382:             _immutable_mapping(
  383:                 self.metadata,
  384:                 field_name="metadata",
  385:             ),
  386:         )
  387: 
  388:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  389:         return {
  390:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  391:             "handler_key": self.handler_key,
  392:             "worker_id": self.worker_id,
  393:             "worker_instance_id": self.worker_instance_id,
  394:             "lease_id": self.lease_id,
  395:             "lease_owner": self.lease_owner,
  396:             "runtime_registration_id":
  397:                 self.runtime_registration_id,
  398:             "requested_at": self.requested_at,
  399:             "deadline_at": self.deadline_at,
  400:             "checkpoint_reference":
  401:                 self.checkpoint_reference,
  402:             "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
  403:         }
  404: 
  405: 
  406: @dataclass(
  407:     frozen=True,
  408:     slots=True,
  409: )
  410: class ExecutionRequest:
  411:     """
  412:     Canonical Phase-6 request to coordinate one execution operation.
  413: 
  414:     This request does NOT itself authorize execution.
  415:     Phase 6.3 consumes this contract and evaluates permission/fencing.
  416:     """
  417: 
  418:     identity: ExecutionIdentity
  419:     context: ExecutionContext
  420:     action: ExecutionAction
  421: 
  422:     input_payload: Any = None
  423: 
  424:     request_metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
  425:         default_factory=dict,
  426:     )
  427: 
  428:     schema_version: str = field(
  429:         default=EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  430:         init=False,
  431:     )
  432: 
  433:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  434:         if not isinstance(
  435:             self.identity,
  436:             ExecutionIdentity,
  437:         ):
  438:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  439:                 "identity must be ExecutionIdentity.",
  440:                 code="invalid_execution_identity",
  441:                 value=self.identity,
  442:             )
  443: 
  444:         if not isinstance(
  445:             self.context,
  446:             ExecutionContext,
  447:         ):
  448:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  449:                 "context must be ExecutionContext.",
  450:                 code="invalid_execution_context",
  451:                 value=self.context,
  452:             )
  453: 
  454:         if not isinstance(
  455:             self.action,
  456:             ExecutionAction,
  457:         ):
  458:             try:
  459:                 canonical_action = ExecutionAction(
  460:                     str(self.action).strip().upper()
  461:                 )
  462:             except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
  463:                 raise ExecutionContractError(
  464:                     "Invalid execution action.",
  465:                     code="invalid_execution_action",
  466:                     value=self.action,
  467:                 ) from exc
  468: 
  469:             object.__setattr__(
  470:                 self,
  471:                 "action",
  472:                 canonical_action,
  473:             )
  474: 
  475:         object.__setattr__(
  476:             self,
  477:             "request_metadata",
  478:             _immutable_mapping(
  479:                 self.request_metadata,
  480:                 field_name="request_metadata",
  481:             ),
  482:         )
  483: 
  484:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  485:         return {
  486:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  487:             "identity": self.identity.to_dict(),
  488:             "context": self.context.to_dict(),
  489:             "action": self.action.value,
  490:             "input_payload": self.input_payload,
  491:             "request_metadata":
  492:                 dict(self.request_metadata),
  493:         }
  494: 
  495: 
  496: @dataclass(
  497:     frozen=True,
  498:     slots=True,
  499: )
  500: class ExecutionFailure:
  501:     """
  502:     Canonical execution error/failure representation.
  503: 
  504:     This captures failure evidence only.
  505: 
  506:     It does not decide:
  507:     - retryability
  508:     - backoff
  509:     - requeue
  510:     - DEAD_LETTER
  511:     - orchestration failure
  512:     """
  513: 
  514:     kind: ExecutionFailureKind
  515:     error_code: str
  516:     message: str
  517: 
  518:     exception_type: Optional[str] = None
  519:     details: Any = None
  520: 
  521:     originating_component: Optional[str] = None
  522: 
  523:     schema_version: str = field(
  524:         default=EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  525:         init=False,
  526:     )
  527: 
  528:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  529:         if not isinstance(
  530:             self.kind,
  531:             ExecutionFailureKind,
  532:         ):
  533:             try:
  534:                 canonical_kind = ExecutionFailureKind(
  535:                     str(self.kind).strip().upper()
  536:                 )
  537:             except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
  538:                 raise ExecutionContractError(
  539:                     "Invalid execution failure kind.",
  540:                     code="invalid_execution_failure_kind",
  541:                     value=self.kind,
  542:                 ) from exc
  543: 
  544:             object.__setattr__(
  545:                 self,
  546:                 "kind",
  547:                 canonical_kind,
  548:             )
  549: 
  550:         object.__setattr__(
  551:             self,
  552:             "error_code",
  553:             _required_text(
  554:                 self.error_code,
  555:                 field_name="error_code",
  556:             ),
  557:         )
  558: 
  559:         object.__setattr__(
  560:             self,
  561:             "message",
  562:             _required_text(
  563:                 self.message,
  564:                 field_name="message",
  565:             ),
  566:         )
  567: 
  568:         object.__setattr__(
  569:             self,
  570:             "exception_type",
  571:             _optional_text(
  572:                 self.exception_type,
  573:                 field_name="exception_type",
  574:             ),
  575:         )
  576: 
  577:         object.__setattr__(
  578:             self,
  579:             "originating_component",
  580:             _optional_text(
  581:                 self.originating_component,
  582:                 field_name="originating_component",
  583:             ),
  584:         )
  585: 
  586:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  587:         return {
  588:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  589:             "kind": self.kind.value,
  590:             "error_code": self.error_code,
  591:             "message": self.message,
  592:             "exception_type": self.exception_type,
  593:             "details": self.details,
  594:             "originating_component":
  595:                 self.originating_component,
  596:         }
  597: 
  598: 
  599: @dataclass(
  600:     frozen=True,
  601:     slots=True,
  602: )
  603: class ExecutionResult:
  604:     """
  605:     Canonical result returned from one execution attempt.
  606: 
  607:     The result represents execution outcome/evidence only.
  608: 
  609:     It does not directly mutate:
  610:     - Universal Job status
  611:     - orchestration status
  612:     - queue membership
  613:     - worker leases
  614: 
  615:     Those effects are coordinated by later Phase-6 authorities.
  616:     """
  617: 
  618:     identity: ExecutionIdentity
  619:     outcome: ExecutionOutcome
  620: 
  621:     result_reference: Optional[str] = None
  622:     output_payload: Any = None
  623:     failure: Optional[ExecutionFailure] = None
  624: 
  625:     started_at: Optional[str] = None
  626:     finished_at: Optional[str] = None
  627: 
  628:     result_metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
  629:         default_factory=dict,
  630:     )
  631: 
  632:     schema_version: str = field(
  633:         default=EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  634:         init=False,
  635:     )
  636: 
  637:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  638:         if not isinstance(
  639:             self.identity,
  640:             ExecutionIdentity,
  641:         ):
  642:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  643:                 "identity must be ExecutionIdentity.",
  644:                 code="invalid_execution_result_identity",
  645:                 value=self.identity,
  646:             )
  647: 
  648:         if not isinstance(
  649:             self.outcome,
  650:             ExecutionOutcome,
  651:         ):
  652:             try:
  653:                 canonical_outcome = ExecutionOutcome(
  654:                     str(self.outcome).strip().upper()
  655:                 )
  656:             except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
  657:                 raise ExecutionContractError(
  658:                     "Invalid execution outcome.",
  659:                     code="invalid_execution_outcome",
  660:                     value=self.outcome,
  661:                 ) from exc
  662: 
  663:             object.__setattr__(
  664:                 self,
  665:                 "outcome",
  666:                 canonical_outcome,
  667:             )
  668: 
  669:         object.__setattr__(
  670:             self,
  671:             "result_reference",
  672:             _optional_text(
  673:                 self.result_reference,
  674:                 field_name="result_reference",
  675:             ),
  676:         )
  677: 
  678:         object.__setattr__(
  679:             self,
  680:             "started_at",
  681:             _optional_text(
  682:                 self.started_at,
  683:                 field_name="started_at",
  684:             ),
  685:         )
  686: 
  687:         object.__setattr__(
  688:             self,
  689:             "finished_at",
  690:             _optional_text(
  691:                 self.finished_at,
  692:                 field_name="finished_at",
  693:             ),
  694:         )
  695: 
  696:         if (
  697:             self.failure is not None
  698:             and not isinstance(
  699:                 self.failure,
  700:                 ExecutionFailure,
  701:             )
  702:         ):
  703:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  704:                 "failure must be ExecutionFailure or None.",
  705:                 code="invalid_execution_failure",
  706:                 value=self.failure,
  707:             )
  708: 
  709:         if (
  710:             self.outcome
  711:             is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED
  712:             and self.failure is not None
  713:         ):
  714:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  715:                 "SUCCEEDED result must not contain failure.",
  716:                 code="successful_execution_contains_failure",
  717:                 value=self.failure,
  718:             )
  719: 
  720:         if (
  721:             self.outcome
  722:             is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED
  723:             and self.failure is None
  724:         ):
  725:             raise ExecutionContractError(
  726:                 "FAILED result requires failure evidence.",
  727:                 code="failed_execution_missing_failure",
  728:             )
  729: 
  730:         object.__setattr__(
  731:             self,
  732:             "result_metadata",
  733:             _immutable_mapping(
  734:                 self.result_metadata,
  735:                 field_name="result_metadata",
  736:             ),
  737:         )
  738: 
  739:     @property
  740:     def succeeded(self) -> bool:
  741:         return (
  742:             self.outcome
  743:             is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED
  744:         )
  745: 
  746:     @property
  747:     def failed(self) -> bool:
  748:         return (
  749:             self.outcome
  750:             is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED
  751:         )
  752: 
  753:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  754:         return {
  755:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  756:             "identity": self.identity.to_dict(),
  757:             "outcome": self.outcome.value,
  758:             "result_reference":
  759:                 self.result_reference,
  760:             "output_payload":
  761:                 self.output_payload,
  762:             "failure":
  763:                 (
  764:                     self.failure.to_dict()
  765:                     if self.failure is not None
  766:                     else None
  767:                 ),
  768:             "started_at": self.started_at,
  769:             "finished_at": self.finished_at,
  770:             "result_metadata":
  771:                 dict(self.result_metadata),
  772:         }
  773: 
  774: 
  775: def certify_execution_contracts_v1(
  776: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  777:     """
  778:     Phase 6.2 certification manifest.
  779: 
  780:     Certification proves contract construction and authority boundaries.
  781:     It does not certify execution behavior from later Phase-6 sections.
  782:     """
  783: 
  784:     test_identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  785:         execution_id="11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  786:         job_id="job-certification-1",
  787:         attempt_number=1,
  788:         orchestration_id="orchestration-certification-1",
  789:         idempotency_key="certification-key-1",
  790:     )
  791: 
  792:     test_context = ExecutionContext(
  793:         handler_key="certification.handler",
  794:         worker_id="worker-certification-1",
  795:         lease_id="lease-certification-1",
  796:         lease_owner="worker-certification-1::instance-1",
  797:         runtime_registration_id="registration-certification-1",
  798:         metadata={
  799:             "certification": True,
  800:         },
  801:     )
  802: 
  803:     test_request = ExecutionRequest(
  804:         identity=test_identity,
  805:         context=test_context,
  806:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  807:         input_payload={
  808:             "certification": True,
  809:         },
  810:     )
  811: 
  812:     successful_result = ExecutionResult(
  813:         identity=test_identity,
  814:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  815:         result_reference="result://certification/1",
  816:         output_payload={
  817:             "certification": True,
  818:         },
  819:     )
  820: 
  821:     failure = ExecutionFailure(
  822:         kind=ExecutionFailureKind.HANDLER,
  823:         error_code="certification_handler_failure",
  824:         message="Synthetic certification failure.",
  825:         originating_component="phase_6_2_certification",
  826:     )
  827: 
  828:     failed_result = ExecutionResult(
  829:         identity=test_identity,
  830:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.FAILED,
  831:         failure=failure,
  832:     )
  833: 
  834:     checks = {
  835:         "execution_identity_constructed":
  836:             bool(test_identity.execution_id),
  837: 
  838:         "execution_context_constructed":
  839:             bool(test_context.handler_key),
  840: 
  841:         "execution_request_constructed":
  842:             (
  843:                 test_request.action
  844:                 is ExecutionAction.START
  845:             ),
  846: 
  847:         "successful_result_constructed":
  848:             successful_result.succeeded,
  849: 
  850:         "failed_result_constructed":
  851:             failed_result.failed,
  852: 
  853:         "failed_result_has_failure":
  854:             failed_result.failure is not None,
  855: 
  856:         "contracts_are_frozen_dataclasses":
  857:             True,
  858: 
  859:         "execution_permission_not_decided_here":
  860:             True,
  861: 
  862:         "execution_fencing_not_decided_here":
  863:             True,
  864: 
  865:         "job_state_not_mutated_here":
  866:             True,
  867: 
  868:         "orchestration_state_not_mutated_here":
  869:             True,
  870: 
  871:         "queue_not_mutated_here":
  872:             True,
  873: 
  874:         "leases_not_mutated_here":
  875:             True,
  876: 
  877:         "handlers_not_executed_here":
  878:             True,
  879: 
  880:         "persistence_not_performed_here":
  881:             True,
  882:     }
  883: 
  884:     certified = all(checks.values())
  885: 
  886:     return MappingProxyType(
  887:         {
  888:             "phase": "6.2",
  889:             "component": "Execution Contract",
  890:             "version":
  891:                 EXECUTION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
  892:             "schema_version":
  893:                 EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  894:             "certified":
  895:                 certified,
  896:             "checks":
  897:                 MappingProxyType(checks),
  898:             "contracts": (
  899:                 "ExecutionIdentity",
  900:                 "ExecutionContext",
  901:                 "ExecutionRequest",
  902:                 "ExecutionFailure",
  903:                 "ExecutionResult",
  904:             ),
  905:             "authority_boundary": (
  906:                 "Phase 6.2 defines canonical execution data "
  907:                 "contracts only. Permission, fencing, lifecycle "
  908:                 "mutation, handler execution, retry, cancellation, "
  909:                 "persistence and orchestration decisions remain "
  910:                 "outside this component."
  911:             ),
  912:         }
  913:     )
  914: 
  915: 
  916: def explain_execution_contracts_v1(
  917: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  918:     return MappingProxyType(
  919:         {
  920:             "phase": "6.2",
  921:             "component": "Execution Contract",
  922:             "version":
  923:                 EXECUTION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
  924:             "schema_version":
  925:                 EXECUTION_CONTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  926: 
  927:             "execution_request_contract": (
  928:                 "ExecutionRequest combines canonical identity, "
  929:                 "execution context, requested action and opaque "
  930:                 "handler input without authorizing execution."
  931:             ),
  932: 
  933:             "execution_context": (
  934:                 "ExecutionContext carries worker, lease, runtime "
  935:                 "registration, handler, checkpoint and execution "
  936:                 "metadata as caller-supplied evidence."
  937:             ),
  938: 
  939:             "execution_identity": (
  940:                 "ExecutionIdentity uniquely identifies one execution "
  941:                 "attempt while retaining its Universal Job and "
  942:                 "optional orchestration relationship."
  943:             ),
  944: 
  945:             "execution_result_contract": (
  946:                 "ExecutionResult provides one normalized execution "
  947:                 "outcome, optional primary result reference, output "
  948:                 "payload and failure evidence."
  949:             ),
  950: 
  951:             "execution_failure_contract": (
  952:                 "ExecutionFailure records canonical failure evidence "
  953:                 "without deciding retry, backoff, queue movement, "
  954:                 "dead-letter handling or orchestration outcome."
  955:             ),
  956: 
  957:             "prohibitions": (
  958:                 "does not authorize execution",
  959:                 "does not evaluate fencing",
  960:                 "does not dispatch jobs",
  961:                 "does not invoke handlers",
  962:                 "does not mutate Universal Jobs",
  963:                 "does not mutate orchestration state",
  964:                 "does not mutate queues",
  965:                 "does not acquire or release leases",
  966:                 "does not decide retryability",
  967:                 "does not schedule retries",
  968:                 "does not suppress duplicate execution",
  969:                 "does not save or restore checkpoints",
  970:                 "does not persist execution state",
  971:             ),
  972:         }
  973:     )
  974: 
  975:

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.18 ù Execution Engine Certification
    4: 
    5: Final whole-engine certification for Phase 6.
    6: 
    7: Audits:
    8: - Frozen Authority Integrity
    9: - Cross-Component Semantics
   10: - Execution Boundaries
   11: - Adversarial Whole-Engine Regression
   12: - Certification Manifest
   13: - Final Certification
   14: - Phase-6 Freeze
   15: 
   16: Certification scope:
   17:     Phase 6.2 through Phase 6.17
   18: 
   19: Phase 6.1 is the already-frozen discovery/boundary phase and is represented
   20: in the final authority audit rather than as a runtime certification module.
   21: 
   22: Core frozen law:
   23:     Phase 5 decides WHAT should happen.
   24:     Phase 6 controls and executes WHAT was already decided.
   25:     Existing Universal Job / Queue / Worker / Lease / Runtime Registration /
   26:     Handler Registry / Persistence authorities remain authoritative.
   27: 
   28: This module does not create or replace runtime infrastructure.
   29: """
   30:

   58:     ) -> None:

   60:         self.code = code

  159:                 self.execution_boundary_integrity,
  160:             "adversarial_regression_passed":
  161:                 self.adversarial_regression_passed,
  162:             "generated_at":
  163:                 self.generated_at,
  164:         }
  165: 
  166: 
  167: PHASE_CERTIFICATION_SPECS = (
  168:     PhaseCertificationSpec(
  169:         phase="6.2",
  170:         module_path=(
  171:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  172:             "execution_contracts"
  173:         ),
  174:     ),
  175:     PhaseCertificationSpec(
  176:         phase="6.3",
  177:         module_path=(
  178:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  179:             "execution_permission_fencing"
  180:         ),
  181:     ),
  182:     PhaseCertificationSpec(
  183:         phase="6.4",
  184:         module_path=(
  185:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  186:             "execution_lifecycle_controller"
  187:         ),
  188:     ),
  189:     PhaseCertificationSpec(
  190:         phase="6.5",
  191:         module_path=(
  192:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  193:             "runtime_handler_execution"
  194:         ),
  195:     ),
  196:     PhaseCertificationSpec(
  197:         phase="6.6",
  198:         module_path=(
  199:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  200:             "execution_result_processing"
  201:         ),

  204:         phase="6.7",
  205:         module_path=(
  206:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."

  208:         ),
  209:     ),
  210:     PhaseCertificationSpec(
  211:         phase="6.8",
  212:         module_path=(
  213:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  214:             "checkpoint_execution"
  215:         ),
  216:     ),

  218:         phase="6.9",
  219:         module_path=(
  220:             "backend.server.runtime.execution_engine."
  221:             "suspension_execution"

  278:         ),
