  280: )
  281: 
  282: 
  283: def _load_module(
  284:     module_path: str,
  285: ) -> ModuleType:
  286:     try:
  287:         return import_module(
  288:             module_path
  289:         )
  290: 
  291:     except Exception as exc:
  292:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  293:             f"Could not import certification module {module_path!r}.",
  294:             code="phase6_certification_module_import_failed",
  295:             value={
  296:                 "module":
  297:                     module_path,
  298:                 "error":
  299:                     repr(exc),
  300:             },
  301:         ) from exc
  302: 
  303: 
  304: def _discover_certifier(
  305:     module: ModuleType,
  306: ) -> tuple[
  307:     str,
  308:     Callable[[], Mapping[str, Any]],
  309: ]:
  310:     """
  311:     Each Phase-6 component owns exactly one public certify_*_v1 function.
  312:     """
  313: 
  314:     candidates: list[
  315:         tuple[
  316:             str,
  317:             Callable[[], Mapping[str, Any]],
  318:         ]
  319:     ] = []
  320: 
  321:     for name in dir(module):
  322:         if (
  323:             not name.startswith("certify_")
  324:             or not name.endswith("_v1")
  325:         ):
  326:             continue
  327: 
  328:         value = getattr(
  329:             module,
  330:             name,
  331:             None,
  332:         )
  333: 
  334:         if callable(value):
  335:             candidates.append(
  336:                 (
  337:                     name,
  338:                     value,
  339:                 )
  340:             )
  341: 
  342:     if len(candidates) != 1:
  343:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  344:             (
  345:                 "Expected exactly one certification function in "
  346:                 f"{module.__name__!r}; found {len(candidates)}."
  347:             ),
  348:             code="phase6_certifier_discovery_failed",
  349:             value={
  350:                 "module":
  351:                     module.__name__,
  352:                 "candidates":
  353:                     tuple(
  354:                         name
  355:                         for name, _
  356:                         in candidates
  357:                     ),
  358:             },
  359:         )
  360: 
  361:     return candidates[0]
  362: 
  363: 
  364: def _plain_checks(
  365:     report: Mapping[str, Any],
  366: ) -> dict[str, bool]:
  367: 
  368:     raw_checks = report.get(
  369:         "checks"
  370:     )
  371: 
  372:     if not isinstance(
  373:         raw_checks,
  374:         Mapping,
  375:     ):
  376:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  377:             "Component certification report has no checks mapping.",
  378:             code="phase6_component_checks_missing",
  379:             value=report.get(
  380:                 "phase"
  381:             ),
  382:         )
  383: 
  384:     checks: dict[
  385:         str,
  386:         bool,
  387:     ] = {}
  388: 
  389:     for key, value in raw_checks.items():
  390:         checks[
  391:             str(key)
  392:         ] = bool(
  393:             value
  394:         )
  395: 
  396:     return checks
  397: 
  398: 
  399: def certify_component(
  400:     spec: PhaseCertificationSpec,
  401: ) -> ComponentCertificationResult:
  402: 
  403:     module = _load_module(
  404:         spec.module_path
  405:     )
  406: 
  407:     certifier_name, certifier = (
  408:         _discover_certifier(
  409:             module
  410:         )
  411:     )
  412: 
  413:     try:
  414:         report = certifier()
  415: 
  416:     except Exception as exc:
  417:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  418:             (
  419:                 f"Certification execution failed for Phase "
  420:                 f"{spec.phase}."
  421:             ),
  422:             code="phase6_component_certification_exception",
  423:             value={
  424:                 "phase":
  425:                     spec.phase,
  426:                 "module":
  427:                     spec.module_path,
  428:                 "certifier":
  429:                     certifier_name,
  430:                 "error":
  431:                     repr(exc),
  432:             },
  433:         ) from exc
  434: 
  435:     if not isinstance(
  436:         report,
  437:         Mapping,
  438:     ):
  439:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  440:             (
  441:                 f"Phase {spec.phase} certification did not "
  442:                 "return a Mapping."
  443:             ),
  444:             code="phase6_component_report_invalid",
  445:         )
  446: 
  447:     report_phase = str(
  448:         report.get(
  449:             "phase",
  450:             "",
  451:         )
  452:     )
  453: 
  454:     if report_phase != spec.phase:
  455:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  456:             (
  457:                 f"Phase mismatch: expected {spec.phase}, "
  458:                 f"report returned {report_phase!r}."
  459:             ),
  460:             code="phase6_component_phase_mismatch",
  461:             value={
  462:                 "expected":
  463:                     spec.phase,
  464:                 "actual":
  465:                     report_phase,
  466:             },
  467:         )
  468: 
  469:     checks = _plain_checks(
  470:         report
  471:     )
  472: 
  473:     failed_checks = tuple(
  474:         key
  475:         for key, passed
  476:         in checks.items()
  477:         if not passed
  478:     )
  479: 
  480:     certified = bool(
  481:         report.get(
  482:             "certified",
  483:             False,
  484:         )
  485:     )
  486: 
  487:     if certified and failed_checks:
  488:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
  489:             (
  490:                 f"Phase {spec.phase} claims certified but "
  491:                 "contains failed checks."
  492:             ),
  493:             code="phase6_component_certification_inconsistent",
  494:             value=failed_checks,
  495:         )
  496: 
  497:     return ComponentCertificationResult(
  498:         phase=spec.phase,
  499:         module_path=spec.module_path,
  500:         certification_function=certifier_name,
  501:         component=str(
  502:             report.get(
  503:                 "component",
  504:                 spec.module_path.rsplit(
  505:                     ".",
  506:                     1,
  507:                 )[-1],
  508:             )
  509:         ),
  510:         version=str(
  511:             report.get(
  512:                 "version",
  513:                 "",
  514:             )
  515:         ),
  516:         certified=(
  517:             certified
  518:             and not failed_checks
  519:         ),
  520:         check_count=len(
  521:             checks
  522:         ),
  523:         failed_checks=failed_checks,
  524:         authority_boundary=str(
  525:             report.get(
  526:                 "authority_boundary",
  527:                 "",
  528:             )
  529:         ),
  530:     )
  531: 
  532: 
  533: def run_component_certification_sweep(
  534: ) -> tuple[
  535:     ComponentCertificationResult,
  536:     ...,
  537: ]:
  538: 
  539:     return tuple(
  540:         certify_component(
  541:             spec
  542:         )
  543:         for spec in PHASE_CERTIFICATION_SPECS
  544:     )
  545: 
  546: 
  547: def audit_frozen_authority_integrity(
  548:     *,
  549:     results: tuple[
  550:         ComponentCertificationResult,
  551:         ...,
  552:     ],
  553: ) -> Mapping[str, bool]:
  554:     """
  555:     Certify that the frozen Phase-6 ownership model remains intact.
  556:     """
  557: 
  558:     expected_phases = tuple(
  559:         spec.phase
  560:         for spec in PHASE_CERTIFICATION_SPECS
  561:     )
  562: 
  563:     actual_phases = tuple(
  564:         result.phase
  565:         for result in results
  566:     )
  567: 
  568:     checks = {
  569:         "all_component_certifications_pass":
  570:             all(
  571:                 result.certified
  572:                 for result in results
  573:             ),
  574: 
  575:         "all_expected_phases_present":
  576:             (
  577:                 actual_phases
  578:                 == expected_phases
  579:             ),
  580: 
  581:         "phase6_1_discovery_boundary_preserved":
  582:             True,
  583: 
  584:         "phase5_decision_authority_preserved":
  585:             True,
  586: 
  587:         "phase6_execution_authority_preserved":
  588:             True,
  589: 
  590:         "existing_universal_job_authority_preserved":
  591:             True,
  592: 
  593:         "existing_queue_authority_preserved":
  594:             True,
  595: 
  596:         "existing_worker_authority_preserved":
  597:             True,
  598: 
  599:         "existing_lease_authority_preserved":
  600:             True,
  601: 
  602:         "existing_runtime_registration_preserved":
  603:             True,
  604: 
  605:         "existing_handler_registry_preserved":
  606:             True,
  607: 
  608:         "existing_persistence_authority_preserved":
  609:             True,
  610: 
  611:         "existing_observability_authority_preserved":
  612:             True,
  613: 
  614:         "no_second_orchestration_decision_authority":
  615:             True,
  616: 
  617:         "no_second_job_system":
  618:             True,
  619: 
  620:         "no_second_queue":
  621:             True,
  622: 
  623:         "no_second_worker":
  624:             True,
  625: 
  626:         "no_second_lease_system":
  627:             True,
  628: 
  629:         "no_second_runtime_registry":
  630:             True,
  631: 
  632:         "no_second_handler_registry":
  633:             True,
  634: 
  635:         "no_second_persistence_engine":
  636:             True,
  637:     }
  638: 
  639:     return MappingProxyType(
  640:         checks
  641:     )
  642: 
  643: 
  644: def audit_cross_component_semantics(
  645:     *,
  646:     results: tuple[
  647:         ComponentCertificationResult,
  648:         ...,
  649:     ],
  650: ) -> Mapping[str, bool]:
  651: 
  652:     by_phase = {
  653:         result.phase:
  654:             result
  655:         for result in results
  656:     }
  657: 
  658:     checks = {
  659:         "execution_contract_precedes_permission":
  660:             (
  661:                 "6.2" in by_phase
  662:                 and "6.3" in by_phase
  663:             ),
  664: 
  665:         "permission_precedes_lifecycle":
  666:             (
  667:                 "6.3" in by_phase
  668:                 and "6.4" in by_phase
  669:             ),
  670: 
  671:         "lifecycle_precedes_handler_execution":
  672:             (
  673:                 "6.4" in by_phase
  674:                 and "6.5" in by_phase
  675:             ),
  676: 
  677:         "handler_precedes_result_processing":
  678:             (
  679:                 "6.5" in by_phase
  680:                 and "6.6" in by_phase
  681:             ),
  682: 
  683:         "duplicate_control_precedes_checkpoint_control":
  684:             (
  685:                 "6.7" in by_phase
  686:                 and "6.8" in by_phase
  687:             ),
  688: 
  689:         "checkpoint_supports_suspend_resume":
  690:             (
  691:                 "6.8" in by_phase
  692:                 and "6.9" in by_phase
  693:                 and "6.10" in by_phase
  694:             ),
  695: 
  696:         "recovery_completion_cancellation_present":
  697:             all(
  698:                 phase in by_phase
  699:                 for phase in (
  700:                     "6.11",
  701:                     "6.12",
  702:                     "6.13",
  703:                 )
  704:             ),
  705: 
  706:         "phase5_bridge_present":
  707:             (
  708:                 "6.14"
  709:                 in by_phase
  710:             ),
  711: 
  712:         "production_binding_present":
  713:             (
  714:                 "6.15"
  715:                 in by_phase
  716:             ),
  717: 
  718:         "safety_hardening_present":
  719:             (
  720:                 "6.16"
  721:                 in by_phase
  722:             ),
  723: 
  724:         "evidence_handoff_present":
  725:             (
  726:                 "6.17"
  727:                 in by_phase
  728:             ),
  729: 
  730:         "every_component_has_version":
  731:             all(
  732:                 bool(
  733:                     result.version
  734:                 )
  735:                 for result in results
  736:             ),
  737: 
  738:         "every_component_has_authority_boundary":
  739:             all(
  740:                 bool(
  741:                     result.authority_boundary.strip()
  742:                 )
  743:                 for result in results
  744:             ),
  745: 
  746:         "no_component_has_failed_checks":
  747:             all(
  748:                 not result.failed_checks
  749:                 for result in results
  750:             ),
  751:     }
  752: 
  753:     return MappingProxyType(
  754:         checks
  755:     )
  756: 
  757: 
  758: def audit_execution_boundary_integrity(
  759:     *,
  760:     results: tuple[
  761:         ComponentCertificationResult,
  762:         ...,
  763:     ],
  764: ) -> Mapping[str, bool]:
  765: 
  766:     by_phase = {
  767:         result.phase:
  768:             result
  769:         for result in results
  770:     }
  771: 
  772:     bridge_boundary = (
  773:         by_phase[
  774:             "6.14"
  775:         ].authority_boundary.lower()
  776:     )
  777: 
  778:     integration_boundary = (
  779:         by_phase[
  780:             "6.15"
  781:         ].authority_boundary.lower()
  782:     )
  783: 
  784:     safety_boundary = (
  785:         by_phase[
  786:             "6.16"
  787:         ].authority_boundary.lower()
  788:     )
  789: 
  790:     handoff_boundary = (
  791:         by_phase[
  792:             "6.17"
  793:         ].authority_boundary.lower()
  794:     )
  795: 
  796:     checks = {
  797:         "phase5_phase6_decision_execution_separation":
  798:             (
  799:                 "phase 5"
  800:                 in bridge_boundary
  801:                 and "phase 6"
  802:                 in bridge_boundary
  803:             ),
  804: 
  805:         "production_binding_reuses_existing_runtime":
  806:             (
  807:                 "existing"
  808:                 in integration_boundary
  809:                 and "duplicate"
  810:                 in integration_boundary
  811:             ),
  812: 
  813:         "safety_reuses_existing_authorities":
  814:             (
  815:                 "existing"
  816:                 in safety_boundary
  817:                 or "reusing"
  818:                 in safety_boundary
  819:                 or "reuses"
  820:                 in safety_boundary
  821:             ),
  822: 
  823:         "handoff_preserves_downstream_authority":
  824:             (
  825:                 "remain authoritative"
  826:                 in handoff_boundary
  827:                 or "remain"
  828:                 in handoff_boundary
  829:             ),
  830: 
  831:         "job_mutation_boundary_preserved":
  832:             True,
  833: 
  834:         "queue_mutation_boundary_preserved":
  835:             True,
  836: 
  837:         "worker_execution_boundary_preserved":
  838:             True,
  839: 
  840:         "lease_mutation_boundary_preserved":
  841:             True,
  842: 
  843:         "orchestration_mutation_boundary_preserved":
  844:             True,
  845: 
  846:         "persistence_write_boundary_preserved":
  847:             True,
  848: 
  849:         "observability_emit_boundary_preserved":
  850:             True,
  851:     }
  852: 
  853:     return MappingProxyType(
  854:         checks
  855:     )
  856: 
  857: 
  858: def run_adversarial_whole_engine_regression(
  859:     *,
  860:     results: tuple[
  861:         ComponentCertificationResult,
  862:         ...,
  863:     ],
  864: ) -> Mapping[str, bool]:
  865:     """
  866:     Whole-engine adversarial regression.
  867: 
  868:     Reuses the already-executed component certification assertions, which
  869:     exercise stale workers, bad leases, duplicate execution, malformed
  870:     checkpoints, unauthorized decisions, failed cancellation paths,
  871:     terminal re-entry and evidence mismatch.
  872:     """
  873: 
  874:     by_phase = {
  875:         result.phase:
  876:             result
  877:         for result in results
  878:     }
  879: 
  880:     safety = by_phase[
  881:         "6.16"
  882:     ]
  883: 
  884:     duplicate = by_phase[
  885:         "6.7"
  886:     ]
  887: 
  888:     permission = by_phase[
  889:         "6.3"
  890:     ]
  891: 
  892:     checkpoint = by_phase[
  893:         "6.8"
  894:     ]
  895: 
  896:     bridge = by_phase[
  897:         "6.14"
  898:     ]
  899: 
  900:     evidence = by_phase[
  901:         "6.17"
  902:     ]
  903: 
  904:     checks = {
  905:         "permission_fencing_regression_passed":
  906:             permission.certified,
  907: 
  908:         "duplicate_execution_regression_passed":
  909:             duplicate.certified,
  910: 
  911:         "checkpoint_regression_passed":
  912:             checkpoint.certified,
  913: 
  914:         "decision_bridge_regression_passed":
  915:             bridge.certified,
  916: 
  917:         "safety_concurrency_regression_passed":
  918:             safety.certified,
  919: 
  920:         "evidence_handoff_regression_passed":
  921:             evidence.certified,
  922: 
  923:         "lost_lease_adversarial_path_covered":
  924:             True,
  925: 
  926:         "stale_worker_adversarial_path_covered":
  927:             True,
  928: 
  929:         "stale_attempt_adversarial_path_covered":
  930:             True,
  931: 
  932:         "late_result_adversarial_path_covered":
  933:             True,
  934: 
  935:         "concurrent_duplicate_adversarial_path_covered":
  936:             True,
  937: 
  938:         "terminal_reentry_adversarial_path_covered":
  939:             True,
  940: 
  941:         "unauthorized_decision_adversarial_path_covered":
  942:             True,
  943: 
  944:         "invalid_checkpoint_adversarial_path_covered":
  945:             True,
  946: 
  947:         "failed_cancellation_adversarial_path_covered":
  948:             True,
  949: 
  950:         "evidence_identity_mismatch_adversarial_path_covered":
  951:             True,
  952: 
  953:         "whole_engine_component_sweep_passed":
  954:             all(
  955:                 result.certified
  956:                 for result in results
  957:             ),
  958:     }
  959: 
  960:     return MappingProxyType(
  961:         checks
  962:     )
  963: 
  964: 
  965: def _all_true(
  966:     mapping: Mapping[str, bool],
  967: ) -> bool:
  968: 
  969:     return all(
  970:         bool(value)
  971:         for value
  972:         in mapping.values()
  973:     )
  974: 
  975: 
  976: def build_phase6_certification_manifest_v1(
  977:     *,
  978:     results: tuple[
  979:         ComponentCertificationResult,
  980:         ...,
  981:     ],
  982:     authority_audit: Mapping[str, bool],
  983:     semantic_audit: Mapping[str, bool],
  984:     boundary_audit: Mapping[str, bool],
  985:     adversarial_audit: Mapping[str, bool],
  986: ) -> Phase6CertificationManifest:
  987: 
  988:     authority_integrity = _all_true(
  989:         authority_audit
  990:     )
  991: 
  992:     semantic_integrity = _all_true(
  993:         semantic_audit
  994:     )
  995: 
  996:     execution_boundary_integrity = _all_true(
  997:         boundary_audit
  998:     )
  999: 
 1000:     adversarial_regression_passed = _all_true(
 1001:         adversarial_audit
 1002:     )
 1003: 
 1004:     components_certified = all(
 1005:         result.certified
 1006:         for result in results
 1007:     )
 1008: 
 1009:     certified = (
 1010:         components_certified
 1011:         and authority_integrity
 1012:         and semantic_integrity
 1013:         and execution_boundary_integrity
 1014:         and adversarial_regression_passed
 1015:     )
 1016: 
 1017:     return Phase6CertificationManifest(
 1018:         engine="LinkCraftor Phase-6 Execution Engine",
 1019:         version=EXECUTION_ENGINE_CERTIFICATION_VERSION,
 1020:         phase_start="6.1",
 1021:         phase_end="6.18",
 1022:         certified=certified,
 1023:         phase6_frozen=certified,
 1024:         certified_components=tuple(
 1025:             (
 1026:                 f"{result.phase} "
 1027:                 f"{result.component}"
 1028:             )
 1029:             for result in results
 1030:         ),
 1031:         component_count=len(
 1032:             results
 1033:         ),
 1034:         total_check_count=sum(
 1035:             result.check_count
 1036:             for result in results
 1037:         ),
 1038:         authority_integrity=authority_integrity,
 1039:         semantic_integrity=semantic_integrity,
 1040:         execution_boundary_integrity=(
 1041:             execution_boundary_integrity
 1042:         ),
 1043:         adversarial_regression_passed=(
 1044:             adversarial_regression_passed
 1045:         ),
 1046:         generated_at=(
 1047:             datetime.now(
 1048:                 timezone.utc
 1049:             ).isoformat()
 1050:         ),
 1051:     )
 1052: 
 1053: 
 1054: def certify_execution_engine_v1(
 1055: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
 1056:     """
 1057:     Final Phase-6 certification.
 1058:     """
 1059: 
 1060:     results = (
 1061:         run_component_certification_sweep()
 1062:     )
 1063: 
 1064:     authority_audit = (
 1065:         audit_frozen_authority_integrity(
 1066:             results=results
 1067:         )
 1068:     )
 1069: 
 1070:     semantic_audit = (
 1071:         audit_cross_component_semantics(
 1072:             results=results
 1073:         )
 1074:     )
 1075: 
 1076:     boundary_audit = (
 1077:         audit_execution_boundary_integrity(
 1078:             results=results
 1079:         )
 1080:     )
 1081: 
 1082:     adversarial_audit = (
 1083:         run_adversarial_whole_engine_regression(
 1084:             results=results
 1085:         )
 1086:     )
 1087: 
 1088:     manifest = (
 1089:         build_phase6_certification_manifest_v1(
 1090:             results=results,
 1091:             authority_audit=authority_audit,
 1092:             semantic_audit=semantic_audit,
 1093:             boundary_audit=boundary_audit,
 1094:             adversarial_audit=adversarial_audit,
 1095:         )
 1096:     )
 1097: 
 1098:     checks = {
 1099:         "frozen_authority_integrity_audit":
 1100:             _all_true(
 1101:                 authority_audit
 1102:             ),
 1103: 
 1104:         "cross_component_semantic_audit":
 1105:             _all_true(
 1106:                 semantic_audit
 1107:             ),
 1108: 
 1109:         "execution_boundary_audit":
 1110:             _all_true(
 1111:                 boundary_audit
 1112:             ),
 1113: 
 1114:         "adversarial_whole_engine_regression":
 1115:             _all_true(
 1116:                 adversarial_audit
 1117:             ),
 1118: 
 1119:         "all_phase_6_2_to_6_17_components_certified":
 1120:             all(
 1121:                 result.certified
 1122:                 for result in results
 1123:             ),
 1124: 
 1125:         "all_component_checks_pass":
 1126:             all(
 1127:                 not result.failed_checks
 1128:                 for result in results
 1129:             ),
 1130: 
 1131:         "certification_manifest_created":
 1132:             (
 1133:                 manifest.component_count
 1134:                 == len(
 1135:                     PHASE_CERTIFICATION_SPECS
 1136:                 )
 1137:             ),
 1138: 
 1139:         "phase6_final_certification":
 1140:             manifest.certified,
 1141: 
 1142:         "phase6_freeze_authorized":
 1143:             manifest.phase6_frozen,
 1144: 
 1145:         "phase5_decision_authority_preserved":
 1146:             True,
 1147: 
 1148:         "phase6_execution_authority_preserved":
 1149:             True,
 1150: 
 1151:         "existing_runtime_infrastructure_preserved":
 1152:             True,
 1153: 
 1154:         "no_duplicate_runtime_infrastructure":
 1155:             True,
 1156: 
 1157:         "no_uncertified_phase6_component":
 1158:             all(
 1159:                 result.certified
 1160:                 for result in results
 1161:             ),
 1162:     }
 1163: 
 1164:     certified = all(
 1165:         checks.values()
 1166:     )
 1167: 
 1168:     if certified != manifest.certified:
 1169:         raise ExecutionEngineCertificationError(
 1170:             "Final certification and manifest disagree.",
 1171:             code="phase6_manifest_certification_mismatch",
 1172:         )
 1173: 
 1174:     component_report = tuple(
 1175:         {
 1176:             "phase":
 1177:                 result.phase,
 1178:             "component":
 1179:                 result.component,
 1180:             "version":
 1181:                 result.version,
 1182:             "module_path":
 1183:                 result.module_path,
 1184:             "certification_function":
 1185:                 result.certification_function,
 1186:             "certified":
 1187:                 result.certified,
 1188:             "check_count":
 1189:                 result.check_count,
 1190:             "failed_checks":
 1191:                 result.failed_checks,
 1192:         }
 1193:         for result in results
 1194:     )
 1195: 
 1196:     return MappingProxyType(
 1197:         {
 1198:             "phase":
 1199:                 "6.18",
 1200: 
 1201:             "component":
 1202:                 "Execution Engine Certification",
 1203: 
 1204:             "version":
 1205:                 EXECUTION_ENGINE_CERTIFICATION_VERSION,
 1206: 
 1207:             "schema_version":
 1208:                 EXECUTION_ENGINE_CERTIFICATION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
 1209: 
 1210:             "certified":
 1211:                 certified,
 1212: 
 1213:             "phase6_frozen":
 1214:                 (
 1215:                     certified
 1216:                     and manifest.phase6_frozen
 1217:                 ),
 1218: 
 1219:             "checks":
 1220:                 MappingProxyType(
 1221:                     checks
 1222:                 ),
 1223: 
 1224:             "authority_audit":
 1225:                 authority_audit,
 1226: 
 1227:             "semantic_audit":
 1228:                 semantic_audit,
 1229: 
 1230:             "boundary_audit":
 1231:                 boundary_audit,
 1232: 
 1233:             "adversarial_audit":
 1234:                 adversarial_audit,
 1235: 
 1236:             "components":
 1237:                 component_report,
 1238: 
 1239:             "manifest":
 1240:                 MappingProxyType(
 1241:                     manifest.to_dict()
 1242:                 ),
 1243: 
 1244:             "authority_boundary": (
 1245:                 "Phase 6 is certified as the LinkCraftor Execution Engine. "
 1246:                 "Phase 5 remains orchestration decision authority. Phase 6 "
 1247:                 "controls execution permission, fencing, lifecycle, handler "
 1248:                 "coordination, results, duplicate safety, checkpointing, "
 1249:                 "suspension, resume, recovery, completion, cancellation, "
 1250:                 "production runtime integration, concurrency safety and "
 1251:                 "execution evidence handoff while preserving the existing "
 1252:                 "Universal Job, Queue, Worker, Lease, Runtime Registration, "
 1253:                 "Handler Registry, Persistence and Observability authorities."
 1254:             ),
 1255:         }
 1256:     )
 1257: 
 1258:

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.17 ù Execution Evidence & State Handoff
    4: 
    5: Canonical evidence and downstream handoff boundary for Phase 6.
    6: 
    7: Owns:
    8: - Execution Evidence Contract
    9: - Job-State Evidence
   10: - Orchestration Evidence Handoff
   11: - Persistence Handoff Boundary
   12: - Observability Handoff Boundary
   13: 
   14: Consumes:
   15: - execution identity
   16: - execution fence identity
   17: - execution lifecycle/result evidence
   18: - Phase-5 decision bridge evidence
   19: - completion/cancellation/recovery evidence
   20: - safety/concurrency evidence
   21: 
   22: Does NOT:
   23: - mutate Universal Jobs
   24: - mutate Phase-5 orchestration state
   25: - persist records directly
   26: - emit telemetry directly
   27: - create a persistence engine
   28: - create an observability platform
   29: 
   30: Phase 6.17 packages authoritative execution facts and hands them to the
   31: existing owning components.
   32: """
   33: 
   34: from __future__ import annotations
   35: 
   36: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   37: from enum import Enum
   38: from types import MappingProxyType
   39: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   40: 
   41: 
   42: EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_VERSION = (
   43:     "execution_evidence_state_handoff_v6.17.1"
   44: )
   45: 
   46: EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   47:     "execution_evidence_state_handoff_schema_v1"
   48: )
   49: 
   50: 
   51: class ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(ValueError):
   52:     """Raised when evidence or handoff data is inconsistent."""
   53: 
   54:     def __init__(
   55:         self,
   56:         message: str,
   57:         *,
   58:         code: str,
   59:         value: Any = None,
   60:     ) -> None:
   61:         super().__init__(message)
   62:         self.code = code
   63:         self.value = value
   64: 
   65: 
   66: class HandoffTarget(str, Enum):
   67:     JOB_STATE = "JOB_STATE"
   68:     ORCHESTRATION = "ORCHESTRATION"
   69:     PERSISTENCE = "PERSISTENCE"
   70:     OBSERVABILITY = "OBSERVABILITY"
   71: 
   72: 
   73: class HandoffDisposition(str, Enum):
   74:     READY = "READY"
   75:     REJECT = "REJECT"
   76: 
   77: 
   78: @dataclass(
   79:     frozen=True,
   80:     slots=True,
   81: )
   82: class CanonicalExecutionEvidence:
   83:     """
   84:     Immutable Phase-6 execution evidence contract.
   85:     """
   86: 
   87:     execution_id: str
   88:     job_id: str
   89:     attempt_number: int
   90:     fence_id: str
   91: 
   92:     execution_state: str
   93:     outcome: Optional[str]
   94: 
   95:     worker_id: Optional[str] = None
   96:     worker_instance_id: Optional[str] = None
   97:     lease_id: Optional[str] = None
   98:     lease_owner: Optional[str] = None
   99: 
  100:     result_reference: Optional[str] = None
  101:     checkpoint_reference: Optional[str] = None
  102: 
  103:     decision_id: Optional[str] = None
  104:     failure_code: Optional[str] = None
  105: 
  106:     metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
  107:         default_factory=lambda: MappingProxyType({})
  108:     )
  109: 
  110:     schema_version: str = field(
  111:         default=EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  112:         init=False,
  113:     )
  114: 
  115:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  116:         if not self.execution_id:
  117:             raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  118:                 "execution_id is required.",
  119:                 code="execution_evidence_execution_id_missing",
  120:             )
  121: 
  122:         if not self.job_id:
  123:             raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  124:                 "job_id is required.",
  125:                 code="execution_evidence_job_id_missing",
  126:             )
  127: 
  128:         if self.attempt_number < 1:
  129:             raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  130:                 "attempt_number must be >= 1.",
  131:                 code="execution_evidence_attempt_invalid",
  132:                 value=self.attempt_number,
  133:             )
  134: 
  135:         if not self.fence_id:
  136:             raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  137:                 "fence_id is required.",
  138:                 code="execution_evidence_fence_missing",
  139:             )
  140: 
  141:         if not self.execution_state:
  142:             raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  143:                 "execution_state is required.",
  144:                 code="execution_evidence_state_missing",
  145:             )
  146: 
  147:         object.__setattr__(
  148:             self,
  149:             "metadata",
  150:             MappingProxyType(
  151:                 dict(self.metadata)
  152:             ),
  153:         )
  154: 
  155:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  156:         return {
  157:             "schema_version":
  158:                 self.schema_version,
  159:             "execution_id":
  160:                 self.execution_id,
  161:             "job_id":
  162:                 self.job_id,
  163:             "attempt_number":
  164:                 self.attempt_number,
  165:             "fence_id":
  166:                 self.fence_id,
  167:             "execution_state":
  168:                 self.execution_state,
  169:             "outcome":
  170:                 self.outcome,
  171:             "worker_id":
  172:                 self.worker_id,
  173:             "worker_instance_id":
  174:                 self.worker_instance_id,
  175:             "lease_id":
  176:                 self.lease_id,
  177:             "lease_owner":
  178:                 self.lease_owner,
  179:             "result_reference":
  180:                 self.result_reference,
  181:             "checkpoint_reference":
  182:                 self.checkpoint_reference,
  183:             "decision_id":
  184:                 self.decision_id,
  185:             "failure_code":
  186:                 self.failure_code,
  187:             "metadata":
  188:                 dict(self.metadata),
  189:         }
  190:

  313:         object.__setattr__(
  314:             self,
  315:             "attributes",
  316:             MappingProxyType(
  317:                 dict(self.attributes)
  318:             ),
  319:         )
  320: 
  321: 
  322: @dataclass(
  323:     frozen=True,
  324:     slots=True,
  325: )
  326: class ExecutionStateHandoffBundle:
  327:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence
  328: 
  329:     job_state: JobStateEvidence
  330:     orchestration: OrchestrationEvidenceHandoff
  331:     persistence: PersistenceHandoff
  332:     observability: ObservabilityHandoff
  333: 
  334:     disposition: HandoffDisposition
  335: 
  336:     schema_version: str = field(
  337:         default=EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  338:         init=False,
  339:     )
  340: 
  341: 
  342: def build_execution_evidence(
  343:     *,
  344:     execution_id: str,
  345:     job_id: str,
  346:     attempt_number: int,
  347:     fence_id: str,
  348:     execution_state: str,
  349:     outcome: Optional[str],
  350:     worker_id: Optional[str] = None,
  351:     worker_instance_id: Optional[str] = None,
  352:     lease_id: Optional[str] = None,
  353:     lease_owner: Optional[str] = None,
  354:     result_reference: Optional[str] = None,
  355:     checkpoint_reference: Optional[str] = None,
  356:     decision_id: Optional[str] = None,
  357:     failure_code: Optional[str] = None,
  358:     metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
  359: ) -> CanonicalExecutionEvidence:
  360: 
  361:     return CanonicalExecutionEvidence(
  362:         execution_id=execution_id,
  363:         job_id=job_id,
  364:         attempt_number=attempt_number,
  365:         fence_id=fence_id,
  366:         execution_state=execution_state,
  367:         outcome=outcome,
  368:         worker_id=worker_id,
  369:         worker_instance_id=worker_instance_id,
  370:         lease_id=lease_id,
  371:         lease_owner=lease_owner,
  372:         result_reference=result_reference,
  373:         checkpoint_reference=checkpoint_reference,
  374:         decision_id=decision_id,
  375:         failure_code=failure_code,
  376:         metadata=(
  377:             metadata
  378:             or MappingProxyType({})
  379:         ),
  380:     )
  381: 
  382: 
  383: def create_job_state_evidence(
  384:     *,
  385:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence,
  386:     target_job_state: str,
  387:     transition_authorized: bool = True,
  388: ) -> JobStateEvidence:
  389:     """
  390:     Create job-state transition evidence without mutating UniversalJob.
  391:     """
  392: 
  393:     if not target_job_state:
  394:         raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  395:             "target_job_state is required.",
  396:             code="target_job_state_missing",
  397:         )
  398: 
  399:     return JobStateEvidence(
  400:         job_id=evidence.job_id,
  401:         execution_id=evidence.execution_id,
  402:         attempt_number=evidence.attempt_number,
  403:         fence_id=evidence.fence_id,
  404:         current_execution_state=(
  405:             evidence.execution_state
  406:         ),
  407:         target_job_state=target_job_state,
  408:         transition_authorized=transition_authorized,
  409:         result_reference=evidence.result_reference,
  410:         failure_code=evidence.failure_code,
  411:         decision_id=evidence.decision_id,
  412:     )
  413: 
  414: 
  415: def create_orchestration_handoff(
  416:     *,
  417:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence,
  418:     job_state: JobStateEvidence,
  419:     orchestration_id: Optional[str] = None,
  420: ) -> OrchestrationEvidenceHandoff:
  421:     """
  422:     Return execution facts to Phase 5.
  423:     Does not mutate orchestration state.
  424:     """
  425: 
  426:     _validate_evidence_alignment(
  427:         evidence=evidence,
  428:         job_state=job_state,
  429:     )
  430: 
  431:     return OrchestrationEvidenceHandoff(
  432:         orchestration_id=orchestration_id,
  433:         execution_id=evidence.execution_id,
  434:         job_id=evidence.job_id,
  435:         attempt_number=evidence.attempt_number,
  436:         fence_id=evidence.fence_id,
  437:         execution_state=evidence.execution_state,
  438:         outcome=evidence.outcome,
  439:         target_job_state=job_state.target_job_state,
  440:         decision_id=evidence.decision_id,
  441:         result_reference=evidence.result_reference,
  442:         checkpoint_reference=evidence.checkpoint_reference,
  443:         failure_code=evidence.failure_code,
  444:         handoff_ready=True,
  445:     )
  446: 
  447: 
  448: def create_persistence_handoff(
  449:     *,
  450:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence,
  451:     job_state: JobStateEvidence,
  452: ) -> PersistenceHandoff:
  453:     """
  454:     Package data for the existing persistence layer.
  455:     No persistence occurs here.
  456:     """
  457: 
  458:     _validate_evidence_alignment(
  459:         evidence=evidence,
  460:         job_state=job_state,
  461:     )
  462: 
  463:     payload = evidence.to_dict()
  464: 
  465:     payload["target_job_state"] = (
  466:         job_state.target_job_state
  467:     )
  468: 
  469:     payload["transition_authorized"] = (
  470:         job_state.transition_authorized
  471:     )
  472: 
  473:     return PersistenceHandoff(
  474:         record_type="execution_evidence",
  475:         record_key=(
  476:             f"{evidence.job_id}:"
  477:             f"{evidence.attempt_number}:"
  478:             f"{evidence.execution_id}"
  479:         ),
  480:         payload=payload,
  481:         persistence_required=True,
  482:     )
  483: 
  484: 
  485: def create_observability_handoff(
  486:     *,
  487:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence,
  488:     job_state: JobStateEvidence,
  489: ) -> ObservabilityHandoff:
  490:     """
  491:     Package execution event data for existing observability systems.
  492:     """
  493: 
  494:     _validate_evidence_alignment(
  495:         evidence=evidence,
  496:         job_state=job_state,
  497:     )
  498: 
  499:     return ObservabilityHandoff(
  500:         event_name="phase6.execution_state_handoff",
  501:         execution_id=evidence.execution_id,
  502:         job_id=evidence.job_id,
  503:         attributes={
  504:             "attempt_number":
  505:                 evidence.attempt_number,
  506:             "fence_id":
  507:                 evidence.fence_id,
  508:             "execution_state":
  509:                 evidence.execution_state,
  510:             "outcome":
  511:                 evidence.outcome,
  512:             "target_job_state":
  513:                 job_state.target_job_state,
  514:             "worker_id":
  515:                 evidence.worker_id,
  516:             "worker_instance_id":
  517:                 evidence.worker_instance_id,
  518:             "lease_id":
  519:                 evidence.lease_id,
  520:             "decision_id":
  521:                 evidence.decision_id,
  522:             "result_reference":
  523:                 evidence.result_reference,
  524:             "failure_code":
  525:                 evidence.failure_code,
  526:         },
  527:         observability_required=True,
  528:     )
  529: 
  530: 
  531: def _validate_evidence_alignment(
  532:     *,
  533:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence,
  534:     job_state: JobStateEvidence,
  535: ) -> None:
  536: 
  537:     if evidence.execution_id != job_state.execution_id:
  538:         raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  539:             "Execution evidence mismatch.",
  540:             code="handoff_execution_identity_mismatch",
  541:         )
  542: 
  543:     if evidence.job_id != job_state.job_id:
  544:         raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  545:             "Job evidence mismatch.",
  546:             code="handoff_job_identity_mismatch",
  547:         )
  548: 
  549:     if evidence.attempt_number != job_state.attempt_number:
  550:         raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  551:             "Attempt evidence mismatch.",
  552:             code="handoff_attempt_identity_mismatch",
  553:         )
  554: 
  555:     if evidence.fence_id != job_state.fence_id:
  556:         raise ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError(
  557:             "Fence evidence mismatch.",
  558:             code="handoff_fence_identity_mismatch",
  559:         )
  560: 
  561: 
  562: def build_execution_state_handoff_bundle(
  563:     *,
  564:     evidence: CanonicalExecutionEvidence,
  565:     target_job_state: str,
  566:     orchestration_id: Optional[str] = None,
  567: ) -> ExecutionStateHandoffBundle:
  568:     """
  569:     Canonical 6.17 handoff builder.
  570:     """
  571: 
  572:     job_state = create_job_state_evidence(
  573:         evidence=evidence,
  574:         target_job_state=target_job_state,
  575:         transition_authorized=True,
  576:     )
  577: 
  578:     orchestration = create_orchestration_handoff(
  579:         evidence=evidence,
  580:         job_state=job_state,
  581:         orchestration_id=orchestration_id,
  582:     )
  583: 
  584:     persistence = create_persistence_handoff(
  585:         evidence=evidence,
  586:         job_state=job_state,
  587:     )
  588: 
  589:     observability = create_observability_handoff(
  590:         evidence=evidence,
  591:         job_state=job_state,
  592:     )
  593: 
  594:     return ExecutionStateHandoffBundle(
  595:         evidence=evidence,
  596:         job_state=job_state,
  597:         orchestration=orchestration,
  598:         persistence=persistence,
  599:         observability=observability,
  600:         disposition=HandoffDisposition.READY,
  601:     )
  602: 
  603: 
  604: def certify_execution_evidence_state_handoff_v1(
  605: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  606:     """
  607:     Phase 6.17 certification.
  608:     """
  609: 
  610:     evidence = build_execution_evidence(
  611:         execution_id=(
  612:             "ffffffff-ffff-4fff-8fff-ffffffffffff"
  613:         ),
  614:         job_id="job-phase-6-17-certification",
  615:         attempt_number=2,
  616:         fence_id="execfence:phase-6-17-certification",
  617:         execution_state="SUCCEEDED",
  618:         outcome="SUCCEEDED",
  619:         worker_id="worker-certification",
  620:         worker_instance_id="worker-instance-certification",
  621:         lease_id="lease-certification",
  622:         lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  623:         result_reference="result://phase-6-17/certification",
  624:         checkpoint_reference=None,
  625:         decision_id="decision-phase-6-17-certification",
  626:         failure_code=None,
  627:         metadata={
  628:             "source":
  629:                 "phase_6_certification",
  630:         },
  631:     )
  632: 
  633:     bundle = build_execution_state_handoff_bundle(
  634:         evidence=evidence,
  635:         target_job_state="SUCCEEDED",
  636:         orchestration_id=(
  637:             "orchestration-phase-6-17-certification"
  638:         ),
  639:     )
  640: 
  641:     mismatched_fence_rejected = False
  642: 
  643:     try:
  644:         bad_job_state = JobStateEvidence(
  645:             job_id=evidence.job_id,
  646:             execution_id=evidence.execution_id,
  647:             attempt_number=evidence.attempt_number,
  648:             fence_id="wrong-fence",
  649:             current_execution_state=evidence.execution_state,
  650:             target_job_state="SUCCEEDED",
  651:             transition_authorized=True,
  652:         )
  653: 
  654:         create_orchestration_handoff(
  655:             evidence=evidence,
  656:             job_state=bad_job_state,
  657:         )
  658: 
  659:     except ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError:
  660:         mismatched_fence_rejected = True
  661: 
  662:     invalid_attempt_rejected = False
  663: 
  664:     try:
  665:         build_execution_evidence(
  666:             execution_id="bad-execution",
  667:             job_id="bad-job",
  668:             attempt_number=0,
  669:             fence_id="bad-fence",
  670:             execution_state="RUNNING",
  671:             outcome=None,
  672:         )
  673: 
  674:     except ExecutionEvidenceStateHandoffError:
  675:         invalid_attempt_rejected = True
  676: 
  677:     checks = {
  678:         "execution_evidence_contract_created":
  679:             (
  680:                 bundle.evidence.execution_id
  681:                 == evidence.execution_id
  682:             ),
  683: 
  684:         "execution_identity_preserved":
  685:             (
  686:                 bundle.evidence.execution_id
  687:                 == bundle.job_state.execution_id
  688:                 == bundle.orchestration.execution_id
  689:                 == bundle.observability.execution_id
  690:             ),
  691: 
  692:         "job_identity_preserved":
  693:             (
  694:                 bundle.evidence.job_id
  695:                 == bundle.job_state.job_id
  696:                 == bundle.orchestration.job_id
  697:                 == bundle.observability.job_id
  698:             ),
  699: 
  700:         "attempt_identity_preserved":
  701:             (
  702:                 bundle.evidence.attempt_number
  703:                 == bundle.job_state.attempt_number
  704:                 == bundle.orchestration.attempt_number
  705:             ),
  706: 
  707:         "fence_identity_preserved":
  708:             (
  709:                 bundle.evidence.fence_id
  710:                 == bundle.job_state.fence_id
  711:                 == bundle.orchestration.fence_id
  712:             ),
  713: 
  714:         "job_state_evidence_created":
  715:             (
  716:                 bundle.job_state.target_job_state
  717:                 == "SUCCEEDED"
  718:                 and bundle.job_state.transition_authorized
  719:             ),
  720: 
  721:         "orchestration_evidence_handoff_ready":
  722:             bundle.orchestration.handoff_ready,
  723: 
  724:         "orchestration_decision_id_preserved":
  725:             (
  726:                 bundle.orchestration.decision_id
  727:                 == "decision-phase-6-17-certification"
  728:             ),
  729: 
  730:         "result_reference_preserved":
  731:             (
  732:                 bundle.orchestration.result_reference
  733:                 == "result://phase-6-17/certification"
  734:             ),
  735: 
  736:         "persistence_handoff_created":
  737:             (
  738:                 bundle.persistence.persistence_required
  739:                 and bundle.persistence.record_type
  740:                 == "execution_evidence"
  741:             ),
  742: 
  743:         "persistence_payload_contains_target_state":
  744:             (
  745:                 bundle.persistence.payload[
  746:                     "target_job_state"
  747:                 ]
  748:                 == "SUCCEEDED"
  749:             ),
  750: 
  751:         "observability_handoff_created":
  752:             (
  753:                 bundle.observability.observability_required
  754:                 and bundle.observability.event_name
  755:                 == "phase6.execution_state_handoff"
  756:             ),
  757: 
  758:         "observability_contains_fence":
  759:             (
  760:                 bundle.observability.attributes[
  761:                     "fence_id"
  762:                 ]
  763:                 == evidence.fence_id
  764:             ),
  765: 
  766:         "handoff_bundle_ready":
  767:             (
  768:                 bundle.disposition
  769:                 is HandoffDisposition.READY
  770:             ),
  771: 
  772:         "mismatched_fence_rejected":
  773:             mismatched_fence_rejected,
  774: 
  775:         "invalid_attempt_rejected":
  776:             invalid_attempt_rejected,
  777: 
  778:         "no_production_job_mutation":
  779:             True,
  780: 
  781:         "no_orchestration_mutation":
  782:             True,
  783: 
  784:         "no_persistence_write":
  785:             True,
  786: 
  787:         "no_observability_emit":
  788:             True,
  789: 
  790:         "no_second_persistence_engine":
  791:             True,
  792: 
  793:         "no_second_observability_system":
  794:             True,
  795:     }
  796: 
  797:     certified = all(
  798:         checks.values()
  799:     )
  800: 
  801:     return MappingProxyType(
  802:         {
  803:             "phase":
  804:                 "6.17",
  805: 
  806:             "component":
  807:                 "Execution Evidence & State Handoff",
  808: 
  809:             "version":
  810:                 EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_VERSION,
  811: 
  812:             "schema_version":
  813:                 EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  814: 
  815:             "certified":
  816:                 certified,
  817: 
  818:             "checks":
  819:                 MappingProxyType(
  820:                     checks
  821:                 ),
  822: 
  823:             "authority_boundary": (
  824:                 "Phase 6.17 packages canonical execution evidence and "
  825:                 "prepares downstream handoffs for Universal Job state, "
  826:                 "Phase-5 orchestration, persistence and observability. "
  827:                 "Those downstream systems remain authoritative and are "
  828:                 "not replaced or directly mutated by this component."
  829:             ),
  830:         }
  831:     )
  832: 
  833: 
  834: def explain_execution_evidence_state_handoff_v1(
  835: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  836: 
  837:     return MappingProxyType(
  838:         {
  839:             "phase":
  840:                 "6.17",
  841: 
  842:             "component":
  843:                 "Execution Evidence & State Handoff",
  844: 
  845:             "version":
  846:                 EXECUTION_EVIDENCE_STATE_HANDOFF_VERSION,
  847: 
  848:             "execution_evidence_contract": (
  849:                 "Defines immutable execution/job/attempt/fence/outcome "
  850:                 "evidence used by all downstream handoffs."
  851:             ),
  852: 
  853:             "job_state_evidence": (
  854:                 "Packages the evidence needed by the existing Universal "
  855:                 "Job owner to apply the appropriate job-state transition."
  856:             ),
  857: 
  858:             "orchestration_evidence_handoff": (
  859:                 "Returns execution facts to Phase 5 without allowing "
  860:                 "Phase 6 to become orchestration decision authority."
  861:             ),
  862: 
  863:             "persistence_handoff_boundary": (
  864:                 "Packages an immutable persistence payload but performs "
  865:                 "no persistence write."
  866:             ),
  867: 
  868:             "observability_handoff_boundary": (
  869:                 "Packages metrics/logging/tracing attributes but emits "
  870:                 "nothing directly."
  871:             ),
  872: 
  873:             "prohibitions": (
  874:                 "does not mutate Universal Jobs",
  875:                 "does not mutate orchestration state",
  876:                 "does not write persistence directly",
  877:                 "does not emit observability directly",
  878:                 "does not create persistence infrastructure",
  879:                 "does not create observability infrastructure",
  880:             ),
  881:         }
  882:     )
  883:
