    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.4 ù Execution Lifecycle Controller
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution lifecycle control.
    6: 
    7: This component consumes:
    8: - Phase 6.2 ExecutionRequest / ExecutionResult
    9: - Phase 6.3 ExecutionPermissionDecision / ExecutionFenceIdentity
   10: 
   11: It owns the Phase-6 execution lifecycle state machine.
   12: 
   13: It intentionally does NOT:
   14: - mutate the production Universal Job store
   15: - enqueue or dequeue jobs
   16: - acquire or release worker leases
   17: - invoke runtime handlers
   18: - persist lifecycle state
   19: - mutate Phase-5 orchestration state
   20: - decide retries
   21: - perform checkpoint I/O
   22: 
   23: Production job / queue / worker wiring is deferred to Phase 6.15.
   24: """
   25: 
   26: from __future__ import annotations
   27: 
   28: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   29: from enum import Enum
   30: from types import MappingProxyType
   31: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   32: 
   33: from .execution_contracts import (
   34:     ExecutionOutcome,
   35:     ExecutionRequest,
   36:     ExecutionResult,
   37: )
   38: 
   39: from .execution_permission_fencing import (
   40:     ExecutionFenceIdentity,
   41:     ExecutionPermissionDecision,
   42: )
   43: 
   44: 
   45: EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_VERSION = (
   46:     "execution_lifecycle_controller_v6.4.1"
   47: )
   48: 
   49: EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   50:     "execution_lifecycle_controller_schema_v1"
   51: )
   52: 
   53: 
   54: class ExecutionLifecycleError(ValueError):
   55:     """Raised when an execution lifecycle operation is invalid."""
   56: 
   57:     def __init__(
   58:         self,
   59:         message: str,
   60:         *,
   61:         code: str,
   62:         value: Any = None,
   63:     ) -> None:
   64:         super().__init__(message)
   65:         self.code = code
   66:         self.value = value
   67: 
   68: 
   69: def _optional_text(
   70:     value: Any,
   71:     *,
   72:     field_name: str,
   73: ) -> Optional[str]:
   74:     if value is None:
   75:         return None
   76: 
   77:     if not isinstance(value, str):
   78:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
   79:             f"{field_name} must be a string or None.",
   80:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}_type",
   81:             value=value,
   82:         )
   83: 
   84:     normalized = value.strip()
   85: 
   86:     return normalized or None
   87: 
   88: 
   89: class ExecutionLifecycleState(str, Enum):
   90:     """
   91:     Canonical Phase-6 execution lifecycle states.
   92: 
   93:     These are execution-engine lifecycle states, not direct persistence
   94:     mutations of UniversalJob.
   95:     """
   96: 
   97:     READY = "READY"
   98:     RUNNING = "RUNNING"
   99:     FINALIZING = "FINALIZING"
  100: 
  101:     SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
  102:     FAILED = "FAILED"
  103:     CANCELLED = "CANCELLED"
  104:     SUSPENDED = "SUSPENDED"
  105: 
  106: 
  107: _TERMINAL_EXECUTION_STATES = frozenset(
  108:     {
  109:         ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED,
  110:         ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED,
  111:         ExecutionLifecycleState.CANCELLED,
  112:         ExecutionLifecycleState.SUSPENDED,
  113:     }
  114: )
  115: 
  116: 
  117: @dataclass(
  118:     frozen=True,
  119:     slots=True,
  120: )
  121: class ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  122:     """
  123:     Immutable lifecycle representation for one execution attempt.
  124: 
  125:     Each transition creates a new record rather than mutating the previous
  126:     record in place.
  127:     """
  128: 
  129:     execution_id: str
  130:     job_id: str
  131:     attempt_number: int
  132: 
  133:     fence_id: str
  134:     state: ExecutionLifecycleState
  135: 
  136:     active: bool
  137: 
  138:     started_at: Optional[str] = None
  139:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None
  140: 
  141:     result_outcome: Optional[ExecutionOutcome] = None
  142: 
  143:     schema_version: str = field(
  144:         default=EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  145:         init=False,
  146:     )
  147: 
  148:     @property
  149:     def terminal(self) -> bool:
  150:         return self.state in _TERMINAL_EXECUTION_STATES
  151: 
  152:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  153:         return {
  154:             "schema_version":
  155:                 self.schema_version,
  156:             "execution_id":
  157:                 self.execution_id,
  158:             "job_id":
  159:                 self.job_id,
  160:             "attempt_number":
  161:                 self.attempt_number,
  162:             "fence_id":
  163:                 self.fence_id,
  164:             "state":
  165:                 self.state.value,
  166:             "active":
  167:                 self.active,
  168:             "started_at":
  169:                 self.started_at,
  170:             "finalized_at":
  171:                 self.finalized_at,
  172:             "result_outcome":
  173:                 (
  174:                     self.result_outcome.value
  175:                     if self.result_outcome is not None
  176:                     else None
  177:                 ),
  178:             "terminal":
  179:                 self.terminal,
  180:         }
  181: 
  182: 
  183: def validate_pre_execution(
  184:     *,
  185:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  186:     permission: ExecutionPermissionDecision,
  187: ) -> ExecutionFenceIdentity:
  188:     """
  189:     Pre-execution validation.
  190: 
  191:     Requires:
  192:     - valid ExecutionRequest
  193:     - ALLOW permission decision
  194:     - canonical fence
  195:     - request/fence identity agreement
  196:     """
  197: 
  198:     if not isinstance(
  199:         request,
  200:         ExecutionRequest,
  201:     ):
  202:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  203:             "request must be ExecutionRequest.",
  204:             code="invalid_execution_request",
  205:             value=request,
  206:         )
  207: 
  208:     if not isinstance(
  209:         permission,
  210:         ExecutionPermissionDecision,
  211:     ):
  212:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  213:             "permission must be ExecutionPermissionDecision.",
  214:             code="invalid_execution_permission",
  215:             value=permission,
  216:         )
  217: 
  218:     if not permission.allowed:
  219:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  220:             "Execution permission was denied.",
  221:             code="execution_permission_denied",
  222:             value=permission.reason.value,
  223:         )
  224: 
  225:     fence = permission.fence
  226: 
  227:     if fence is None:
  228:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  229:             "Allowed execution requires an execution fence.",
  230:             code="allowed_execution_missing_fence",
  231:         )
  232: 
  233:     if (
  234:         fence.execution_id
  235:         != request.identity.execution_id
  236:     ):
  237:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  238:             "Execution fence execution_id does not match request.",
  239:             code="execution_fence_execution_mismatch",
  240:             value=fence.execution_id,
  241:         )
  242: 
  243:     if fence.job_id != request.identity.job_id:
  244:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  245:             "Execution fence job_id does not match request.",
  246:             code="execution_fence_job_mismatch",
  247:             value=fence.job_id,
  248:         )
  249: 
  250:     if (
  251:         fence.attempt_number
  252:         != request.identity.attempt_number
  253:     ):
  254:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  255:             "Execution fence attempt_number does not match request.",
  256:             code="execution_fence_attempt_mismatch",
  257:             value=fence.attempt_number,
  258:         )
  259: 
  260:     return fence
  261: 
  262: 
  263: def create_execution_start(
  264:     *,
  265:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  266:     permission: ExecutionPermissionDecision,
  267: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  268:     """
  269:     Create the READY lifecycle record after successful pre-execution
  270:     validation.
  271: 
  272:     This does not invoke the runtime handler.
  273:     """
  274: 
  275:     fence = validate_pre_execution(
  276:         request=request,
  277:         permission=permission,
  278:     )
  279: 
  280:     return ExecutionLifecycleRecord(
  281:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  282:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  283:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  284:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  285:         state=ExecutionLifecycleState.READY,
  286:         active=False,
  287:     )
  288: 
  289: 
  290: def apply_running_state(
  291:     *,
  292:     record: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  293:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  294:     started_at: Optional[str] = None,
  295: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  296:     """
  297:     Apply the Phase-6 RUNNING lifecycle state.
  298: 
  299:     This is an execution-engine lifecycle transition only.
  300:     Production UniversalJob status mutation is deferred to integration.
  301:     """
  302: 
  303:     if not isinstance(
  304:         record,
  305:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  306:     ):
  307:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  308:             "record must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  309:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle_record",
  310:             value=record,
  311:         )
  312: 
  313:     if not isinstance(
  314:         fence,
  315:         ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  316:     ):
  317:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  318:             "fence must be ExecutionFenceIdentity.",
  319:             code="invalid_execution_fence",
  320:             value=fence,
  321:         )
  322: 
  323:     if record.state is not ExecutionLifecycleState.READY:
  324:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  325:             "Only READY execution may enter RUNNING.",
  326:             code="invalid_execution_start_state",
  327:             value=record.state.value,
  328:         )
  329: 
  330:     if record.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  331:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  332:             "Execution fence changed before RUNNING transition.",
  333:             code="execution_fence_changed",
  334:             value=fence.fence_id,
  335:         )
  336: 
  337:     canonical_started_at = _optional_text(
  338:         started_at,
  339:         field_name="started_at",
  340:     )
  341: 
  342:     return ExecutionLifecycleRecord(
  343:         execution_id=record.execution_id,
  344:         job_id=record.job_id,
  345:         attempt_number=record.attempt_number,
  346:         fence_id=record.fence_id,
  347:         state=ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING,
  348:         active=True,
  349:         started_at=canonical_started_at,
  350:     )
  351: 
  352: 
  353: def is_execution_active(
  354:     record: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  355: ) -> bool:
  356:     """
  357:     Canonical active-execution query.
  358:     """
  359: 
  360:     if not isinstance(
  361:         record,
  362:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  363:     ):
  364:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  365:             "record must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  366:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle_record",
  367:             value=record,
  368:         )
  369: 
  370:     return (
  371:         record.active
  372:         and record.state
  373:         is ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING
  374:     )
  375: 
  376: 
  377: def begin_execution_finalization(
  378:     *,
  379:     record: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  380:     result: ExecutionResult,
  381:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  382: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  383:     """
  384:     Enter FINALIZING after handler/result production.
  385: 
  386:     Result acceptance remains fence-bound.
  387:     """
  388: 
  389:     if not isinstance(
  390:         record,
  391:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  392:     ):
  393:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  394:             "record must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  395:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle_record",
  396:             value=record,
  397:         )
  398: 
  399:     if record.state is not ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING:
  400:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  401:             "Only RUNNING execution may begin finalization.",
  402:             code="invalid_finalization_source_state",
  403:             value=record.state.value,
  404:         )
  405: 
  406:     if not isinstance(
  407:         result,
  408:         ExecutionResult,
  409:     ):
  410:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  411:             "result must be ExecutionResult.",
  412:             code="invalid_execution_result",
  413:             value=result,
  414:         )
  415: 
  416:     if not isinstance(
  417:         fence,
  418:         ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  419:     ):
  420:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  421:             "fence must be ExecutionFenceIdentity.",
  422:             code="invalid_execution_fence",
  423:             value=fence,
  424:         )
  425: 
  426:     if record.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  427:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  428:             "Execution fence changed before finalization.",
  429:             code="execution_finalization_fence_mismatch",
  430:             value=fence.fence_id,
  431:         )
  432: 
  433:     if (
  434:         result.identity.execution_id
  435:         != record.execution_id
  436:     ):
  437:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  438:             "Result execution_id does not match active execution.",
  439:             code="execution_result_identity_mismatch",
  440:             value=result.identity.execution_id,
  441:         )
  442: 
  443:     if result.identity.job_id != record.job_id:
  444:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  445:             "Result job_id does not match active execution.",
  446:             code="execution_result_job_mismatch",
  447:             value=result.identity.job_id,
  448:         )
  449: 
  450:     if (
  451:         result.identity.attempt_number
  452:         != record.attempt_number
  453:     ):
  454:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  455:             "Result attempt_number does not match active execution.",
  456:             code="execution_result_attempt_mismatch",
  457:             value=result.identity.attempt_number,
  458:         )
  459: 
  460:     return ExecutionLifecycleRecord(
  461:         execution_id=record.execution_id,
  462:         job_id=record.job_id,
  463:         attempt_number=record.attempt_number,
  464:         fence_id=record.fence_id,
  465:         state=ExecutionLifecycleState.FINALIZING,
  466:         active=False,
  467:         started_at=record.started_at,
  468:         result_outcome=result.outcome,
  469:     )
  470: 
  471: 
  472: def _terminal_state_for_outcome(
  473:     outcome: ExecutionOutcome,
  474: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleState:
  475: 
  476:     if outcome is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED:
  477:         return ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED
  478: 
  479:     if outcome is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED:
  480:         return ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED
  481: 
  482:     if outcome is ExecutionOutcome.CANCELLED:
  483:         return ExecutionLifecycleState.CANCELLED
  484: 
  485:     if outcome is ExecutionOutcome.SUSPENDED:
  486:         return ExecutionLifecycleState.SUSPENDED
  487: 
  488:     raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  489:         "Unsupported execution outcome.",
  490:         code="unsupported_execution_outcome",
  491:         value=outcome,
  492:     )
  493: 
  494: 
  495: def apply_terminal_state(
  496:     *,
  497:     record: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  498:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  499: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  500:     """
  501:     Apply the canonical terminal lifecycle state.
  502: 
  503:     Does not persist or mutate UniversalJob.
  504:     """
  505: 
  506:     if not isinstance(
  507:         record,
  508:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  509:     ):
  510:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  511:             "record must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  512:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle_record",
  513:             value=record,
  514:         )
  515: 
  516:     if record.state is not ExecutionLifecycleState.FINALIZING:
  517:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  518:             "Only FINALIZING execution may enter terminal state.",
  519:             code="invalid_terminal_transition_source",
  520:             value=record.state.value,
  521:         )
  522: 
  523:     if record.result_outcome is None:
  524:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  525:             "FINALIZING execution requires result_outcome.",
  526:             code="missing_finalization_outcome",
  527:         )
  528: 
  529:     terminal_state = _terminal_state_for_outcome(
  530:         record.result_outcome,
  531:     )
  532: 
  533:     canonical_finalized_at = _optional_text(
  534:         finalized_at,
  535:         field_name="finalized_at",
  536:     )
  537: 
  538:     return ExecutionLifecycleRecord(
  539:         execution_id=record.execution_id,
  540:         job_id=record.job_id,
  541:         attempt_number=record.attempt_number,
  542:         fence_id=record.fence_id,
  543:         state=terminal_state,
  544:         active=False,
  545:         started_at=record.started_at,
  546:         finalized_at=canonical_finalized_at,
  547:         result_outcome=record.result_outcome,
  548:     )
  549: 
  550: 
  551: def finalize_execution(
  552:     *,
  553:     record: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  554:     result: ExecutionResult,
  555:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  556:     finalized_at: Optional[str] = None,
  557: ) -> ExecutionLifecycleRecord:
  558:     """
  559:     Canonical convenience operation:
  560: 
  561:     RUNNING
  562:       -> FINALIZING
  563:       -> terminal execution state
  564:     """
  565: 
  566:     finalizing = begin_execution_finalization(
  567:         record=record,
  568:         result=result,
  569:         fence=fence,
  570:     )
  571: 
  572:     return apply_terminal_state(
  573:         record=finalizing,
  574:         finalized_at=finalized_at,
  575:     )
  576: 
  577: 
  578: def certify_execution_lifecycle_controller_v1(
  579: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  580:     """
  581:     Phase 6.4 certification.
  582: 
  583:     Synthetic in-memory contracts only.
  584:     No production runtime state is modified.
  585:     """
  586: 
  587:     from .execution_contracts import (
  588:         ExecutionAction,
  589:         ExecutionContext,
  590:         ExecutionIdentity,
  591:     )
  592: 
  593:     from .execution_permission_fencing import (
  594:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  595:         evaluate_execution_permission,
  596:     )
  597: 
  598:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  599:         execution_id=(
  600:             "33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333"
  601:         ),
  602:         job_id="job-phase-6-4-certification",
  603:         attempt_number=1,
  604:     )
  605: 
  606:     context = ExecutionContext(
  607:         handler_key="certification.handler",
  608:         worker_id="worker-certification",
  609:         lease_id="lease-certification",
  610:         lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  611:     )
  612: 
  613:     request = ExecutionRequest(
  614:         identity=identity,
  615:         context=context,
  616:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  617:     )
  618: 
  619:     evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  620:         job_status="RUNNING",
  621:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  622:         current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  623:         current_lease_owner=(
  624:             "worker-certification::instance-1"
  625:         ),
  626:         lease_state="ACTIVE",
  627:     )
  628: 
  629:     permission = evaluate_execution_permission(
  630:         request=request,
  631:         evidence=evidence,
  632:     )
  633: 
  634:     ready = create_execution_start(
  635:         request=request,
  636:         permission=permission,
  637:     )
  638: 
  639:     fence = permission.fence
  640: 
  641:     if fence is None:
  642:         raise ExecutionLifecycleError(
  643:             "Certification permission did not produce fence.",
  644:             code="certification_missing_fence",
  645:         )
  646: 
  647:     running = apply_running_state(
  648:         record=ready,
  649:         fence=fence,
  650:         started_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  651:     )
  652: 
  653:     success_result = ExecutionResult(
  654:         identity=identity,
  655:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  656:         result_reference="result://phase-6-4/certification",
  657:     )
  658: 
  659:     finalizing = begin_execution_finalization(
  660:         record=running,
  661:         result=success_result,
  662:         fence=fence,
  663:     )
  664: 
  665:     terminal = apply_terminal_state(
  666:         record=finalizing,
  667:         finalized_at="2026-01-01T00:00:01Z",
  668:     )
  669: 
  670:     convenience_terminal = finalize_execution(
  671:         record=running,
  672:         result=success_result,
  673:         fence=fence,
  674:         finalized_at="2026-01-01T00:00:01Z",
  675:     )
  676: 
  677:     denied_validation_passed = False
  678: 
  679:     try:
  680:         denied_permission = ExecutionPermissionDecision(
  681:             disposition=permission.disposition.DENY,
  682:             reason=permission.reason,
  683:             fence=None,
  684:             job_state_eligible=False,
  685:             lease_valid=False,
  686:             worker_valid=False,
  687:             duplicate_safe=False,
  688:         )
  689: 
  690:         create_execution_start(
  691:             request=request,
  692:             permission=denied_permission,
  693:         )
  694: 
  695:     except ExecutionLifecycleError:
  696:         denied_validation_passed = True
  697: 
  698:     checks = {
  699:         "pre_execution_validation_passed":
  700:             permission.allowed,
  701: 
  702:         "execution_start_created_ready":
  703:             (
  704:                 ready.state
  705:                 is ExecutionLifecycleState.READY
  706:             ),
  707: 
  708:         "running_state_applied":
  709:             (
  710:                 running.state
  711:                 is ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING
  712:             ),
  713: 
  714:         "active_execution_tracking_passed":
  715:             is_execution_active(running),
  716: 
  717:         "running_execution_marked_active":
  718:             running.active,
  719: 
  720:         "finalization_started":
  721:             (
  722:                 finalizing.state
  723:                 is ExecutionLifecycleState.FINALIZING
  724:             ),
  725: 
  726:         "finalizing_execution_not_active":
  727:             not finalizing.active,
  728: 
  729:         "terminal_state_applied":
  730:             (
  731:                 terminal.state
  732:                 is ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED
  733:             ),
  734: 
  735:         "terminal_execution_not_active":
  736:             not terminal.active,
  737: 
  738:         "terminal_property_true":
  739:             terminal.terminal,
  740: 
  741:         "finalize_execution_matches":
  742:             (
  743:                 convenience_terminal.state
  744:                 is terminal.state
  745:             ),
  746: 
  747:         "fence_preserved_across_lifecycle":
  748:             (
  749:                 ready.fence_id
  750:                 == running.fence_id
  751:                 == finalizing.fence_id
  752:                 == terminal.fence_id
  753:             ),
  754: 
  755:         "denied_permission_rejected":
  756:             denied_validation_passed,
  757: 
  758:         "no_production_job_mutation":
  759:             True,
  760: 
  761:         "no_queue_mutation":
  762:             True,
  763: 
  764:         "no_lease_mutation":
  765:             True,
  766: 
  767:         "no_handler_execution":
  768:             True,
  769: 
  770:         "no_orchestration_mutation":
  771:             True,
  772: 
  773:         "no_persistence":
  774:             True,
  775:     }
  776: 
  777:     certified = all(checks.values())
  778: 
  779:     return MappingProxyType(
  780:         {
  781:             "phase": "6.4",
  782:             "component":
  783:                 "Execution Lifecycle Controller",
  784:             "version":
  785:                 EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_VERSION,
  786:             "schema_version":
  787:                 EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  788:             "certified":
  789:                 certified,
  790:             "checks":
  791:                 MappingProxyType(checks),
  792:             "authority_boundary": (
  793:                 "Phase 6.4 owns the canonical in-engine execution "
  794:                 "lifecycle from permission validation through READY, "
  795:                 "RUNNING, FINALIZING and terminal execution states. "
  796:                 "It does not yet apply production UniversalJob, queue, "
  797:                 "lease or orchestration mutations."
  798:             ),
  799:         }
  800:     )
  801: 
  802: 
  803: def explain_execution_lifecycle_controller_v1(
  804: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  805: 
  806:     return MappingProxyType(
  807:         {
  808:             "phase": "6.4",
  809:             "component":
  810:                 "Execution Lifecycle Controller",
  811:             "version":
  812:                 EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_VERSION,
  813:             "schema_version":
  814:                 EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  815: 
  816:             "pre_execution_validation": (
  817:                 "Requires an allowed Phase-6.3 permission decision "
  818:                 "and validates that the execution fence matches the "
  819:                 "request identity."
  820:             ),
  821: 
  822:             "execution_start": (
  823:                 "Creates an immutable READY lifecycle record."
  824:             ),
  825: 
  826:             "running_state_application": (
  827:                 "Transitions READY to the Phase-6 RUNNING lifecycle "
  828:                 "state while preserving the execution fence."
  829:             ),
  830: 
  831:             "active_execution_tracking": (
  832:                 "RUNNING lifecycle records are canonically active; "
  833:                 "FINALIZING and terminal records are not active."
  834:             ),
  835: 
  836:             "execution_finalization": (
  837:                 "Validates result identity and execution fence before "
  838:                 "entering FINALIZING."
  839:             ),
  840: 
  841:             "terminal_state_application": (
  842:                 "Maps normalized execution outcome to SUCCEEDED, "
  843:                 "FAILED, CANCELLED or SUSPENDED lifecycle state."
  844:             ),
  845: 
  846:             "prohibitions": (
  847:                 "does not mutate production Universal Jobs",
  848:                 "does not enqueue jobs",
  849:                 "does not dequeue jobs",
  850:                 "does not acquire leases",
  851:                 "does not renew leases",
  852:                 "does not release leases",
  853:                 "does not invoke runtime handlers",
  854:                 "does not decide retries",
  855:                 "does not requeue jobs",
  856:                 "does not mutate orchestration state",
  857:                 "does not persist lifecycle state",
  858:                 "does not save or restore checkpoints",
  859:             ),
  860:         }
  861:     )
  862: 
  863: 
  864: __all__ = [
  865:     "EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_VERSION",
  866:     "EXECUTION_LIFECYCLE_SCHEMA_VERSION",
  867:     "ExecutionLifecycleError",
  868:     "ExecutionLifecycleState",
  869:     "ExecutionLifecycleRecord",
  870:     "validate_pre_execution",
  871:     "create_execution_start",
  872:     "apply_running_state",
  873:     "is_execution_active",
  874:     "begin_execution_finalization",
  875:     "apply_terminal_state",
  876:     "finalize_execution",
  877:     "certify_execution_lifecycle_controller_v1",
  878:     "explain_execution_lifecycle_controller_v1",
  879: ]

==============================================================================================================
END FILE: backend/server/runtime/execution_engine/execution_lifecycle_controller.py

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.3 ù Execution Permission & Fencing
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution-permission and fencing authority.
    6: 
    7: This component consumes the Phase-6.2 ExecutionRequest contract and
    8: caller-supplied evidence from existing job, worker, lease and duplicate
    9: authorities.
   10: 
   11: It intentionally does NOT:
   12: - dequeue or enqueue jobs
   13: - mutate Universal Job state
   14: - mutate orchestration state
   15: - acquire, renew or release leases
   16: - discover workers
   17: - classify worker health or staleness itself
   18: - execute handlers
   19: - perform retry/requeue
   20: - persist state
   21: - suppress queue entries
   22: - perform checkpoint I/O
   23: 
   24: Its job is to answer:
   25: 
   26: "May this specific execution proceed right now, and under which
   27: canonical execution fence?"
   28: """
   29: 
   30: from __future__ import annotations
   31: 
   32: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   33: from enum import Enum
   34: from hashlib import sha256
   35: from types import MappingProxyType
   36: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   37: 
   38: from .execution_contracts import (
   39:     ExecutionAction,
   40:     ExecutionRequest,
   41: )
   42: 
   43: 
   44: EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_VERSION = (
   45:     "execution_permission_fencing_v6.3.1"
   46: )
   47: 
   48: EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   49:     "execution_permission_fencing_schema_v1"
   50: )
   51: 
   52: 
   53: class ExecutionPermissionFencingError(ValueError):
   54:     """Raised when execution-permission evidence is invalid."""
   55: 
   56:     def __init__(
   57:         self,
   58:         message: str,
   59:         *,
   60:         code: str,
   61:         value: Any = None,
   62:     ) -> None:
   63:         super().__init__(message)
   64:         self.code = code
   65:         self.value = value
   66: 
   67: 
   68: def _required_text(
   69:     value: Any,
   70:     *,
   71:     field_name: str,
   72: ) -> str:
   73:     if not isinstance(value, str):
   74:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
   75:             f"{field_name} must be a string.",
   76:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}_type",
   77:             value=value,
   78:         )
   79: 
   80:     normalized = value.strip()
   81: 
   82:     if not normalized:
   83:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
   84:             f"{field_name} must not be empty.",
   85:             code=f"empty_{field_name}",
   86:             value=value,
   87:         )
   88: 
   89:     return normalized
   90: 
   91: 
   92: def _optional_text(
   93:     value: Any,
   94:     *,
   95:     field_name: str,
   96: ) -> Optional[str]:
   97:     if value is None:
   98:         return None
   99: 
  100:     if not isinstance(value, str):
  101:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  102:             f"{field_name} must be a string or None.",
  103:             code=f"invalid_{field_name}_type",
  104:             value=value,
  105:         )
  106: 
  107:     normalized = value.strip()
  108: 
  109:     return normalized or None
  110: 
  111: 
  112: class ExecutionPermissionDisposition(str, Enum):
  113:     ALLOW = "ALLOW"
  114:     DENY = "DENY"
  115: 
  116: 
  117: class ExecutionPermissionReason(str, Enum):
  118:     AUTHORIZED = "AUTHORIZED"
  119: 
  120:     INVALID_JOB_STATE = "INVALID_JOB_STATE"
  121:     TERMINAL_JOB_STATE = "TERMINAL_JOB_STATE"
  122: 
  123:     LEASE_REQUIRED = "LEASE_REQUIRED"
  124:     LEASE_NOT_ACTIVE = "LEASE_NOT_ACTIVE"
  125:     LEASE_ID_MISMATCH = "LEASE_ID_MISMATCH"
  126:     LEASE_OWNER_MISMATCH = "LEASE_OWNER_MISMATCH"
  127: 
  128:     STALE_WORKER = "STALE_WORKER"
  129: 
  130:     DUPLICATE_EXECUTION = "DUPLICATE_EXECUTION"
  131: 
  132:     WORKER_ID_MISMATCH = "WORKER_ID_MISMATCH"
  133: 
  134: 
  135: _TERMINAL_JOB_STATES = frozenset(
  136:     {
  137:         "SUCCEEDED",
  138:         "CANCELLED",
  139:         "DEAD_LETTER",
  140:         "EXPIRED",
  141:     }
  142: )
  143: 
  144: 
  145: _ACTION_ELIGIBLE_JOB_STATES = MappingProxyType(
  146:     {
  147:         ExecutionAction.START: frozenset(
  148:             {
  149:                 "LEASED",
  150:                 "RUNNING",
  151:             }
  152:         ),
  153: 
  154:         ExecutionAction.RETRY: frozenset(
  155:             {
  156:                 "LEASED",
  157:                 "RUNNING",
  158:             }
  159:         ),
  160: 
  161:         ExecutionAction.RESUME: frozenset(
  162:             {
  163:                 "LEASED",
  164:                 "RUNNING",
  165:             }
  166:         ),
  167: 
  168:         ExecutionAction.SUSPEND: frozenset(
  169:             {
  170:                 "RUNNING",
  171:             }
  172:         ),
  173: 
  174:         ExecutionAction.CANCEL: frozenset(
  175:             {
  176:                 "QUEUED",
  177:                 "LEASED",
  178:                 "RUNNING",
  179:                 "SUSPENDED",
  180:                 "FAILED",
  181:             }
  182:         ),
  183: 
  184:         ExecutionAction.COMPLETE: frozenset(
  185:             {
  186:                 "RUNNING",
  187:             }
  188:         ),
  189:     }
  190: )

  198:         ExecutionAction.SUSPEND,
  199:         ExecutionAction.COMPLETE,

  204: @dataclass(
  205:     frozen=True,
  206:     slots=True,
  207: )
  208: class ExecutionPermissionEvidence:
  209:     """
  210:     Caller-supplied execution evidence.
  211: 
  212:     Phase 6.3 consumes evidence from existing authorities.
  213: 
  214:     It does not itself:
  215:     - determine worker staleness
  216:     - acquire leases
  217:     - inspect persistent job stores
  218:     - detect duplicates from storage
  219: 
  220:     Those authorities provide their conclusions here.
  221:     """
  222: 
  223:     job_status: str
  224: 
  225:     current_worker_id: Optional[str] = None
  226: 
  227:     current_lease_id: Optional[str] = None
  228:     current_lease_owner: Optional[str] = None
  229:     lease_state: Optional[str] = None
  230: 
  231:     worker_stale: bool = False
  232: 
  233:     duplicate_execution_detected: bool = False
  234: 
  235:     schema_version: str = field(
  236:         default=EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  237:         init=False,
  238:     )
  239: 
  240:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
  241:         object.__setattr__(
  242:             self,
  243:             "job_status",
  244:             _required_text(
  245:                 self.job_status,
  246:                 field_name="job_status",
  247:             ).upper(),
  248:         )
  249: 
  250:         object.__setattr__(
  251:             self,
  252:             "current_worker_id",
  253:             _optional_text(
  254:                 self.current_worker_id,
  255:                 field_name="current_worker_id",
  256:             ),
  257:         )
  258: 
  259:         object.__setattr__(
  260:             self,
  261:             "current_lease_id",
  262:             _optional_text(
  263:                 self.current_lease_id,
  264:                 field_name="current_lease_id",
  265:             ),
  266:         )
  267: 
  268:         object.__setattr__(
  269:             self,
  270:             "current_lease_owner",
  271:             _optional_text(
  272:                 self.current_lease_owner,
  273:                 field_name="current_lease_owner",
  274:             ),
  275:         )
  276: 
  277:         lease_state = _optional_text(
  278:             self.lease_state,
  279:             field_name="lease_state",
  280:         )
  281: 
  282:         if lease_state is not None:
  283:             lease_state = lease_state.upper()
  284: 
  285:         object.__setattr__(
  286:             self,
  287:             "lease_state",
  288:             lease_state,
  289:         )
  290: 
  291:         if not isinstance(
  292:             self.worker_stale,
  293:             bool,
  294:         ):
  295:             raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  296:                 "worker_stale must be bool.",
  297:                 code="invalid_worker_stale",
  298:                 value=self.worker_stale,
  299:             )
  300: 
  301:         if not isinstance(
  302:             self.duplicate_execution_detected,
  303:             bool,
  304:         ):
  305:             raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  306:                 "duplicate_execution_detected must be bool.",
  307:                 code="invalid_duplicate_execution_detected",
  308:                 value=self.duplicate_execution_detected,
  309:             )
  310: 
  311:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  312:         return {
  313:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  314:             "job_status": self.job_status,
  315:             "current_worker_id":
  316:                 self.current_worker_id,
  317:             "current_lease_id":
  318:                 self.current_lease_id,
  319:             "current_lease_owner":
  320:                 self.current_lease_owner,
  321:             "lease_state":
  322:                 self.lease_state,
  323:             "worker_stale":
  324:                 self.worker_stale,
  325:             "duplicate_execution_detected":
  326:                 self.duplicate_execution_detected,
  327:         }
  328: 
  329: 
  330: @dataclass(
  331:     frozen=True,
  332:     slots=True,
  333: )
  334: class ExecutionFenceIdentity:
  335:     """
  336:     Immutable identity binding execution to its current ownership facts.
  337: 
  338:     A fence binds:
  339:     - execution identity
  340:     - Universal Job identity
  341:     - attempt number
  342:     - worker identity
  343:     - lease identity
  344:     - lease owner
  345: 
  346:     Later Phase-6 components can require the same fence before accepting
  347:     lifecycle transitions or execution results.
  348:     """
  349: 
  350:     fence_id: str
  351: 
  352:     execution_id: str
  353:     job_id: str
  354:     attempt_number: int
  355: 
  356:     worker_id: Optional[str]
  357:     lease_id: Optional[str]
  358:     lease_owner: Optional[str]
  359: 
  360:     schema_version: str = field(
  361:         default=EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  362:         init=False,
  363:     )
  364: 
  365:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  366:         return {
  367:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  368:             "fence_id": self.fence_id,
  369:             "execution_id": self.execution_id,
  370:             "job_id": self.job_id,
  371:             "attempt_number": self.attempt_number,
  372:             "worker_id": self.worker_id,
  373:             "lease_id": self.lease_id,
  374:             "lease_owner": self.lease_owner,
  375:         }
  376: 
  377: 
  378: def create_execution_fence_identity(
  379:     *,
  380:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  381:     evidence: ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  382: ) -> ExecutionFenceIdentity:
  383:     """
  384:     Create deterministic execution-fence identity.
  385: 
  386:     This function does not persist or acquire anything.
  387:     """
  388: 
  389:     if not isinstance(
  390:         request,
  391:         ExecutionRequest,
  392:     ):
  393:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  394:             "request must be ExecutionRequest.",
  395:             code="invalid_execution_request",
  396:             value=request,
  397:         )
  398: 
  399:     if not isinstance(
  400:         evidence,
  401:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  402:     ):
  403:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  404:             "evidence must be ExecutionPermissionEvidence.",
  405:             code="invalid_execution_permission_evidence",
  406:             value=evidence,
  407:         )
  408: 
  409:     worker_id = (
  410:         evidence.current_worker_id
  411:         or request.context.worker_id
  412:     )
  413: 
  414:     lease_id = (
  415:         evidence.current_lease_id
  416:         or request.context.lease_id
  417:     )
  418: 
  419:     lease_owner = (
  420:         evidence.current_lease_owner
  421:         or request.context.lease_owner
  422:     )
  423: 
  424:     canonical_material = "|".join(
  425:         (
  426:             request.identity.execution_id,
  427:             request.identity.job_id,
  428:             str(request.identity.attempt_number),
  429:             worker_id or "",
  430:             lease_id or "",
  431:             lease_owner or "",
  432:         )
  433:     )
  434: 
  435:     digest = sha256(
  436:         canonical_material.encode("utf-8")
  437:     ).hexdigest()
  438: 
  439:     return ExecutionFenceIdentity(
  440:         fence_id=f"execfence:{digest}",
  441:         execution_id=request.identity.execution_id,
  442:         job_id=request.identity.job_id,
  443:         attempt_number=request.identity.attempt_number,
  444:         worker_id=worker_id,
  445:         lease_id=lease_id,
  446:         lease_owner=lease_owner,
  447:     )
  448: 
  449: 
  450: @dataclass(
  451:     frozen=True,
  452:     slots=True,
  453: )
  454: class ExecutionPermissionDecision:
  455:     """
  456:     Canonical Phase-6.3 permission decision.
  457: 
  458:     ALLOW means execution may proceed to the next Phase-6 control layer.
  459: 
  460:     It does NOT mean the handler has executed.
  461:     """
  462: 
  463:     disposition: ExecutionPermissionDisposition
  464:     reason: ExecutionPermissionReason
  465: 
  466:     fence: Optional[ExecutionFenceIdentity]
  467: 
  468:     job_state_eligible: bool
  469:     lease_valid: bool
  470:     worker_valid: bool
  471:     duplicate_safe: bool
  472: 
  473:     schema_version: str = field(
  474:         default=EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  475:         init=False,
  476:     )
  477: 
  478:     @property
  479:     def allowed(self) -> bool:
  480:         return (
  481:             self.disposition
  482:             is ExecutionPermissionDisposition.ALLOW
  483:         )
  484: 
  485:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  486:         return {
  487:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  488:             "disposition":
  489:                 self.disposition.value,
  490:             "reason":
  491:                 self.reason.value,
  492:             "allowed":
  493:                 self.allowed,
  494:             "fence":
  495:                 (
  496:                     self.fence.to_dict()
  497:                     if self.fence is not None
  498:                     else None
  499:                 ),
  500:             "job_state_eligible":
  501:                 self.job_state_eligible,
  502:             "lease_valid":
  503:                 self.lease_valid,
  504:             "worker_valid":
  505:                 self.worker_valid,
  506:             "duplicate_safe":
  507:                 self.duplicate_safe,
  508:         }
  509: 
  510: 
  511: def _deny(
  512:     *,
  513:     reason: ExecutionPermissionReason,
  514:     job_state_eligible: bool,
  515:     lease_valid: bool,
  516:     worker_valid: bool,
  517:     duplicate_safe: bool,
  518: ) -> ExecutionPermissionDecision:
  519: 
  520:     return ExecutionPermissionDecision(
  521:         disposition=ExecutionPermissionDisposition.DENY,
  522:         reason=reason,
  523:         fence=None,
  524:         job_state_eligible=job_state_eligible,
  525:         lease_valid=lease_valid,
  526:         worker_valid=worker_valid,
  527:         duplicate_safe=duplicate_safe,
  528:     )
  529: 
  530: 
  531: def evaluate_execution_permission(
  532:     *,
  533:     request: ExecutionRequest,
  534:     evidence: ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  535: ) -> ExecutionPermissionDecision:
  536:     """
  537:     Canonical Phase-6.3 execution permission evaluation.
  538: 
  539:     Evaluation order:
  540: 
  541:     1. terminal-state rejection
  542:     2. action/job-state eligibility
  543:     3. stale-worker rejection
  544:     4. worker ownership validation
  545:     5. lease validation
  546:     6. duplicate-execution rejection
  547:     7. canonical fence creation
  548:     """
  549: 
  550:     if not isinstance(
  551:         request,
  552:         ExecutionRequest,
  553:     ):
  554:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  555:             "request must be ExecutionRequest.",
  556:             code="invalid_execution_request",
  557:             value=request,
  558:         )
  559: 
  560:     if not isinstance(
  561:         evidence,
  562:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  563:     ):
  564:         raise ExecutionPermissionFencingError(
  565:             "evidence must be ExecutionPermissionEvidence.",
  566:             code="invalid_execution_permission_evidence",
  567:             value=evidence,
  568:         )
  569: 
  570:     job_status = evidence.job_status
  571: 
  572:     if job_status in _TERMINAL_JOB_STATES:
  573:         return _deny(
  574:             reason=(
  575:                 ExecutionPermissionReason.TERMINAL_JOB_STATE
  576:             ),
  577:             job_state_eligible=False,
  578:             lease_valid=False,
  579:             worker_valid=False,
  580:             duplicate_safe=False,
  581:         )
  582: 
  583:     eligible_states = (
  584:         _ACTION_ELIGIBLE_JOB_STATES.get(
  585:             request.action,
  586:             frozenset(),
  587:         )
  588:     )
  589: 
  590:     job_state_eligible = (
  591:         job_status in eligible_states
  592:     )
  593: 
  594:     if not job_state_eligible:
  595:         return _deny(
  596:             reason=(
  597:                 ExecutionPermissionReason.INVALID_JOB_STATE
  598:             ),
  599:             job_state_eligible=False,
  600:             lease_valid=False,
  601:             worker_valid=False,
  602:             duplicate_safe=False,
  603:         )
  604: 
  605:     if evidence.worker_stale:
  606:         return _deny(
  607:             reason=ExecutionPermissionReason.STALE_WORKER,
  608:             job_state_eligible=True,
  609:             lease_valid=False,
  610:             worker_valid=False,
  611:             duplicate_safe=False,
  612:         )
  613: 
  614:     requested_worker = request.context.worker_id
  615:     current_worker = evidence.current_worker_id
  616: 
  617:     if (
  618:         requested_worker is not None
  619:         and current_worker is not None
  620:         and requested_worker != current_worker
  621:     ):
  622:         return _deny(
  623:             reason=(
  624:                 ExecutionPermissionReason.WORKER_ID_MISMATCH
  625:             ),
  626:             job_state_eligible=True,
  627:             lease_valid=False,
  628:             worker_valid=False,
  629:             duplicate_safe=False,
  630:         )
  631: 
  632:     worker_valid = True
  633: 
  634:     lease_valid = True
  635: 
  636:     if request.action in _LEASE_REQUIRED_ACTIONS:
  637: 
  638:         requested_lease_id = request.context.lease_id
  639:         requested_lease_owner = request.context.lease_owner
  640: 
  641:         if (
  642:             requested_lease_id is None
  643:             or requested_lease_owner is None
  644:             or evidence.current_lease_id is None
  645:             or evidence.current_lease_owner is None
  646:         ):
  647:             return _deny(
  648:                 reason=ExecutionPermissionReason.LEASE_REQUIRED,
  649:                 job_state_eligible=True,
  650:                 lease_valid=False,
  651:                 worker_valid=True,
  652:                 duplicate_safe=False,
  653:             )
  654: 
  655:         if evidence.lease_state != "ACTIVE":
  656:             return _deny(
  657:                 reason=(
  658:                     ExecutionPermissionReason.LEASE_NOT_ACTIVE
  659:                 ),
  660:                 job_state_eligible=True,
  661:                 lease_valid=False,
  662:                 worker_valid=True,
  663:                 duplicate_safe=False,
  664:             )
  665: 
  666:         if (
  667:             requested_lease_id
  668:             != evidence.current_lease_id
  669:         ):
  670:             return _deny(
  671:                 reason=(
  672:                     ExecutionPermissionReason.LEASE_ID_MISMATCH
  673:                 ),
  674:                 job_state_eligible=True,
  675:                 lease_valid=False,
  676:                 worker_valid=True,
  677:                 duplicate_safe=False,
  678:             )
  679: 
  680:         if (
  681:             requested_lease_owner
  682:             != evidence.current_lease_owner
  683:         ):
  684:             return _deny(
  685:                 reason=(
  686:                     ExecutionPermissionReason.LEASE_OWNER_MISMATCH
  687:                 ),
  688:                 job_state_eligible=True,
  689:                 lease_valid=False,
  690:                 worker_valid=True,
  691:                 duplicate_safe=False,
  692:             )
  693: 
  694:         lease_valid = True
  695: 
  696:     if evidence.duplicate_execution_detected:
  697:         return _deny(
  698:             reason=(
  699:                 ExecutionPermissionReason.DUPLICATE_EXECUTION
  700:             ),
  701:             job_state_eligible=True,
  702:             lease_valid=lease_valid,
  703:             worker_valid=worker_valid,
  704:             duplicate_safe=False,
  705:         )
  706: 
  707:     fence = create_execution_fence_identity(
  708:         request=request,
  709:         evidence=evidence,
  710:     )
  711: 
  712:     return ExecutionPermissionDecision(
  713:         disposition=ExecutionPermissionDisposition.ALLOW,
  714:         reason=ExecutionPermissionReason.AUTHORIZED,
  715:         fence=fence,
  716:         job_state_eligible=True,
  717:         lease_valid=lease_valid,
  718:         worker_valid=worker_valid,
  719:         duplicate_safe=True,
  720:     )
  721: 
  722: 
  723: def certify_execution_permission_fencing_v1(
  724: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  725:     """
  726:     Phase 6.3 certification.
  727: 
  728:     Uses synthetic evidence only.
  729:     No production runtime state is mutated.
  730:     """
  731: 
  732:     from .execution_contracts import (
  733:         ExecutionContext,
  734:         ExecutionIdentity,
  735:     )
  736: 
  737:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  738:         execution_id=(
  739:             "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222"
  740:         ),
  741:         job_id="job-phase-6-3-certification",
  742:         attempt_number=1,
  743:     )
  744: 
  745:     context = ExecutionContext(
  746:         handler_key="certification.handler",
  747:         worker_id="worker-certification",
  748:         worker_instance_id="instance-certification",
  749:         lease_id="lease-certification",
  750:         lease_owner=(
  751:             "worker-certification::instance-certification"
  752:         ),
  753:     )
  754: 
  755:     request = ExecutionRequest(
  756:         identity=identity,
  757:         context=context,
  758:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  759:     )
  760: 
  761:     valid_evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  762:         job_status="RUNNING",
  763:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  764:         current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  765:         current_lease_owner=(
  766:             "worker-certification::instance-certification"
  767:         ),
  768:         lease_state="ACTIVE",
  769:         worker_stale=False,
  770:         duplicate_execution_detected=False,
  771:     )
  772: 
  773:     allowed = evaluate_execution_permission(
  774:         request=request,
  775:         evidence=valid_evidence,
  776:     )
  777: 
  778:     second_fence = create_execution_fence_identity(
  779:         request=request,
  780:         evidence=valid_evidence,
  781:     )
  782: 
  783:     terminal_evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  784:         job_status="SUCCEEDED",
  785:     )
  786: 
  787:     terminal_denied = evaluate_execution_permission(
  788:         request=request,
  789:         evidence=terminal_evidence,
  790:     )
  791: 
  792:     stale_evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  793:         job_status="RUNNING",
  794:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  795:         current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  796:         current_lease_owner=(
  797:             "worker-certification::instance-certification"
  798:         ),
  799:         lease_state="ACTIVE",
  800:         worker_stale=True,
  801:     )
  802: 
  803:     stale_denied = evaluate_execution_permission(
  804:         request=request,
  805:         evidence=stale_evidence,
  806:     )
  807: 
  808:     wrong_lease_evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  809:         job_status="RUNNING",
  810:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  811:         current_lease_id="different-lease",
  812:         current_lease_owner=(
  813:             "worker-certification::instance-certification"
  814:         ),
  815:         lease_state="ACTIVE",
  816:     )
  817: 
  818:     wrong_lease_denied = evaluate_execution_permission(
  819:         request=request,
  820:         evidence=wrong_lease_evidence,
  821:     )
  822: 
  823:     wrong_owner_evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  824:         job_status="RUNNING",
  825:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  826:         current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  827:         current_lease_owner=(
  828:             "different-worker::different-instance"
  829:         ),
  830:         lease_state="ACTIVE",
  831:     )
  832: 
  833:     wrong_owner_denied = evaluate_execution_permission(
  834:         request=request,
  835:         evidence=wrong_owner_evidence,
  836:     )
  837: 
  838:     duplicate_evidence = ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  839:         job_status="RUNNING",
  840:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  841:         current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  842:         current_lease_owner=(
  843:             "worker-certification::instance-certification"
  844:         ),
  845:         lease_state="ACTIVE",
  846:         duplicate_execution_detected=True,
  847:     )
  848: 
  849:     duplicate_denied = evaluate_execution_permission(
  850:         request=request,
  851:         evidence=duplicate_evidence,
  852:     )
  853: 
  854:     checks = {
  855:         "valid_execution_allowed":
  856:             allowed.allowed,
  857: 
  858:         "allowed_execution_has_fence":
  859:             allowed.fence is not None,
  860: 
  861:         "job_state_eligibility_passed":
  862:             allowed.job_state_eligible,
  863: 
  864:         "lease_validation_passed":
  865:             allowed.lease_valid,
  866: 
  867:         "worker_validation_passed":
  868:             allowed.worker_valid,
  869: 
  870:         "duplicate_protection_passed":
  871:             allowed.duplicate_safe,
  872: 
  873:         "terminal_state_rejected":
  874:             (
  875:                 terminal_denied.reason
  876:                 is ExecutionPermissionReason.TERMINAL_JOB_STATE
  877:             ),
  878: 
  879:         "stale_execution_rejected":
  880:             (
  881:                 stale_denied.reason
  882:                 is ExecutionPermissionReason.STALE_WORKER
  883:             ),
  884: 
  885:         "lease_id_mismatch_rejected":
  886:             (
  887:                 wrong_lease_denied.reason
  888:                 is ExecutionPermissionReason.LEASE_ID_MISMATCH
  889:             ),
  890: 
  891:         "lease_owner_mismatch_rejected":
  892:             (
  893:                 wrong_owner_denied.reason
  894:                 is ExecutionPermissionReason.LEASE_OWNER_MISMATCH
  895:             ),
  896: 
  897:         "duplicate_execution_rejected":
  898:             (
  899:                 duplicate_denied.reason
  900:                 is ExecutionPermissionReason.DUPLICATE_EXECUTION
  901:             ),
  902: 
  903:         "fence_identity_deterministic":
  904:             (
  905:                 allowed.fence is not None
  906:                 and allowed.fence.fence_id
  907:                 == second_fence.fence_id
  908:             ),
  909: 
  910:         "no_job_mutation":
  911:             True,
  912: 
  913:         "no_queue_mutation":
  914:             True,
  915: 
  916:         "no_lease_mutation":
  917:             True,
  918: 
  919:         "no_handler_execution":
  920:             True,
  921: 
  922:         "no_orchestration_mutation":
  923:             True,
  924: 
  925:         "no_persistence":
  926:             True,
  927:     }
  928: 
  929:     certified = all(checks.values())
  930: 
  931:     return MappingProxyType(
  932:         {
  933:             "phase": "6.3",
  934:             "component":
  935:                 "Execution Permission & Fencing",
  936:             "version":
  937:                 EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_VERSION,
  938:             "schema_version":
  939:                 EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  940:             "certified":
  941:                 certified,
  942:             "checks":
  943:                 MappingProxyType(checks),
  944:             "authority_boundary": (
  945:                 "Phase 6.3 evaluates whether one ExecutionRequest "
  946:                 "may proceed using caller-supplied job, worker, "
  947:                 "lease, stale-worker and duplicate-execution "
  948:                 "evidence, and produces a deterministic execution "
  949:                 "fence. It does not execute or mutate runtime state."
  950:             ),
  951:         }
  952:     )
  953: 
  954: 
  955: def explain_execution_permission_fencing_v1(
  956: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  957: 
  958:     return MappingProxyType(
  959:         {
  960:             "phase": "6.3",
  961:             "component":
  962:                 "Execution Permission & Fencing",
  963:             "version":
  964:                 EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_VERSION,
  965:             "schema_version":
  966:                 EXECUTION_PERMISSION_FENCING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  967: 
  968:             "execution_permission_evaluation": (
  969:                 "Evaluates whether a Phase-6.2 ExecutionRequest "
  970:                 "may proceed."
  971:             ),
  972: 
  973:             "job_state_eligibility": (
  974:                 "Rejects terminal or action-ineligible job states "
  975:                 "without mutating the Universal Job."
  976:             ),
  977: 
  978:             "lease_ownership_validation": (
  979:                 "Consumes existing lease evidence and requires "
  980:                 "matching active lease identity and ownership for "
  981:                 "lease-bound execution actions."
  982:             ),
  983: 
  984:             "stale_execution_rejection": (
  985:                 "Consumes caller-supplied stale-worker evidence "
  986:                 "and rejects execution when the worker is stale."
  987:             ),
  988: 
  989:             "duplicate_execution_protection": (
  990:                 "Consumes duplicate-execution evidence and rejects "
  991:                 "a detected duplicate before handler execution."
  992:             ),
  993: 
  994:             "execution_fence_identity": (
  995:                 "Creates deterministic immutable fencing identity "
  996:                 "binding execution, job, attempt, worker and lease."
  997:             ),
  998: 
  999:             "prohibitions": (
 1000:                 "does not dequeue jobs",
 1001:                 "does not enqueue jobs",
 1002:                 "does not mutate Universal Jobs",
 1003:                 "does not mutate orchestration state",
 1004:                 "does not acquire leases",
 1005:                 "does not renew leases",
 1006:                 "does not release leases",
 1007:                 "does not classify worker staleness",
 1008:                 "does not discover workers",
 1009:                 "does not invoke handlers",
 1010:                 "does not perform retries",
 1011:                 "does not perform requeues",
 1012:                 "does not persist execution state",
 1013:                 "does not save or restore checkpoints",
 1014:             ),
 1015:         }
 1016:     )

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.6 ù Execution Result Processing
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution-result processing layer.
    6: 
    7: Consumes:
    8: - Phase 6.2 ExecutionResult
    9: - Phase 6.3 ExecutionFenceIdentity
   10: - Phase 6.4 RUNNING ExecutionLifecycleRecord
   11: - Phase 6.5 handler-produced result
   12: 
   13: Owns:
   14: - successful result normalization
   15: - failed result normalization
   16: - result-to-job-state resolution
   17: - execution evidence generation
   18: - result integrity validation
   19: 
   20: Does NOT:
   21: - mutate UniversalJob
   22: - persist job state
   23: - enqueue/requeue jobs
   24: - acquire/release leases
   25: - mutate orchestration state
   26: - decide retry policy
   27: - perform completion/cancellation transitions
   28: 
   29: Production job-state application occurs in later Phase-6 components
   30: and final integration.
   31: """
   32: 
   33: from __future__ import annotations
   34: 
   35: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   36: from enum import Enum
   37: from types import MappingProxyType
   38: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   39:

   41:     ExecutionFailure,

   43:     ExecutionResult,
   44: )
   45:

   47:     ExecutionFenceIdentity,
   48: )

   52:     ExecutionLifecycleState,
   53: )

   57:     "execution_result_processing_v6.6.1"
   58: )

   61:     "execution_result_processing_schema_v1"
   62: )
