   65: class ExecutionResultProcessingError(ValueError):
   66:     """Raised when execution-result processing is invalid."""
   67: 
   68:     def __init__(
   69:         self,
   70:         message: str,
   71:         *,
   72:         code: str,
   73:         value: Any = None,
   74:     ) -> None:
   75:         super().__init__(message)
   76:         self.code = code
   77:         self.value = value
   78: 
   79: 
   80: class ResolvedJobState(str, Enum):
   81:     """
   82:     Intended Universal Job state derived from execution outcome.
   83: 
   84:     This is a resolution only.
   85:     It does not mutate the Universal Job.
   86:     """
   87: 
   88:     SUCCEEDED = "SUCCEEDED"
   89:     FAILED = "FAILED"
   90:     CANCELLED = "CANCELLED"
   91:     SUSPENDED = "SUSPENDED"
   92: 
   93: 
   94: @dataclass(
   95:     frozen=True,
   96:     slots=True,
   97: )
   98: class ExecutionResultIntegrity:
   99:     execution_id_match: bool
  100:     job_id_match: bool
  101:     attempt_number_match: bool
  102:     fence_match: bool
  103:     lifecycle_running: bool
  104:     lifecycle_active: bool
  105:     outcome_valid: bool
  106:     failure_shape_valid: bool
  107: 
  108:     schema_version: str = field(
  109:         default=EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  110:         init=False,
  111:     )
  112: 
  113:     @property
  114:     def valid(self) -> bool:
  115:         return all(
  116:             (
  117:                 self.execution_id_match,
  118:                 self.job_id_match,
  119:                 self.attempt_number_match,
  120:                 self.fence_match,
  121:                 self.lifecycle_running,
  122:                 self.lifecycle_active,
  123:                 self.outcome_valid,
  124:                 self.failure_shape_valid,
  125:             )
  126:         )
  127: 
  128:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  129:         return {
  130:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  131:             "execution_id_match":
  132:                 self.execution_id_match,
  133:             "job_id_match":
  134:                 self.job_id_match,
  135:             "attempt_number_match":
  136:                 self.attempt_number_match,
  137:             "fence_match":
  138:                 self.fence_match,
  139:             "lifecycle_running":
  140:                 self.lifecycle_running,
  141:             "lifecycle_active":
  142:                 self.lifecycle_active,
  143:             "outcome_valid":
  144:                 self.outcome_valid,
  145:             "failure_shape_valid":
  146:                 self.failure_shape_valid,
  147:             "valid":
  148:                 self.valid,
  149:         }
  150: 
  151: 
  152: @dataclass(
  153:     frozen=True,
  154:     slots=True,
  155: )
  156: class NormalizedExecutionResult:
  157:     """
  158:     Canonical normalized Phase-6 result.
  159: 
  160:     Carries result facts only.
  161:     """
  162: 
  163:     execution_id: str
  164:     job_id: str
  165:     attempt_number: int
  166:     fence_id: str
  167: 
  168:     outcome: ExecutionOutcome
  169:     resolved_job_state: ResolvedJobState
  170: 
  171:     result_reference: Optional[str] = None
  172:     output_payload: Any = None
  173:     failure: Optional[ExecutionFailure] = None
  174: 
  175:     metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
  176:         default_factory=dict,
  177:     )
  178: 
  179:     schema_version: str = field(
  180:         default=EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  181:         init=False,
  182:     )
  183: 
  184:     @property
  185:     def succeeded(self) -> bool:
  186:         return self.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED
  187: 
  188:     @property
  189:     def failed(self) -> bool:
  190:         return self.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED
  191: 
  192:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  193:         return {
  194:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  195:             "execution_id": self.execution_id,
  196:             "job_id": self.job_id,
  197:             "attempt_number": self.attempt_number,
  198:             "fence_id": self.fence_id,
  199:             "outcome": self.outcome.value,
  200:             "resolved_job_state":
  201:                 self.resolved_job_state.value,
  202:             "result_reference":
  203:                 self.result_reference,
  204:             "output_payload":
  205:                 self.output_payload,
  206:             "failure":
  207:                 (
  208:                     self.failure.to_dict()
  209:                     if self.failure is not None
  210:                     else None
  211:                 ),
  212:             "metadata":
  213:                 dict(self.metadata),
  214:         }
  215: 
  216: 
  217: @dataclass(
  218:     frozen=True,
  219:     slots=True,
  220: )
  221: class ExecutionEvidence:
  222:     """
  223:     Canonical execution evidence produced after result processing.
  224:     """
  225: 
  226:     execution_id: str
  227:     job_id: str
  228:     attempt_number: int
  229:     fence_id: str
  230: 
  231:     outcome: ExecutionOutcome
  232:     resolved_job_state: ResolvedJobState
  233: 
  234:     result_reference: Optional[str]
  235:     failure_code: Optional[str]
  236:     integrity_valid: bool
  237: 
  238:     evidence_type: str = "EXECUTION_RESULT"
  239: 
  240:     schema_version: str = field(
  241:         default=EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  242:         init=False,
  243:     )
  244: 
  245:     def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  246:         return {
  247:             "schema_version": self.schema_version,
  248:             "evidence_type": self.evidence_type,
  249:             "execution_id": self.execution_id,
  250:             "job_id": self.job_id,
  251:             "attempt_number": self.attempt_number,
  252:             "fence_id": self.fence_id,
  253:             "outcome": self.outcome.value,
  254:             "resolved_job_state":
  255:                 self.resolved_job_state.value,
  256:             "result_reference":
  257:                 self.result_reference,
  258:             "failure_code":
  259:                 self.failure_code,
  260:             "integrity_valid":
  261:                 self.integrity_valid,
  262:         }
  263: 
  264: 
  265: def validate_result_integrity(
  266:     *,
  267:     result: ExecutionResult,
  268:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  269:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  270: ) -> ExecutionResultIntegrity:
  271:     """
  272:     Validate that a result belongs to the currently active execution.
  273: 
  274:     The fence is also checked against result metadata when Phase 6.5
  275:     supplied fence_id.
  276:     """
  277: 
  278:     if not isinstance(result, ExecutionResult):
  279:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  280:             "result must be ExecutionResult.",
  281:             code="invalid_execution_result",
  282:             value=result,
  283:         )
  284: 
  285:     if not isinstance(
  286:         lifecycle,
  287:         ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  288:     ):
  289:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  290:             "lifecycle must be ExecutionLifecycleRecord.",
  291:             code="invalid_execution_lifecycle",
  292:             value=lifecycle,
  293:         )
  294: 
  295:     if not isinstance(fence, ExecutionFenceIdentity):
  296:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  297:             "fence must be ExecutionFenceIdentity.",
  298:             code="invalid_execution_fence",
  299:             value=fence,
  300:         )
  301: 
  302:     result_fence_id = result.result_metadata.get(
  303:         "fence_id"
  304:     )
  305: 
  306:     fence_match = (
  307:         lifecycle.fence_id == fence.fence_id
  308:         and (
  309:             result_fence_id is None
  310:             or result_fence_id == fence.fence_id
  311:         )
  312:     )
  313: 
  314:     outcome_valid = result.outcome in {
  315:         ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  316:         ExecutionOutcome.FAILED,
  317:         ExecutionOutcome.CANCELLED,
  318:         ExecutionOutcome.SUSPENDED,
  319:     }
  320: 
  321:     failure_shape_valid = (
  322:         (
  323:             result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED
  324:             and result.failure is not None
  325:         )
  326:         or
  327:         (
  328:             result.outcome is not ExecutionOutcome.FAILED
  329:             and result.failure is None
  330:         )
  331:     )
  332: 
  333:     return ExecutionResultIntegrity(
  334:         execution_id_match=(
  335:             result.identity.execution_id
  336:             == lifecycle.execution_id
  337:             == fence.execution_id
  338:         ),
  339:         job_id_match=(
  340:             result.identity.job_id
  341:             == lifecycle.job_id
  342:             == fence.job_id
  343:         ),
  344:         attempt_number_match=(
  345:             result.identity.attempt_number
  346:             == lifecycle.attempt_number
  347:             == fence.attempt_number
  348:         ),
  349:         fence_match=fence_match,
  350:         lifecycle_running=(
  351:             lifecycle.state
  352:             is ExecutionLifecycleState.RUNNING
  353:         ),
  354:         lifecycle_active=lifecycle.active,
  355:         outcome_valid=outcome_valid,
  356:         failure_shape_valid=failure_shape_valid,
  357:     )
  358: 
  359: 
  360: def resolve_result_to_job_state(
  361:     result: ExecutionResult,
  362: ) -> ResolvedJobState:
  363:     """
  364:     Resolve execution outcome to intended Universal Job state.
  365: 
  366:     No Universal Job mutation occurs here.
  367:     """
  368: 
  369:     if not isinstance(result, ExecutionResult):
  370:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  371:             "result must be ExecutionResult.",
  372:             code="invalid_execution_result",
  373:             value=result,
  374:         )
  375: 
  376:     if result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED:
  377:         return ResolvedJobState.SUCCEEDED
  378: 
  379:     if result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED:
  380:         return ResolvedJobState.FAILED
  381: 
  382:     if result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.CANCELLED:
  383:         return ResolvedJobState.CANCELLED
  384: 
  385:     if result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.SUSPENDED:
  386:         return ResolvedJobState.SUSPENDED
  387: 
  388:     raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  389:         "Unsupported execution outcome.",
  390:         code="unsupported_execution_outcome",
  391:         value=result.outcome,
  392:     )
  393: 
  394: 
  395: def normalize_successful_result(
  396:     *,
  397:     result: ExecutionResult,
  398:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  399:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  400: ) -> NormalizedExecutionResult:
  401: 
  402:     if result.outcome is not ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED:
  403:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  404:             "normalize_successful_result requires SUCCEEDED outcome.",
  405:             code="successful_normalizer_wrong_outcome",
  406:             value=result.outcome,
  407:         )
  408: 
  409:     integrity = validate_result_integrity(
  410:         result=result,
  411:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  412:         fence=fence,
  413:     )
  414: 
  415:     if not integrity.valid:
  416:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  417:             "Successful result failed integrity validation.",
  418:             code="successful_result_integrity_failed",
  419:             value=integrity.to_dict(),
  420:         )
  421: 
  422:     return NormalizedExecutionResult(
  423:         execution_id=result.identity.execution_id,
  424:         job_id=result.identity.job_id,
  425:         attempt_number=result.identity.attempt_number,
  426:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  427:         outcome=result.outcome,
  428:         resolved_job_state=ResolvedJobState.SUCCEEDED,
  429:         result_reference=result.result_reference,
  430:         output_payload=result.output_payload,
  431:         failure=None,
  432:         metadata=MappingProxyType(
  433:             {
  434:                 **dict(result.result_metadata),
  435:                 "normalized":
  436:                     True,
  437:                 "integrity_valid":
  438:                     True,
  439:             }
  440:         ),
  441:     )
  442: 
  443: 
  444: def normalize_failed_result(
  445:     *,
  446:     result: ExecutionResult,
  447:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  448:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  449: ) -> NormalizedExecutionResult:
  450: 
  451:     if result.outcome is not ExecutionOutcome.FAILED:
  452:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  453:             "normalize_failed_result requires FAILED outcome.",
  454:             code="failed_normalizer_wrong_outcome",
  455:             value=result.outcome,
  456:         )
  457: 
  458:     if result.failure is None:
  459:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  460:             "FAILED execution requires failure evidence.",
  461:             code="failed_result_missing_failure",
  462:         )
  463: 
  464:     integrity = validate_result_integrity(
  465:         result=result,
  466:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  467:         fence=fence,
  468:     )
  469: 
  470:     if not integrity.valid:
  471:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  472:             "Failed result failed integrity validation.",
  473:             code="failed_result_integrity_failed",
  474:             value=integrity.to_dict(),
  475:         )
  476: 
  477:     return NormalizedExecutionResult(
  478:         execution_id=result.identity.execution_id,
  479:         job_id=result.identity.job_id,
  480:         attempt_number=result.identity.attempt_number,
  481:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  482:         outcome=result.outcome,
  483:         resolved_job_state=ResolvedJobState.FAILED,
  484:         result_reference=result.result_reference,
  485:         output_payload=result.output_payload,
  486:         failure=result.failure,
  487:         metadata=MappingProxyType(
  488:             {
  489:                 **dict(result.result_metadata),
  490:                 "normalized":
  491:                     True,
  492:                 "integrity_valid":
  493:                     True,
  494:             }
  495:         ),
  496:     )
  497: 
  498: 
  499: def normalize_execution_result(
  500:     *,
  501:     result: ExecutionResult,
  502:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  503:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  504: ) -> NormalizedExecutionResult:
  505:     """
  506:     Normalize all canonical execution outcomes.
  507: 
  508:     SUCCEEDED and FAILED use their dedicated normalization paths.
  509:     CANCELLED and SUSPENDED use the same integrity boundary and are
  510:     normalized here for later Phase-6 consumers.
  511:     """
  512: 
  513:     if result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED:
  514:         return normalize_successful_result(
  515:             result=result,
  516:             lifecycle=lifecycle,
  517:             fence=fence,
  518:         )
  519: 
  520:     if result.outcome is ExecutionOutcome.FAILED:
  521:         return normalize_failed_result(
  522:             result=result,
  523:             lifecycle=lifecycle,
  524:             fence=fence,
  525:         )
  526: 
  527:     integrity = validate_result_integrity(
  528:         result=result,
  529:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  530:         fence=fence,
  531:     )
  532: 
  533:     if not integrity.valid:
  534:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  535:             "Execution result failed integrity validation.",
  536:             code="execution_result_integrity_failed",
  537:             value=integrity.to_dict(),
  538:         )
  539: 
  540:     resolved_job_state = resolve_result_to_job_state(
  541:         result
  542:     )
  543: 
  544:     return NormalizedExecutionResult(
  545:         execution_id=result.identity.execution_id,
  546:         job_id=result.identity.job_id,
  547:         attempt_number=result.identity.attempt_number,
  548:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  549:         outcome=result.outcome,
  550:         resolved_job_state=resolved_job_state,
  551:         result_reference=result.result_reference,
  552:         output_payload=result.output_payload,
  553:         failure=result.failure,
  554:         metadata=MappingProxyType(
  555:             {
  556:                 **dict(result.result_metadata),
  557:                 "normalized":
  558:                     True,
  559:                 "integrity_valid":
  560:                     True,
  561:             }
  562:         ),
  563:     )
  564: 
  565: 
  566: def generate_execution_evidence(
  567:     *,
  568:     normalized: NormalizedExecutionResult,
  569: ) -> ExecutionEvidence:
  570:     """
  571:     Generate immutable evidence for downstream lifecycle, orchestration,
  572:     persistence and observability handoff.
  573:     """
  574: 
  575:     if not isinstance(
  576:         normalized,
  577:         NormalizedExecutionResult,
  578:     ):
  579:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  580:             "normalized must be NormalizedExecutionResult.",
  581:             code="invalid_normalized_execution_result",
  582:             value=normalized,
  583:         )
  584: 
  585:     return ExecutionEvidence(
  586:         execution_id=normalized.execution_id,
  587:         job_id=normalized.job_id,
  588:         attempt_number=normalized.attempt_number,
  589:         fence_id=normalized.fence_id,
  590:         outcome=normalized.outcome,
  591:         resolved_job_state=(
  592:             normalized.resolved_job_state
  593:         ),
  594:         result_reference=(
  595:             normalized.result_reference
  596:         ),
  597:         failure_code=(
  598:             normalized.failure.error_code
  599:             if normalized.failure is not None
  600:             else None
  601:         ),
  602:         integrity_valid=(
  603:             bool(
  604:                 normalized.metadata.get(
  605:                     "integrity_valid",
  606:                     False,
  607:                 )
  608:             )
  609:         ),
  610:     )
  611: 
  612: 
  613: def process_execution_result(
  614:     *,
  615:     result: ExecutionResult,
  616:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  617:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  618: ) -> tuple[
  619:     NormalizedExecutionResult,
  620:     ExecutionEvidence,
  621: ]:
  622:     """
  623:     Canonical Phase-6.6 processing operation.
  624: 
  625:     validate integrity
  626:         -> normalize
  627:         -> resolve intended job state
  628:         -> generate execution evidence
  629:     """
  630: 
  631:     normalized = normalize_execution_result(
  632:         result=result,
  633:         lifecycle=lifecycle,
  634:         fence=fence,
  635:     )
  636: 
  637:     evidence = generate_execution_evidence(
  638:         normalized=normalized,
  639:     )
  640: 
  641:     return normalized, evidence
  642: 
  643: 
  644: def certify_execution_result_processing_v1(
  645: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  646:     """
  647:     Phase 6.6 certification.
  648: 
  649:     Synthetic in-memory execution only.
  650:     """
  651: 
  652:     from .execution_contracts import (
  653:         ExecutionAction,
  654:         ExecutionContext,
  655:         ExecutionFailureKind,
  656:         ExecutionIdentity,
  657:         ExecutionRequest,
  658:     )
  659: 
  660:     from .execution_permission_fencing import (
  661:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  662:         evaluate_execution_permission,
  663:     )
  664: 
  665:     from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
  666:         apply_running_state,
  667:         create_execution_start,
  668:     )
  669: 
  670:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  671:         execution_id=(
  672:             "55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555555"
  673:         ),
  674:         job_id="job-phase-6-6-certification",
  675:         attempt_number=1,
  676:     )
  677: 
  678:     context = ExecutionContext(
  679:         handler_key="certification.handler",
  680:         worker_id="worker-certification",
  681:         lease_id="lease-certification",
  682:         lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  683:     )
  684: 
  685:     request = ExecutionRequest(
  686:         identity=identity,
  687:         context=context,
  688:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  689:     )
  690: 
  691:     permission = evaluate_execution_permission(
  692:         request=request,
  693:         evidence=ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  694:             job_status="RUNNING",
  695:             current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  696:             current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  697:             current_lease_owner=(
  698:                 "worker-certification::instance-1"
  699:             ),
  700:             lease_state="ACTIVE",
  701:         ),
  702:     )
  703: 
  704:     fence = permission.fence
  705: 
  706:     if fence is None:
  707:         raise ExecutionResultProcessingError(
  708:             "Certification missing execution fence.",
  709:             code="certification_missing_fence",
  710:         )
  711: 
  712:     ready = create_execution_start(
  713:         request=request,
  714:         permission=permission,
  715:     )
  716: 
  717:     running = apply_running_state(
  718:         record=ready,
  719:         fence=fence,
  720:     )
  721: 
  722:     success = ExecutionResult(
  723:         identity=identity,
  724:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  725:         result_reference="result://phase-6-6/success",
  726:         output_payload={
  727:             "value": 42,
  728:         },
  729:         result_metadata={
  730:             "fence_id":
  731:                 fence.fence_id,
  732:         },
  733:     )
  734: 
  735:     success_integrity = validate_result_integrity(
  736:         result=success,
  737:         lifecycle=running,
  738:         fence=fence,
  739:     )
  740: 
  741:     success_normalized = normalize_successful_result(
  742:         result=success,
  743:         lifecycle=running,
  744:         fence=fence,
  745:     )
  746: 
  747:     success_evidence = generate_execution_evidence(
  748:         normalized=success_normalized,
  749:     )
  750: 
  751:     failure = ExecutionFailure(
  752:         kind=ExecutionFailureKind.HANDLER,
  753:         error_code="synthetic_failure",
  754:         message="Synthetic result-processing failure.",
  755:         originating_component="phase_6_6_certification",
  756:     )
  757: 
  758:     failed = ExecutionResult(
  759:         identity=identity,
  760:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.FAILED,
  761:         failure=failure,
  762:         result_metadata={
  763:             "fence_id":
  764:                 fence.fence_id,
  765:         },
  766:     )
  767: 
  768:     failed_integrity = validate_result_integrity(
  769:         result=failed,
  770:         lifecycle=running,
  771:         fence=fence,
  772:     )
  773: 
  774:     failed_normalized = normalize_failed_result(
  775:         result=failed,
  776:         lifecycle=running,
  777:         fence=fence,
  778:     )
  779: 
  780:     failed_evidence = generate_execution_evidence(
  781:         normalized=failed_normalized,
  782:     )
  783: 
  784:     processed_success, processed_evidence = (
  785:         process_execution_result(
  786:             result=success,
  787:             lifecycle=running,
  788:             fence=fence,
  789:         )
  790:     )
  791: 
  792:     bad_fence_result = ExecutionResult(
  793:         identity=identity,
  794:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  795:         result_metadata={
  796:             "fence_id":
  797:                 "execfence:stale-or-invalid",
  798:         },
  799:     )
  800: 
  801:     bad_integrity = validate_result_integrity(
  802:         result=bad_fence_result,
  803:         lifecycle=running,
  804:         fence=fence,
  805:     )
  806: 
  807:     checks = {
  808:         "successful_result_integrity_valid":
  809:             success_integrity.valid,
  810: 
  811:         "successful_result_normalized":
  812:             (
  813:                 success_normalized.succeeded
  814:                 and success_normalized.output_payload
  815:                 == {"value": 42}
  816:             ),
  817: 
  818:         "failed_result_integrity_valid":
  819:             failed_integrity.valid,
  820: 
  821:         "failed_result_normalized":
  822:             (
  823:                 failed_normalized.failed
  824:                 and failed_normalized.failure is not None
  825:             ),
  826: 
  827:         "success_job_state_resolved":
  828:             (
  829:                 success_normalized.resolved_job_state
  830:                 is ResolvedJobState.SUCCEEDED
  831:             ),
  832: 
  833:         "failure_job_state_resolved":
  834:             (
  835:                 failed_normalized.resolved_job_state
  836:                 is ResolvedJobState.FAILED
  837:             ),
  838: 
  839:         "success_evidence_generated":
  840:             (
  841:                 success_evidence.integrity_valid
  842:                 and success_evidence.failure_code is None
  843:             ),
  844: 
  845:         "failure_evidence_generated":
  846:             (
  847:                 failed_evidence.integrity_valid
  848:                 and failed_evidence.failure_code
  849:                 == "synthetic_failure"
  850:             ),
  851: 
  852:         "canonical_processing_passed":
  853:             (
  854:                 processed_success.succeeded
  855:                 and processed_evidence.integrity_valid
  856:             ),
  857: 
  858:         "stale_fence_result_rejected_by_integrity":
  859:             not bad_integrity.valid,
  860: 
  861:         "execution_identity_preserved":
  862:             (
  863:                 processed_success.execution_id
  864:                 == identity.execution_id
  865:             ),
  866: 
  867:         "job_identity_preserved":
  868:             (
  869:                 processed_success.job_id
  870:                 == identity.job_id
  871:             ),
  872: 
  873:         "attempt_identity_preserved":
  874:             (
  875:                 processed_success.attempt_number
  876:                 == identity.attempt_number
  877:             ),
  878: 
  879:         "fence_identity_preserved":
  880:             (
  881:                 processed_success.fence_id
  882:                 == fence.fence_id
  883:             ),
  884: 
  885:         "no_production_job_mutation":
  886:             True,
  887: 
  888:         "no_queue_mutation":
  889:             True,
  890: 
  891:         "no_lease_mutation":
  892:             True,
  893: 
  894:         "no_retry_decision":
  895:             True,
  896: 
  897:         "no_orchestration_mutation":
  898:             True,
  899: 
  900:         "no_persistence":
  901:             True,
  902:     }
  903: 
  904:     certified = all(checks.values())
  905: 
  906:     return MappingProxyType(
  907:         {
  908:             "phase": "6.6",
  909:             "component":
  910:                 "Execution Result Processing",
  911:             "version":
  912:                 EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_VERSION,
  913:             "schema_version":
  914:                 EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  915:             "certified":
  916:                 certified,
  917:             "checks":
  918:                 MappingProxyType(checks),
  919:             "authority_boundary": (
  920:                 "Phase 6.6 validates execution-result integrity, "
  921:                 "normalizes success/failure and other canonical outcomes, "
  922:                 "resolves the intended Universal Job state and generates "
  923:                 "execution evidence. It does not mutate production job "
  924:                 "state or decide retry/requeue behavior."
  925:             ),
  926:         }
  927:     )
  928: 
  929: 
  930: def explain_execution_result_processing_v1(
  931: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  932: 
  933:     return MappingProxyType(
  934:         {
  935:             "phase": "6.6",
  936:             "component":
  937:                 "Execution Result Processing",
  938:             "version":
  939:                 EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_VERSION,
  940:             "schema_version":
  941:                 EXECUTION_RESULT_PROCESSING_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  942: 
  943:             "successful_result_normalization": (
  944:                 "Validates a SUCCEEDED ExecutionResult and produces a "
  945:                 "canonical normalized result."
  946:             ),
  947: 
  948:             "failed_result_normalization": (
  949:                 "Validates FAILED result/failure evidence and produces "
  950:                 "a canonical normalized result."
  951:             ),
  952: 
  953:             "result_to_job_state_resolution": (
  954:                 "Maps execution outcome to the intended Universal Job "
  955:                 "state without applying the state mutation."
  956:             ),
  957: 
  958:             "execution_evidence_generation": (
  959:                 "Produces immutable downstream execution evidence."
  960:             ),
  961: 
  962:             "result_integrity_validation": (
  963:                 "Requires execution, job, attempt, fence and active "
  964:                 "RUNNING lifecycle identity to agree."
  965:             ),
  966: 
  967:             "prohibitions": (
  968:                 "does not mutate Universal Jobs",
  969:                 "does not apply job-state transitions",
  970:                 "does not enqueue jobs",
  971:                 "does not dequeue jobs",
  972:                 "does not acquire leases",
  973:                 "does not release leases",
  974:                 "does not decide retryability",
  975:                 "does not calculate backoff",
  976:                 "does not requeue jobs",
  977:                 "does not mutate orchestration state",
  978:                 "does not persist results",
  979:             ),
  980:         }
  981:     )
  982: 
  983:

    1: """
    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.16 ù Execution Safety & Concurrency
    4: 
    5: Canonical execution safety/concurrency layer.
    6: 
    7: Owns:
    8: - concurrent execution protection
    9: - lost-lease protection
   10: - late result rejection
   11: - stale worker result rejection
   12: - terminal-state re-entry protection
   13: - execution race resolution
   14: 
   15: Consumes:
   16: - Phase 6.2 execution identity/result
   17: - Phase 6.3 execution fences
   18: - Phase 6.4 lifecycle state
   19: - Phase 6.7 duplicate/idempotency evidence
   20: - Phase 6.15 existing worker/lease/job integration surfaces
   21: 
   22: Does NOT:
   23: - create a second lock manager
   24: - create a second lease system
   25: - create a second duplicate store
   26: - create a second job state machine
   27: - mutate queues directly
   28: - mutate production jobs directly
   29: - persist concurrency state
   30: """
   31: 
   32: from __future__ import annotations
   33: 
   34: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   35: from enum import Enum
   36: from types import MappingProxyType
   37: from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
   38: 
   39: from .execution_contracts import (
   40:     ExecutionResult,
   41: )
   42: 
   43: from .execution_permission_fencing import (
   44:     ExecutionFenceIdentity,
   45: )
   46: 
   47: from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
   48:     ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
   49:     ExecutionLifecycleState,
   50: )
   51: 
   52: from .idempotency_duplicate_control import (
   53:     DuplicateInvocationDecision,
   54:     DuplicateInvocationDisposition,
   55: )
   56: 
   57: 
   58: EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_VERSION = (
   59:     "execution_safety_concurrency_v6.16.1"
   60: )
   61: 
   62: EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION = (
   63:     "execution_safety_concurrency_schema_v1"
   64: )
   65: 
   66: 
   67: class ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(RuntimeError):
   68:     """Raised when an unsafe or stale execution event is rejected."""
   69: 
   70:     def __init__(
   71:         self,
   72:         message: str,
   73:         *,
   74:         code: str,
   75:         value: Any = None,
   76:     ) -> None:
   77:         super().__init__(message)
   78:         self.code = code
   79:         self.value = value
   80: 
   81: 
   82: class SafetyDisposition(str, Enum):
   83:     ALLOW = "ALLOW"
   84:     REJECT = "REJECT"
   85: 
   86: 
   87: class RaceWinner(str, Enum):
   88:     CURRENT_EXECUTION = "CURRENT_EXECUTION"
   89:     EXISTING_TERMINAL_STATE = "EXISTING_TERMINAL_STATE"
   90:     EXISTING_ACTIVE_EXECUTION = "EXISTING_ACTIVE_EXECUTION"
   91:     NONE = "NONE"
   92: 
   93: 
   94: @dataclass(
   95:     frozen=True,
   96:     slots=True,
   97: )
   98: class LeaseSafetyEvidence:
   99:     lease_active: bool
  100: 
  101:     current_lease_id: Optional[str]
  102:     current_lease_owner: Optional[str]
  103: 
  104:     expected_lease_id: Optional[str]
  105:     expected_lease_owner: Optional[str]
  106: 
  107:     schema_version: str = field(
  108:         default=EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  109:         init=False,
  110:     )
  111: 
  112: 
  113: @dataclass(
  114:     frozen=True,
  115:     slots=True,
  116: )
  117: class WorkerSafetyEvidence:
  118:     worker_active: bool
  119: 
  120:     current_worker_id: Optional[str]
  121:     expected_worker_id: Optional[str]
  122: 
  123:     worker_instance_active: bool = True
  124: 
  125:     current_worker_instance_id: Optional[str] = None
  126:     expected_worker_instance_id: Optional[str] = None
  127: 
  128:     schema_version: str = field(
  129:         default=EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  130:         init=False,
  131:     )
  132: 
  133: 
  134: @dataclass(
  135:     frozen=True,
  136:     slots=True,
  137: )
  138: class ResultTimingEvidence:
  139:     lifecycle_terminal: bool
  140:     accepting_results: bool
  141: 
  142:     current_execution_id: str
  143:     result_execution_id: str
  144: 
  145:     current_attempt_number: int
  146:     result_attempt_number: int
  147: 
  148:     schema_version: str = field(
  149:         default=EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  150:         init=False,
  151:     )
  152: 
  153: 
  154: @dataclass(
  155:     frozen=True,
  156:     slots=True,
  157: )
  158: class ExecutionRaceEvidence:
  159:     current_execution_active: bool
  160:     existing_terminal_state: bool
  161:     duplicate_invocation_active: bool
  162:     duplicate_invocation_replay: bool
  163: 
  164:     same_execution_id: bool
  165:     same_attempt_number: bool
  166: 
  167:     schema_version: str = field(
  168:         default=EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  169:         init=False,
  170:     )
  171: 
  172: 
  173: @dataclass(
  174:     frozen=True,
  175:     slots=True,
  176: )
  177: class ExecutionRaceResolution:
  178:     disposition: SafetyDisposition
  179:     winner: RaceWinner
  180: 
  181:     reason_code: str
  182: 
  183:     schema_version: str = field(
  184:         default=EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  185:         init=False,
  186:     )
  187: 
  188: 
  189: @dataclass(
  190:     frozen=True,
  191:     slots=True,
  192: )
  193: class ExecutionSafetyDecision:
  194:     disposition: SafetyDisposition
  195: 
  196:     execution_id: str
  197:     job_id: str
  198:     attempt_number: int
  199:     fence_id: str
  200: 
  201:     reason_code: str
  202: 
  203:     schema_version: str = field(
  204:         default=EXECUTION_SAFETY_CONCURRENCY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  205:         init=False,
  206:     )
  207: 
  208: 
  209: def protect_concurrent_execution(
  210:     *,
  211:     duplicate_decision: DuplicateInvocationDecision,
  212: ) -> None:
  213:     """
  214:     Block active duplicate execution and allow only canonical execution.
  215:     """
  216: 
  217:     if not isinstance(
  218:         duplicate_decision,
  219:         DuplicateInvocationDecision,
  220:     ):
  221:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  222:             "duplicate_decision must be DuplicateInvocationDecision.",
  223:             code="invalid_duplicate_decision",
  224:             value=duplicate_decision,
  225:         )
  226: 
  227:     if (
  228:         duplicate_decision.disposition
  229:         is DuplicateInvocationDisposition.REJECT_DUPLICATE
  230:     ):
  231:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  232:             "Concurrent duplicate execution rejected.",
  233:             code="concurrent_execution_rejected",
  234:         )
  235: 
  236: 
  237: def validate_lease_safety(
  238:     *,
  239:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  240:     evidence: LeaseSafetyEvidence,
  241: ) -> None:
  242:     """
  243:     Reject execution after lease loss, mismatch, or ownership change.
  244:     """
  245: 
  246:     if not evidence.lease_active:
  247:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  248:             "Execution lease is no longer active.",
  249:             code="lost_lease",
  250:         )
  251: 
  252:     if (
  253:         evidence.expected_lease_id is not None
  254:         and evidence.current_lease_id
  255:         != evidence.expected_lease_id
  256:     ):
  257:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  258:             "Execution lease id changed.",
  259:             code="lease_id_changed",
  260:         )
  261: 
  262:     if (
  263:         evidence.expected_lease_owner is not None
  264:         and evidence.current_lease_owner
  265:         != evidence.expected_lease_owner
  266:     ):
  267:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  268:             "Execution lease owner changed.",
  269:             code="lease_owner_changed",
  270:         )
  271: 
  272:     if (
  273:         fence.lease_id is not None
  274:         and evidence.current_lease_id
  275:         != fence.lease_id
  276:     ):
  277:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  278:             "Current lease no longer matches execution fence.",
  279:             code="fenced_lease_mismatch",
  280:         )
  281: 
  282:     if (
  283:         fence.lease_owner is not None
  284:         and evidence.current_lease_owner
  285:         != fence.lease_owner
  286:     ):
  287:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  288:             "Current lease owner no longer matches execution fence.",
  289:             code="fenced_lease_owner_mismatch",
  290:         )
  291: 
  292: 
  293: def validate_worker_safety(
  294:     *,
  295:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  296:     evidence: WorkerSafetyEvidence,
  297: ) -> None:
  298:     """
  299:     Reject stale worker or worker-instance results.
  300:     """
  301: 
  302:     if not evidence.worker_active:
  303:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  304:             "Worker is stale or inactive.",
  305:             code="stale_worker",
  306:         )
  307: 
  308:     if not evidence.worker_instance_active:
  309:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  310:             "Worker instance is stale or inactive.",
  311:             code="stale_worker_instance",
  312:         )
  313: 
  314:     if (
  315:         evidence.expected_worker_id is not None
  316:         and evidence.current_worker_id
  317:         != evidence.expected_worker_id
  318:     ):
  319:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  320:             "Worker ownership changed.",
  321:             code="worker_id_changed",
  322:         )
  323: 
  324:     if (
  325:         fence.worker_id is not None
  326:         and evidence.current_worker_id
  327:         != fence.worker_id
  328:     ):
  329:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  330:             "Worker no longer matches execution fence.",
  331:             code="fenced_worker_mismatch",
  332:         )
  333: 
  334:     if (
  335:         evidence.expected_worker_instance_id is not None
  336:         and evidence.current_worker_instance_id
  337:         != evidence.expected_worker_instance_id
  338:     ):
  339:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  340:             "Worker instance ownership changed.",
  341:             code="worker_instance_changed",
  342:         )
  343: 
  344: 
  345: def reject_late_result(
  346:     *,
  347:     timing: ResultTimingEvidence,
  348: ) -> None:
  349:     """
  350:     Reject result after terminalization or from obsolete execution/attempt.
  351:     """
  352: 
  353:     if timing.lifecycle_terminal:
  354:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  355:             "Late result rejected after terminal lifecycle.",
  356:             code="late_result_after_terminal_state",
  357:         )
  358: 
  359:     if not timing.accepting_results:
  360:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  361:             "Execution is no longer accepting results.",
  362:             code="result_window_closed",
  363:         )
  364: 
  365:     if (
  366:         timing.result_execution_id
  367:         != timing.current_execution_id
  368:     ):
  369:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  370:             "Result belongs to stale execution.",
  371:             code="stale_execution_result",
  372:         )
  373: 
  374:     if (
  375:         timing.result_attempt_number
  376:         != timing.current_attempt_number
  377:     ):
  378:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  379:             "Result belongs to stale attempt.",
  380:             code="stale_attempt_result",
  381:         )
  382: 
  383: 
  384: def protect_terminal_state_reentry(
  385:     *,
  386:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  387: ) -> None:
  388:     """
  389:     Prevent terminal execution from returning to executable state.
  390:     """
  391: 
  392:     if lifecycle.state in {
  393:         ExecutionLifecycleState.SUCCEEDED,
  394:         ExecutionLifecycleState.FAILED,
  395:         ExecutionLifecycleState.CANCELLED,
  396:     }:
  397:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  398:             "Terminal execution cannot re-enter execution.",
  399:             code="terminal_state_reentry_rejected",
  400:             value=lifecycle.state.value,
  401:         )
  402: 
  403: 
  404: def resolve_execution_race(
  405:     *,
  406:     evidence: ExecutionRaceEvidence,
  407: ) -> ExecutionRaceResolution:
  408:     """
  409:     Deterministically resolve execution races.
  410: 
  411:     Priority:
  412:     1. Existing terminal state wins.
  413:     2. Existing active execution wins against duplicate concurrent execution.
  414:     3. Replay of an already-completed duplicate does not create new execution.
  415:     4. Otherwise current execution may proceed.
  416:     """
  417: 
  418:     if evidence.existing_terminal_state:
  419:         return ExecutionRaceResolution(
  420:             disposition=SafetyDisposition.REJECT,
  421:             winner=RaceWinner.EXISTING_TERMINAL_STATE,
  422:             reason_code="terminal_state_wins_race",
  423:         )
  424: 
  425:     if (
  426:         evidence.current_execution_active
  427:         and evidence.duplicate_invocation_active
  428:     ):
  429:         return ExecutionRaceResolution(
  430:             disposition=SafetyDisposition.REJECT,
  431:             winner=RaceWinner.EXISTING_ACTIVE_EXECUTION,
  432:             reason_code="active_execution_wins_race",
  433:         )
  434: 
  435:     if evidence.duplicate_invocation_replay:
  436:         return ExecutionRaceResolution(
  437:             disposition=SafetyDisposition.REJECT,
  438:             winner=RaceWinner.EXISTING_TERMINAL_STATE,
  439:             reason_code="existing_result_replay_wins",
  440:         )
  441: 
  442:     if (
  443:         not evidence.same_execution_id
  444:         or not evidence.same_attempt_number
  445:     ):
  446:         return ExecutionRaceResolution(
  447:             disposition=SafetyDisposition.REJECT,
  448:             winner=RaceWinner.NONE,
  449:             reason_code="execution_identity_race_mismatch",
  450:         )
  451: 
  452:     return ExecutionRaceResolution(
  453:         disposition=SafetyDisposition.ALLOW,
  454:         winner=RaceWinner.CURRENT_EXECUTION,
  455:         reason_code="current_execution_may_proceed",
  456:     )
  457: 
  458: 
  459: def validate_result_safety(
  460:     *,
  461:     lifecycle: ExecutionLifecycleRecord,
  462:     fence: ExecutionFenceIdentity,
  463:     result: ExecutionResult,
  464:     lease_evidence: LeaseSafetyEvidence,
  465:     worker_evidence: WorkerSafetyEvidence,
  466:     timing_evidence: ResultTimingEvidence,
  467: ) -> ExecutionSafetyDecision:
  468:     """
  469:     Full stale/late/lost-lease result acceptance gate.
  470:     """
  471: 
  472:     if result.identity.execution_id != lifecycle.execution_id:
  473:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  474:             "Result execution does not match lifecycle.",
  475:             code="result_lifecycle_execution_mismatch",
  476:         )
  477: 
  478:     if result.identity.job_id != lifecycle.job_id:
  479:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  480:             "Result job does not match lifecycle.",
  481:             code="result_lifecycle_job_mismatch",
  482:         )
  483: 
  484:     if (
  485:         result.identity.attempt_number
  486:         != lifecycle.attempt_number
  487:     ):
  488:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  489:             "Result attempt does not match lifecycle.",
  490:             code="result_lifecycle_attempt_mismatch",
  491:         )
  492: 
  493:     if lifecycle.fence_id != fence.fence_id:
  494:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  495:             "Lifecycle fence changed.",
  496:             code="lifecycle_fence_changed",
  497:         )
  498: 
  499:     validate_lease_safety(
  500:         fence=fence,
  501:         evidence=lease_evidence,
  502:     )
  503: 
  504:     validate_worker_safety(
  505:         fence=fence,
  506:         evidence=worker_evidence,
  507:     )
  508: 
  509:     reject_late_result(
  510:         timing=timing_evidence,
  511:     )
  512: 
  513:     return ExecutionSafetyDecision(
  514:         disposition=SafetyDisposition.ALLOW,
  515:         execution_id=lifecycle.execution_id,
  516:         job_id=lifecycle.job_id,
  517:         attempt_number=lifecycle.attempt_number,
  518:         fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  519:         reason_code="result_safety_validated",
  520:     )
  521: 
  522: 
  523: def certify_execution_safety_concurrency_v1(
  524: ) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
  525:     """
  526:     Phase 6.16 certification.
  527:     """
  528: 
  529:     from .execution_contracts import (
  530:         ExecutionAction,
  531:         ExecutionContext,
  532:         ExecutionIdentity,
  533:         ExecutionOutcome,
  534:         ExecutionRequest,
  535:     )
  536: 
  537:     from .execution_permission_fencing import (
  538:         ExecutionPermissionEvidence,
  539:         evaluate_execution_permission,
  540:     )
  541: 
  542:     from .execution_lifecycle_controller import (
  543:         apply_running_state,
  544:         create_execution_start,
  545:         finalize_execution,
  546:     )
  547: 
  548:     from .idempotency_duplicate_control import (
  549:         DuplicateInvocationEvidence,
  550:         evaluate_duplicate_invocation,
  551:     )
  552: 
  553:     identity = ExecutionIdentity(
  554:         execution_id=(
  555:             "eeeeeeee-eeee-4eee-8eee-eeeeeeeeeeee"
  556:         ),
  557:         job_id="job-phase-6-16-certification",
  558:         attempt_number=1,
  559:         idempotency_key="idem-phase-6-16",
  560:     )
  561: 
  562:     request = ExecutionRequest(
  563:         identity=identity,
  564:         context=ExecutionContext(
  565:             handler_key="certification.handler",
  566:             worker_id="worker-certification",
  567:             worker_instance_id="worker-instance-certification",
  568:             lease_id="lease-certification",
  569:             lease_owner="worker-certification::instance-1",
  570:         ),
  571:         action=ExecutionAction.START,
  572:     )
  573: 
  574:     permission = evaluate_execution_permission(
  575:         request=request,
  576:         evidence=ExecutionPermissionEvidence(
  577:             job_status="RUNNING",
  578:             current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  579:             current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  580:             current_lease_owner=(
  581:                 "worker-certification::instance-1"
  582:             ),
  583:             lease_state="ACTIVE",
  584:         ),
  585:     )
  586: 
  587:     fence = permission.fence
  588: 
  589:     if fence is None:
  590:         raise ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError(
  591:             "Certification missing execution fence.",
  592:             code="certification_missing_fence",
  593:         )
  594: 
  595:     ready = create_execution_start(
  596:         request=request,
  597:         permission=permission,
  598:     )
  599: 
  600:     running = apply_running_state(
  601:         record=ready,
  602:         fence=fence,
  603:         started_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
  604:     )
  605: 
  606:     result = ExecutionResult(
  607:         identity=identity,
  608:         outcome=ExecutionOutcome.SUCCEEDED,
  609:         result_reference="result://phase-6-16/certification",
  610:         result_metadata={
  611:             "fence_id": fence.fence_id,
  612:         },
  613:     )
  614: 
  615:     lease_ok = LeaseSafetyEvidence(
  616:         lease_active=True,
  617:         current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  618:         current_lease_owner=(
  619:             "worker-certification::instance-1"
  620:         ),
  621:         expected_lease_id="lease-certification",
  622:         expected_lease_owner=(
  623:             "worker-certification::instance-1"
  624:         ),
  625:     )
  626: 
  627:     worker_ok = WorkerSafetyEvidence(
  628:         worker_active=True,
  629:         current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  630:         expected_worker_id="worker-certification",
  631:         worker_instance_active=True,
  632:         current_worker_instance_id=(
  633:             "worker-instance-certification"
  634:         ),
  635:         expected_worker_instance_id=(
  636:             "worker-instance-certification"
  637:         ),
  638:     )
  639: 
  640:     timing_ok = ResultTimingEvidence(
  641:         lifecycle_terminal=False,
  642:         accepting_results=True,
  643:         current_execution_id=identity.execution_id,
  644:         result_execution_id=identity.execution_id,
  645:         current_attempt_number=1,
  646:         result_attempt_number=1,
  647:     )
  648: 
  649:     allowed = validate_result_safety(
  650:         lifecycle=running,
  651:         fence=fence,
  652:         result=result,
  653:         lease_evidence=lease_ok,
  654:         worker_evidence=worker_ok,
  655:         timing_evidence=timing_ok,
  656:     )
  657: 
  658:     lost_lease_rejected = False
  659: 
  660:     try:
  661:         validate_result_safety(
  662:             lifecycle=running,
  663:             fence=fence,
  664:             result=result,
  665:             lease_evidence=LeaseSafetyEvidence(
  666:                 lease_active=False,
  667:                 current_lease_id="lease-certification",
  668:                 current_lease_owner=(
  669:                     "worker-certification::instance-1"
  670:                 ),
  671:                 expected_lease_id="lease-certification",
  672:                 expected_lease_owner=(
  673:                     "worker-certification::instance-1"
  674:                 ),
  675:             ),
  676:             worker_evidence=worker_ok,
  677:             timing_evidence=timing_ok,
  678:         )
  679:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  680:         lost_lease_rejected = True
  681: 
  682:     stale_worker_rejected = False
  683: 
  684:     try:
  685:         validate_result_safety(
  686:             lifecycle=running,
  687:             fence=fence,
  688:             result=result,
  689:             lease_evidence=lease_ok,
  690:             worker_evidence=WorkerSafetyEvidence(
  691:                 worker_active=False,
  692:                 current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  693:                 expected_worker_id="worker-certification",
  694:             ),
  695:             timing_evidence=timing_ok,
  696:         )
  697:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  698:         stale_worker_rejected = True
  699: 
  700:     stale_worker_instance_rejected = False
  701: 
  702:     try:
  703:         validate_worker_safety(
  704:             fence=fence,
  705:             evidence=WorkerSafetyEvidence(
  706:                 worker_active=True,
  707:                 current_worker_id="worker-certification",
  708:                 expected_worker_id="worker-certification",
  709:                 worker_instance_active=False,
  710:                 current_worker_instance_id="old-instance",
  711:                 expected_worker_instance_id=(
  712:                     "worker-instance-certification"
  713:                 ),
  714:             ),
  715:         )
  716:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  717:         stale_worker_instance_rejected = True
  718: 
  719:     late_result_rejected = False
  720: 
  721:     try:
  722:         reject_late_result(
  723:             timing=ResultTimingEvidence(
  724:                 lifecycle_terminal=True,
  725:                 accepting_results=False,
  726:                 current_execution_id=identity.execution_id,
  727:                 result_execution_id=identity.execution_id,
  728:                 current_attempt_number=1,
  729:                 result_attempt_number=1,
  730:             ),
  731:         )
  732:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  733:         late_result_rejected = True
  734: 
  735:     stale_attempt_result_rejected = False
  736: 
  737:     try:
  738:         reject_late_result(
  739:             timing=ResultTimingEvidence(
  740:                 lifecycle_terminal=False,
  741:                 accepting_results=True,
  742:                 current_execution_id=identity.execution_id,
  743:                 result_execution_id=identity.execution_id,
  744:                 current_attempt_number=2,
  745:                 result_attempt_number=1,
  746:             ),
  747:         )
  748:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  749:         stale_attempt_result_rejected = True
  750: 
  751:     succeeded = finalize_execution(
  752:         record=running,
  753:         result=result,
  754:         fence=fence,
  755:         finalized_at="2026-01-01T00:00:10Z",
  756:     )
  757: 
  758:     terminal_reentry_rejected = False
  759: 
  760:     try:
  761:         protect_terminal_state_reentry(
  762:             lifecycle=succeeded,
  763:         )
  764:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  765:         terminal_reentry_rejected = True
  766: 
  767:     duplicate_decision = evaluate_duplicate_invocation(
  768:         request=request,
  769:         fence=fence,
  770:         evidence=DuplicateInvocationEvidence(
  771:             matching_execution_exists=True,
  772:             matching_execution_active=True,
  773:             matching_execution_terminal=False,
  774:             matching_execution_id=(
  775:                 "existing-execution-phase-6-16"
  776:             ),
  777:             matching_fence_id=fence.fence_id,
  778:         ),
  779:     )
  780: 
  781:     concurrent_duplicate_rejected = False
  782: 
  783:     try:
  784:         protect_concurrent_execution(
  785:             duplicate_decision=duplicate_decision,
  786:         )
  787:     except ExecutionSafetyConcurrencyError:
  788:         concurrent_duplicate_rejected = True
  789: 
  790:     active_race = resolve_execution_race(
  791:         evidence=ExecutionRaceEvidence(
  792:             current_execution_active=True,
  793:             existing_terminal_state=False,
  794:             duplicate_invocation_active=True,
  795:             duplicate_invocation_replay=False,
  796:             same_execution_id=True,
  797:             same_attempt_number=True,
  798:         ),
  799:     )
  800: 
  801:     terminal_race = resolve_execution_race(
  802:         evidence=ExecutionRaceEvidence(
  803:             current_execution_active=False,
  804:             existing_terminal_state=True,
  805:             duplicate_invocation_active=False,
  806:             duplicate_invocation_replay=False,
  807:             same_execution_id=True,
  808:             same_attempt_number=True,

    2: LinkCraftor Universal Runtime
    3: Phase 6.14 ù Phase-5 Decision Execution Bridge
    4: 
    5: Canonical boundary between orchestration decisions and execution.
    6: 
    7: Core law:
    8:     Phase 5 decides WHAT should happen.
    9:     Phase 6 executes WHAT Phase 5 already decided.
   10: 
   11: Consumes:
   12: - handoff decisions
   13: - suspension decisions
   14: - resume decisions
   15: - recovery decisions
   16: - completion decisions
   17: - cancellation decisions
   18: 
   19: Routes to:
   20: - handoff execution boundary
   21: - Phase 6.9 Suspension Execution
   22: - Phase 6.10 Resume Execution
   23: - Phase 6.11 Recovery / Retry Execution
   24: - Phase 6.12 Completion Execution
   25: - Phase 6.13 Cancellation / Termination Execution
   26: 
   27: Owns:
   28: - decision consumption validation
   29: - decision-to-execution routing identity
   30: - execution target resolution
   31: - evidence coordination
   32: - decision/execution separation enforcement
   33: 
   34: Does NOT:
